Dropping prediction withholds the spatial training signal by construction; dropping observation forfeits cross-view semantic invariance by construction; at the scale we study, no pair substitutes for the third. Every major self-supervised method is a special case of a single unified energy decomposition. We pair every theoretical claim with a controlled experiment, including a patch-retrieval evaluation for the spatial consequence of prediction.

</details>


### [91] [A continually expandable foundation model for brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08319)
*Michail Mamalakis,Carmen Jimenez-Mesa,Yonghao Li,Hao Chen,Chao Li,Antonios Mamalakis,John Suckling,Richard Bethlehem,Stephen J. Price,Richard J. Gilbertson,Pietro Lio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Alcmaeon，一个基于超过425,000个体积的三维脑MRI基础模型，通过Graph-Blueprint Pruning（GBP）方法实现跨临床领域的顺序扩展，能够在学习新领域（如发育、精神、肿瘤成像）的同时保留旧领域能力，减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑MRI模型通常针对单一疾病、人群或成像协议开发，基础模型在一次预训练后难以持续更新——新数据训练会丢失早期能力（灾难性遗忘）。需要一种能够随数据增长而扩展同时保留已有能力的方法。

Method: Alcmaeon采用无人工标注的三维脑MRI基础模型架构，结合体积编码与潜在扩散生成。核心创新是Graph-Blueprint Pruning（GBP），通过保护对早期领域重要的网络模块（蓝图），同时保持其余容量可训练，实现跨临床领域的顺序扩展。

Result: 在从健康衰老和神经退行扩展到发育、精神和肿瘤成像的实验中，GBP在体素级重建指标上比顺序适配和弹性权重巩固（EWC）表现出更少的遗忘，尤其在肿瘤成像适配后优势最大。蓝图提供了模型容量保护与重用的可检查记录。不同模型层级的表示支持图像合成、疾病分类、生存建模和术后预测，但没有任何单一表示在所有任务中都是最优的。

Conclusion: 该研究为脑MRI基础模型提供了可随新数据增长且保留早期能力的可行路径，展示了通过结构化剪枝实现持续学习在医学成像领域的前景。

Abstract: Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to neuroscience and clinical assessment, but models are commonly developed for individual diseases, populations or imaging protocols. Foundation models promise more general representations, yet they are usually pretrained once and can lose earlier capabilities when updated with new data. Here we show that Alcmaeon, a three-dimensional brain MRI foundation model pretrained without manual labels on more than 425,000 volumes and derived imaging maps, can be expanded sequentially across clinical domains. Alcmaeon combines volumetric encoding and latent diffusion generation with Graph-Blueprint Pruning (GBP), which protects network modules important to earlier domains while leaving the remaining capacity trainable. Across expansion from healthy ageing and neurodegeneration to developmental, psychiatric and tumour imaging, GBP showed less forgetting than sequential adaptation and elastic weight consolidation across voxel-level reconstruction measures, with its largest advantage after adaptation to tumour imaging. The blueprints provided an inspectable record of how model capacity was protected and reused. Representations from different model levels supported image synthesis, disease classification, survival modelling and postoperative prediction, although no single representation was optimal for every task. These findings provide a route towards brain MRI foundation models that can grow with emerging data while retaining earlier capabilities.

</details>


### [92] [Circuit Fine-Tuning for Compute-Efficient Transformer Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08336)
*Uri Z. Kialy,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出电路微调（CFT），一种计算高效的视觉Transformer微调框架。与传统PEFT以参数量为效率指标不同，CFT利用电路发现技术，在训练前选出需要微调的模块子图，仅微调恢复出的子图而无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch即可达到峰值精度，相比强PEFT基线节省2.3-6.6倍训练FLOPs和最多16倍墙钟时间，且不增加任何参数和推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法以参数量作为效率指标具有误导性：参数稀疏的方法仍可能产生全模型训练成本，且通常需要长训练计划才能达到峰值精度。参数量少并不等于计算高效，需要一种真正以计算效率为核心的微调框架。

Method: CFT使用电路发现技术（传统上用于解释训练好的模型）在训练前选择要微调的模块。其关键创新在于：属性归因不再针对已训练的任务头，而是针对近零初始化的探测头，从而隔离骨干网络对目标分布的响应而非特定分类器的偏好。然后仅微调恢复出的子图。

Result: CFT无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch达到峰值精度（对比PEFT基线的44-96个epoch），节省2.3-6.6倍训练FLOPs和最多16倍墙钟时间，同时零参数增加和零推理开销增加。在VTAB-1k、Swin分层骨干、CBIS-DDSM医学影像和Gemma-3在CUB-200上的实验均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: CFT证明电路发现可以从解释工具转化为高效的训练框架，将计算效率作为PEFT的核心指标。它展示了在保持零参数增量和零推理开销的同时，通过精确选择微调模块可大幅降低训练成本并加速收敛，为视觉Transformer微调提供了一种新的高效范式。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto standard for adapting Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. While parameter count has been the dominant efficiency metric in PEFT, it does not imply \textit{compute efficiency}: parameter-sparse methods can still incur full-model training cost per step, and typically need long schedules to reach peak accuracy. We introduce Circuit Fine-Tuning (CFT), a compute-efficient framework that uses circuit discovery---conventionally used to explain trained models---to select modules for fine-tuning before training. Whereas attribution is conventionally formulated against a trained task head, we formulate it against a near-zero-initialized probe head, which isolates the response of the backbone to the target distribution rather than the preferences of a particular classifier. CFT then fine-tunes only the recovered subgraph. CFT needs no learning-rate warmup and reaches peak accuracy in ${\sim}20$ epochs on average---versus $44$--$96$ for strong PEFT baselines---yielding $2.3$--$6.6\times$ fewer training FLOPs and up to $16\times$ less wall-clock time, while adding zero parameters and no inference operations. Experiments across a standard visual transfer benchmark (VTAB-1k), hierarchical backbones (Swin), domain-shifted medical imaging (CBIS-DDSM), and a vision-language model (Gemma-3 on CUB-200) demonstrate the effectiveness of CFT. Code is available at https://github.com/UriKialy/CFT

</details>


### [93] [Tropical Cyclone Forecasting via Latent Rectified Flow using Satellite Imagery and Atmospheric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08354)
*Meheru Zannat,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个单次前向传播的深度生成模型，可同时预测热带气旋的卫星红外影像和四个大气物理场（U/V风、气温、地表气压），最长可达9小时。模型采用五通道变分自编码器压缩到潜空间，结合条件整流流UNet与因子化时间注意力模块，并通过可微分的引导流轨道误差进行奖励微调（DRaFT）。在2022年留出风暴上，模型在PSNR/SSIM上优于级联扩散基线，采样速度快约30倍，轨道误差降低15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋在气候变化下破坏力增强，需要高效预测。现有深度生成模型要么只生成卫星影像要么只生成大气场，无法同时生成两者；需要大量采样步骤，在普通硬件上难以运行；且风暴轨道来自回归头，与生成的大气物理场缺乏物理关联。

Method: (1) 五通道VAE将5×256×256帧压缩为4×64×64潜变量；(2) 条件整流流UNet结合因子化时间注意力模块，由三个过去帧预测未来三个帧，输入包含最佳轨道坐标和时间戳；(3) 使用DRaFT奖励微调，通过预测风场进行引导流计算得到可微分的轨道误差来优化模型。

Result: 在2022年留出风暴上达到16.35 dB PSNR和0.759 SSIM，全面超过级联扩散基线（+9小时时+0.84 dB），采样速度快约30倍（56ms vs 1673ms）；+9小时轨道误差62.4公里，比基线低15%；奖励微调研究进一步降低8-11%的轨道误差。

Conclusion: 该模型实现了单次前向传播的联合预测（影像+大气场），兼顾生成质量、采样效率和物理一致性，通过将轨道误差直接耦合到生成的大气场中，显著提升了轨道预测精度，验证了将物理导引机制嵌入生成模型训练的有效性。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones are growing more destructive in a changing climate, and efficient forecasting of their structure and track has become a necessity. Deep generative models promise an alternative to computationally expensive numerical weather prediction (NWP), yet current systems produce either satellite imagery or atmospheric fields, never both; they need many sampling steps, putting them out of reach of modest hardware; and their storm tracks come from regression heads with no physical link to the generated atmosphere. This work presents a single-pass model that jointly forecasts GRIDSAT-B1 infrared imagery and four ERA5 atmospheric fields (U-wind, V-wind, air temperature, and surface pressure) out to nine hours. A five-channel variational autoencoder compresses each 5 x 256 x 256 frame to a 4 x 64 x 64 latent, and a conditional rectified-flow UNet with a factorized temporal-attention module predicts the next three frames from three past frames, their best-track coordinates, and timestamps. The model is then reward-fine-tuned (DRaFT) against a differentiable track error derived from the predicted winds through a steering-flow calculation. On held-out 2022 storms the model reaches 16.35 dB PSNR and 0.759 SSIM, ahead of a reproduced cascaded-diffusion baseline at every lead time (+0.84 dB at +9 h) while sampling ~30x faster (56 ms vs. 1673 ms). Track error at +9 h is 62.4 km, 15% below the baseline, and a reward fine-tuning study demonstrates a further 8-11% track-error reduction across sampler budgets.

</details>


### [94] [VOICE: A Vision-Omics Foundation Model Integrating Direct and Retrieval-Based Prediction of In-situ Single-Cell Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08366)
*Xin Luo,Yicheng Tao,Haoxuan Zeng,Suyuan Wang,Chenzi Ouyang,Meiqi Zhu,Kai Liu,Shuibing Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOICE是一个多模态基础模型，利用配对Xenium数据从H&E图像预测单细胞基因表达。它通过对比学习对齐细胞形态与表达嵌入（2300万细胞），再用直接回归和参考细胞检索双分支预测表达，并以每基因权重融合，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学虽能解析单细胞基因表达，但成本高昂、限于靶向基因panel且样本量小；而H&E染色便宜且大规模常规采集。因此需要一种能从形态学直接预测单细胞基因表达的方法，以便将分子分析扩展到大型组织档案。

Method: VOICE使用对比学习对齐来自病理基础模型的细胞中心H&E形态嵌入和来自转录组基础模型的单细胞表达嵌入（2300万细胞）。后续通过两个分支预测表达：一个分支直接从形态回归表达，另一个分支从相似参考细胞检索实测表达以恢复无形态信号的基因。最终以每基因权重融合两个分支的输出。

Result: VOICE在heldout患者、切片及部分重叠的Xenium基因panel上泛化良好，并在七项指标上持续优于先前的单细胞表达预测方法。

Conclusion: VOICE展示了从常规H&E图像预测单细胞基因表达的可行性，两个互补分支的设计有效覆盖了不同形态可预测性的基因，为大规模组织档案的分子分析提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics can resolve gene expression at single-cell resolution, but it is costly, limited to targeted panels of a few hundred to a few thousand genes, and applicable to only a small number of samples. H&E imaging, by contrast, is cheap and collected routinely at scale. This makes predicting single-cell expression directly from morphology a practical way to bring molecular analysis to large tissue archives. We therefore present VOICE, a multimodal foundation model that predicts single-cell gene expression from H&E images using paired Xenium data. VOICE first aligns cell centered H&E morphology from a pathology foundation model with single-cell expression embeddings from a transcriptome foundation model, trained using contrastive learning over 23 million cells. Next it predicts expression through two branches. One branch directly regresses expression from morphology. The other branch retrieves measured expression from similar reference cells, recovering genes that do not have morphological signal. Because genes vary in morphological predictability, VOICE fuses the two branches with a per-gene weight. After training, VOICE generalizes to heldout patients, slides, and partially overlapping gene panels from Xenium, and it consistently outperforms prior single-cell expression prediction methods on seven metrics.

</details>


### [95] [PARAGraph: Pathology-Anatomy-Aware Hierarchical Graph for Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08368)
*Ziyang Zhang,Yuankai Huo,Yalin Zheng,He Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PARAGraph，一个病理-解剖感知的层次图框架，用于糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）严重程度分级。该框架将每张图像表示为三层层次图，利用视盘-黄斑锚定坐标系编码病变节点的类别、归一化面积和解剖坐标，并通过双融合策略提升对噪声病变分割的鲁棒性。在Messidor-2、APTOS和DDR数据集上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数深度学习模型将DR分级视为图像级分类任务，未显式建模临床相关的证据（如病变类型和空间关系），导致模型的预测缺乏临床可解释性和稳健性。

Method: PARAGraph构建三层层次图（病变节点、中间类别/区域节点、全局解剖/语义节点）；构建视盘-黄斑锚定坐标系提供尺度与旋转归一化的视网膜参考系统，将病变节点编码为类别、归一化面积和解剖坐标；采用双融合策略引入全局视觉上下文到图语义节点和决策级预测分支，缓解病变分割噪声问题。

Result: 在Messidor-2、APTOS和DDR三个公开数据集上，PARAGraph的DR分级性能一致优于现有最先进方法。可解释性和鲁棒性分析表明其预测与病变证据密切相关，且对病变分割噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PARAGraph通过将临床解剖先验显式融入图神经网络框架，实现了既准确又具有临床可解释性的DR分级，验证了结合医学领域知识建模可提升深度学习模型在眼病诊断中的实用价值。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) remains a leading cause of vision loss among working-age adults worldwide, making reliable severity grading clinically important. Despite strong performance, most deep models formulate DR grading as image-level classification and do not explicitly model clinically grounded evidence, such as lesion types and spatial relations. In this paper, we propose PARAGraph, a Pathology-Anatomy-Aware Hierarchical Graph framework for DR grading. PARAGraph represents each image as a three-level hierarchical graph with lesion-level nodes, intermediate category and region nodes, and global anatomical and semantic nodes. To incorporate medical priors into nodes, we construct an optic disc-fovea-anchored coordinate frame that provides a scale- and rotation-normalized retinal reference system. Within this frame, lesion nodes are encoded with category, normalized area, and anatomical coordinates. To mitigate noisy lesion segmentation, PARAGraph uses a dual-fusion strategy that introduces global visual context into a graph semantic node and a decision-level prediction branch, improving robustness when lesion evidence is unreliable. Extensive experiments on Messidor-2, APTOS, and DDR show that PARAGraph achieves consistent DR grading performance over state-of-the-art methods. Interpretability and robustness analyses further demonstrate that its predictions are clinically grounded, closely associated with lesion evidence and robust to lesion segmentation noise.

</details>


### [96] [Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection for Pathology Transformer Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08374)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection (Gated SRP)，一种轻量级的即插即用自注意力校正模块，用于解决病理全切片图像中相邻patch高度相似导致的自注意力局部空间冗余问题，在跨五个TCGA生存队列和五个分类数据集上均取得最优或显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI与自然图像存在本质差异：相邻patch常包含高度相似的组织类型、染色、纹理和细胞组成。这种局部空间冗余会导致自注意力将占主导的邻域特征反复混入patch-token中，从而削弱细微但关键的诊断/预后偏差信号，这是病理学特有的自注意力失效模式。

Method: Gated SRP作为自注意力层的轻量drop-in校正模块：对每个patch token和注意力头，首先从相邻的value向量估计局部冗余轴，然后将注意力输出投影到该轴上，最后通过学习的有符号门控(gate)对冗余对齐分量进行几何校正。

Result: 在五个TCGA生存队列中，Gated SRP在所有对比的注意力变体中均取得最高平均C-index，相比基础注意力有平均提升，且仅增加+0.02%参数；在五个slide级分类数据集上，在16项指标中改善基础注意力达12项，并在三个数据集上取得最佳AUC。

Conclusion: Gated SRP以极低的参数开销有效缓解了WSI中自注意力的局部空间冗余问题，验证了针对病理图像特性设计注意力校正模块的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Transformer models are increasingly used for whole-slide image analysis in computational pathology. Yet, WSIs differ fundamentally from natural images: neighbouring patches often contain highly similar tissue type, stain, texture, and cellular composition. We identify this local spatial redundancy as a pathology-specific failure mode of self-attention, where dominant neighbourhood features can be repeatedly mixed into patch-tokens and weaken subtle diagnostic or prognostic deviations. We propose Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection (Gated SRP), a lightweight drop-in correction module for self-attention layers. For each patch token and attention head, Gated SRP estimates a local redundancy axis from neighbouring value vectors, projects the attention output onto this axis, and applies a learned signed gate to correct the redundancy-aligned component geometrically. Across five TCGA survival cohorts, Gated SRP obtains the highest mean C-index among the compared attention variants in all cohorts, with an average improvement over the base attention, while adding only +0.02% parameters. Across five slide-level classification datasets, it improves the base attention on 12 of 16 reported metrics and achieves the best AUC on three datasets. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/AtlasAnalyticsLab/GatedSRP.

</details>


### [97] [DoRF++: Spherical Representation Learning over Doppler Radiance Fields for Robust Wi-Fi Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08381)
*Navid Hasanzadeh,Shahrokh Valaee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DoRF和DoRF++方法，将神经辐射场（NeRF）概念引入Wi-Fi感知领域，利用CSI提取的多普勒速度投影建模潜在3D人体运动序列，并投射到球面表示上，通过球面Transformer进行活动分类，在跨用户泛化方面显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi CSI在无源、免设备、隐私保护的活动识别方面具有巨大潜力，但跨用户和未知场景下的泛化能力不足，阻碍了实际应用部署。现有方法在真实世界变异性条件下缺乏可靠的泛化性能。

Method: DoRF将CSI中的多普勒速度投影视为稀疏多样的虚拟相机视角，推断潜在3D运动序列，并沿有效多普勒方向投影以解释CSI观测。随后将恢复的运动投射到单位球面上的等角方向网格，生成球面运动表示。DoRF++在此基础上采用球面Transformer进行活动分类。

Result: 在手势数据集上的实验表明，DoRF++在跨用户泛化准确率上显著优于最先进的Wi-Fi HAR方法，尤其在单多天线接收AP设置下的困难手势识别中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将NeRF概念从计算机视觉迁移到Wi-Fi感知，通过球面学习框架有效提升活动识别的跨用户泛化能力，为IEEE 802.11bf标准的先进WLAN感知应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Motivated by the IEEE 802.11bf effort to standardize advanced WLAN sensing, interest in Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI) for passive, device-free, and privacy-preserving activity and gesture recognition has grown rapidly. Recent studies have shown that Doppler velocity projections extracted from CSI, which directly reflect human-motion velocity, enable more robust human activity recognition (HAR) and stronger generalization across users and unseen conditions. Nevertheless, reliable generalization under real-world variability remains a major challenge, hindering the adoption of Wi-Fi sensing in real-world applications. To address this challenge, we introduce Doppler Radiance Fields (DoRF), bringing the concept of neural radiance fields (NeRF) from computer vision into Wi-Fi sensing. DoRF models Doppler velocity projections extracted from Wi-Fi CSI as sparse and diverse virtual-camera views of human motion. It then infers a latent 3D motion sequence whose projections along learned effective Doppler directions explain the CSI-derived Doppler observations. The recovered motion is subsequently projected onto an equiangular grid of directions on the unit sphere, producing a spherical representation of the underlying motion. Since DoRF naturally defines the Doppler representation on spheres, we further introduce DoRF++, a spherical-learning design that applies spherical Transformers for activity classification. Experiments on our collected hand-gesture dataset show that DoRF++ significantly outperforms state-of-the-art Wi-Fi-based HAR methods in cross-user generalization accuracy, especially for difficult gestures in settings with a single multi-antenna receiver access point (AP).

</details>


### [98] [Anatomically Consistent Cross-Contrast Super-Resolution of Anisotropic Brain T2w MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08401)
*Mengqi Shen,Haicheng Wang,Meghna Trivedi,Tony J. Wang,Yuanguang Xu,Yingyan Zeng,Yading Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIPP-SR，一种跨对比度引导的超分辨率框架，用于恢复各向异性T2w脑MRI的层间分辨率。该方法通过训练VIP-GAN学习T1c-to-T2w解剖对应关系，生成三个正交视图的T2w估计，再通过基于投影的优化融合，在不依赖各向同性T2w真值的情况下提升图像质量。实验表明该方法能显著提升下游分割性能（BraTS-MET上Dice从0.330提升到0.465，BraTS-GLI上零样本从0.473提升到0.563），且消融实验证实层间自一致性是主要增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2w脑MRI是神经肿瘤学和放疗计划中重要的流体敏感成像模态，但其扫描通常使用各向异性体素，在冠状面和矢状面上呈现模糊或阶梯状伪影，影响小结构辨识和下游3D分析。现有方法缺乏各向同性T2w真值，难以进行有效的超分辨率重建。

Method: VIPP-SR框架包含三个关键组件：(1)训练视图无关的patch生成器VIP-GAN，从高分辨率轴向切片学习局部T1c-to-T2w解剖对应关系；(2)通过形状保持patch和最深跳连移除减少视图特定捷径，实现零样本层间迁移；(3)基于投影的优化过程在三视图间强制解剖一致性，通过平衡层间自一致性和逐视图数据保真度进行融合。

Result: 在BraTS-MET测试集上，VIPP-SR将平均标签Dice从0.330提升至0.465；在BraTS-GLI队列上零样本泛化，Dice从0.473提升至0.563。消融研究表明层间自一致性是性能提升的主要来源。

Conclusion: VIPP-SR证明在不依赖各向同性T2w真值的前提下，通过跨对比度引导和投影优化可以有效恢复各向异性T2w体素的层间分辨率，显著改善下游分割性能，并具备跨队列的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: T2-weighted (T2w) brain MRI provides fluid-sensitive soft-tissue contrast that is important for neuro-oncology and radiotherapy planning. However, T2w scans are acquired with anisotropic voxels and appear blurred or stair-stepped on coronal and sagittal views, which obscures small structures and weakens any downstream 3D analysis. We propose VIPP-SR (View-Independent Patched Projection Super-Resolution), a cross-contrast guided super-resolution framework that restores the inter-plane resolution of an existing anisotropic T2w volume without an isotropic ground-truth T2w. VIPP-SR first trains a view-independent patched generator (VIP-GAN) to learn local T1c-to-T2w anatomical correspondence from high-resolution axial slices. The trained generator is then applied to axial, coronal, and sagittal views of the T1c volume to generate three orthogonal T2w estimates. Shape-preserving patching and deepest-skip removal reduce view-specific shortcuts, thereby constraining the generator to learn patch-local representations and enabling the zero-shot inter-plane transfer. Central to VIPP-SR, a projection-based optimization then enforces anatomical consistency across the three view-specific volumes, fusing them by balancing inter-plane self-consistency against per-view data fidelity. The generator is trained on BraTS-MET and evaluated on both the held-out BraTS-MET testing set and the BraTS-GLI cohort without retraining, assessing the cross-cohort generalizability. The results validate that VIPP-SR improves downstream segmentation over the real anisotropic T2w baseline, raising mean-label Dice from 0.330 to 0.465 on BraTS-MET and, zero-shot, from 0.473 to 0.563 on BraTS-GLI and ablation studies identify inter-plane self-consistency as the main source of the gain.

</details>


### [99] [Agentic AI-powered flexible fiber-bundle endoscopy for high-resolution NIR-II fluorescence imaging in vivo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08402)
*Yanzhao Shi,Yuanhua Liu,Sixin Xu,Wayne Jason Li,Yuyuan Chen,Danyang Xu,Zhisheng Wu,Hanze Yu,Ian Yu-Hong Wong,Simon Ying-Kit Law,Hongjie Dai,Liangqiong Qu,Feifei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的柔性内窥镜成像平台，通过光学-计算协同设计，利用超薄光纤束与Agent引导的混合专家（GAME）管道，解决了光纤束内窥镜在可见光至NIR-II波段长期存在的低分辨率、蜂窝伪影和串扰问题，实现了超越奈奎斯特采样极限四倍的分辨率提升，并在小鼠活体NIR-II成像及人体胃管、淋巴系统成像中验证了其临床转化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤束内窥镜自1950年代问世以来，长期受限于低空间分辨率、蜂窝伪影和纤芯间串扰等问题，尤其在NIR-II波段（1000-3000 nm）串扰更为显著，限制了其在生物医学成像中的临床应用。

Method: 采用光学-计算协同设计策略：一方面优化超薄光纤束以抑制串扰引起的图像模糊，实现可见光至NIR-II全谱段的高保真图像传输；另一方面开发Agent引导的混合专家（GAME）管道，通过视觉语言模型动态将不同输入图像路由至相应的恢复专家，完成蜂窝伪影去除和图像复原。

Result: GAME管道为细胞、小鼠和人体样本等多种生物医学图像提供统一恢复入口，实现超越奈奎斯特-香农采样极限四倍的分辨率提升；通过小鼠活体NIR-II解剖结构成像以及DMD投影的人体胃管和淋巴系统成像，验证了平台的实用性。

Conclusion: 该AI驱动的柔性内窥镜平台有效克服了光纤束内窥镜在NIR-II波段的传统瓶颈，为未来临床转化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Fiber-bundle endoscopy offers a compact and flexible route for clinical fluorescence imaging through natural human orifices, but since its first report in the 1950s, it has remained limited by low spatial resolution, honeycomb artifacts, and inter-core crosstalk. The crosstalk becomes more pronounced at near-infrared-II wavelengths (NIR-II, 1000-3000 nm), a spectral window that offers superior contrast, resolution, and tissue penetration depth for biomedical imaging. Here, we present an AI-powered flexible endoscopy platform that overcomes these constraints through optical-computational co-design: optimizing ultrathin fiber bundles to mitigate crosstalk-induced image blur and enable high-fidelity image transmission across the visible-to-NIR-II spectral range, and developing an Agent-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (GAME) pipeline for honeycomb-artifact removal and image restoration. GAME provides a single restoration entry point for diverse biomedical images acquired with our endoscope, spanning cell, mouse and human samples. It dynamically routes each input to suitable restoration experts via a vision-language model, facilitating image reconstruction with a fourfold resolution improvement beyond the NyquistShannon sampling limit. The utility of our endoscope is demonstrated through in vivo NIR-II imaging of anatomical structures in mice, as well as imaging of the digital micromirror device (DMD)-projected human gastric tube and lymphatic system, paving the way for future clinical translation.

</details>


### [100] [Learning Deep Modality-Shared Self-Expressiveness for Image Clustering with Textual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08418)
*Xianghan Meng,Wei He,Zhiyuan Huang,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepMORSE，一种基于模态共享自表达模型的深度图像聚类方法，通过显式建模各模态内部子空间结构来替代传统的跨模态对齐策略，在六个基准数据集上取得显著性能提升，并展示了所学表示在下游任务上的强迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的图像聚类方法，通常通过直接最大化图像-文本相似度来强制跨模态对齐，但这种策略没有显式建模每个模态内部的内在结构，可能导致不可靠的对齐或扭曲对聚类至关重要的模态特有结构。

Method: 提出深度模态共享自表达模型（DeepMORSE），通过模态共享的自表达模型来发现跨模态结构，同时学习符合模态特有子空间并集的结构化表示。此外，提供了理论证明：模态共享的自表达系数能抑制类间噪声趋向子空间保持解，并表明小批量优化过程引入了对自表达模型的隐式正则化。

Result: 在六个广泛使用的图像聚类基准上进行了评估，在UCF-101、DTD-47和ImageNet-Dogs数据集上性能提升超过3%。此外，学习的表示在图像检索和零样本分类等下游任务上取得了最先进的性能，无需任何任务特定的损失函数或后处理。

Conclusion: DeepMORSE通过显式建模模态内部子空间结构的方式，提供了一种比直接跨模态对齐更原则性的多模态聚类方法，不仅聚类性能优越，而且所学表示具有较强的迁移性，适用于多种下游任务。

Abstract: Leveraging textual information for image clustering has emerged as a promising direction, largely owing to the powerful representations learned by Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing approaches typically retrieve a textual counterpart for each image and then refine multimodal representations by directly enforcing cross-modal agreement, e.g., maximizing image-text similarity inherited from pretrained VLMs. However, such a strategy aligns heterogeneous representations across modalities without explicitly modeling the intrinsic structure within each modality and thus might yield unreliable alignment or distort modality-specific structures that are crucial for clustering. In this paper, we propose a simple but principled approach, termed deep modality-shared self-expressive model (DeepMORSE), which discovers cross-modal structures via a modality-shared self-expressive model and simultaneously learns structured representations that conform to a union of modality-specific subspaces. Moreover, we theoretically justify that the modality-shared self-expressive coefficients suppress inter-class noise towards a subspace-preserving solution, and show that mini-batch optimization procedure introduces an implicit regularization onto the self-expressive model. We evaluate our DeepMORSE on six widely used image clustering benchmarks and observe performance improvements exceeding 3% on the UCF-101, DTD-47, and ImageNet-Dogs datasets. In addition, we demonstrate the strong transferability of the learned representations by achieving state-of-the-art performance on downstream tasks such as image retrieval and zero-shot classification---without requiring any task-specific losses or post-processing. The code is available at: https://github.com/mengxianghan123/DeepMORSE.

</details>


### [101] [FreCast: Refining Radar Echo Intensity via Phase-Preserving Amplitude Residual Diffusion for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08436)
*Heping Fang,Zihuai Yin,Kaicheng Mao,Peiguang Zhang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreCast，一个两阶段雷达回波预测框架：第一阶段生成初始预测，第二阶段以初始预测的空间结构为约束，修正各位置的强度偏差。在三个数据集上取得了持续的技能指标提升，并在较长预报时效下更好地保持了雨带连续性和强降水结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型虽然能大致捕捉未来降水的整体空间分布，但在单个位置上的雷达回波强度预测仍存在显著偏差。与其无空间约束地重新生成整个回波序列，不如利用预测的降水结构来引导各位置回波强度的精细化修正。

Method: FreCast采用两阶段框架：第一阶段生成未来雷达回波的初始预测；第二阶段以初始预测的空间结构作为约束，进一步修正第一阶段预测中各个位置的回波强度偏差。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，FreCast在预报技能指标上取得了一致性提升。定性结果显示，FreCast在较长预报时效下能更好地保持雨带连续性和强降水结构。

Conclusion: 通过结构引导的精细修正策略，FreCast有效降低了逐位置的回波强度预测误差，是一种有前景的降水临近预报方法。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting predicts the spatiotemporal evolution of future radar echoes from historical radar echo sequences, thereby estimating the occurrence, development, and movement of precipitation over the near term. In recent years, deep learning has become an important approach to precipitation nowcasting. Although state-of-the-art models can generally capture the overall spatial distribution of future precipitation, their predictions still exhibit substantial biases in radar echo intensity at individual locations. This observation motivates a more targeted strategy for reducing forecast errors. Instead of regenerating an entire radar echo sequence without spatial constraints, the predicted precipitation structure can be used to guide the refinement of echo intensities at individual locations. This structure-guided refinement directly targets echo intensity biases. Accordingly, we propose FreCast, a two-stage framework for radar echo prediction. The first stage generates an initial forecast of future radar echoes. The second stage uses the spatial structure of the initial forecast as a constraint to further correct intensity biases at individual locations in the first-stage prediction. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that FreCast achieves consistent improvements across forecast skill metrics. Qualitative results further show that FreCast better preserves rainband continuity and intense precipitation structures at longer lead times.

</details>


### [102] [InstructionCrafter: Generating Consistent and High-Fidelity Visual Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08460)
*Shun Okamoto,Satoshi Iizuka,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructionCrafter，一个基于扩散模型的框架，用于根据文本指令生成逐步的视觉指令图像序列。其核心思想是通过空间冻结训练和指令感知适配器，将时间/指令对齐优化与逐帧视觉质量优化分离，在保持生成先验的同时提升跨帧一致性和步骤忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文生图方法难以同时满足生成逐步视觉指令的三个关键属性：步骤忠实性、跨图像一致性和逐帧视觉质量。已有方法要么因独立采样破坏一致性，要么在低质量视频上微调导致逐帧质量下降，要么使用冻结骨干网络缺乏多步骤理解能力。

Method: InstructionCrafter基于预训练视频扩散骨干网络，采用：1) 空间冻结训练——冻结控制逐帧细节的空间层，仅更新时间和文本条件路径，保留生成先验并减少约50%的可训练参数；2) 两个轻量级适配器——一致适配器聚合整个指令序列和相邻步骤的文本线索以保持对象身份和属性一致，上下文感知时间适配器将交叉注意力输出转换为时间自注意力的偏置，显式传播帧间关系。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在步骤忠实性、跨图像一致性和逐帧视觉质量方面均达到最先进的整体性能，同时显著减少了噪声、模糊和虚假字幕。

Conclusion: 通过分离时间/指令对齐与逐帧视觉质量的优化，InstructionCrafter有效解决了多步视觉指令生成中的核心矛盾，为相关任务提供了新的技术路径，且代码和模型将公开。

Abstract: Given textual task instructions, generating step-by-step visual instructions as an image sequence requires the simultaneous satisfaction of multiple properties, specifically step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality. Existing text-to-image generation approaches rarely meet all three properties, owing to independent sampling that breaks consistency, finetuning on low-quality video that degrades per-frame quality, and frozen backbones that lack multi-step understanding. In this work, we propose InstructionCrafter, a diffusion-based framework with the key idea of separating the optimization of temporal and instructional alignment from per-frame visual quality via (1) spatial-freeze training and (2) instruction-aware adapters. Built on a pretrained video diffusion backbone, InstructionCrafter freezes the spatial layers that control per-frame detail and updates only temporal and text-conditioning pathways to learn instruction semantics and inter-step relations, which preserves the generative prior for per-frame quality and reduces trainable parameters by about 50 percent compared with full finetuning. We also introduce two lightweight adapters that enhance the model's understanding of instructional context. The Consistent Adapter aggregates textual cues from the entire instruction sequence and from neighboring steps to keep object identity and attributes consistent across frames, and the Context-Aware Temporal Adapter converts cross-attention outputs into biases for temporal self-attention, explicitly propagating inter-frame relations. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance on step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality while significantly reducing noise, blur, and spurious subtitles. Our code and trained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [103] [RayLift: Lifting Complementary Ray-Wise Evidence with 3D Geometry Priors for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08476)
*Meng Wang,Hongxia Yu,Wenzhe He,Xingdong Song,Huilong Pi,Jiapeng Zhang,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RayLift框架，用于相机三维语义场景补全（SSC）。不同于现有方法将立体深度视为确定性几何约束，RayLift将立体几何作为度量参考，并结合射线证据自适应恢复可靠的三维结构。核心包含三个模块：互补上下文编码器（从冻结的3D视觉基础模型提取几何先验）、深度射线证据提升模块（联合建模几何不相似性、深度置信度和空间不确定性来沿射线自适应采样加权候选表面位置）、语义感知体素集成器（将射线证据注入体素特征）。在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360上取得竞争性能且优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的3D语义场景补全方法通常将立体深度估计视为确定性的几何约束，导致深度不确定性和局部匹配误差直接传播到体素表示中，影响场景重建精度和语义理解质量。

Method: RayLift包含三个核心模块：(1) 互补上下文编码器，利用冻结的3D视觉基础模型提取几何感知先验以丰富场景上下文；(2) 深度射线证据提升模块，联合建模几何不相似性、深度置信度和空间不确定性，沿相机射线自适应采样和加权候选表面位置；(3) 语义感知体素集成器，通过显式建模空间支持将射线证据注入体素特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，RayLift取得了具有竞争力的性能，并一致性地优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RayLift通过将立体几何作为度量参考而非确定性约束，并结合自适应射线证据机制，有效缓解了深度不确定性对3D语义场景补全的负面影响，为基于相机的场景理解提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic scene completion (SSC) provides comprehensive scene understanding for autonomous driving and robotics. However, existing methods often treat stereo depth estimates as deterministic geometric constraints, causing depth uncertainty and local correspondence errors to propagate directly into voxel representations. To address this issue, we propose RayLift, a framework that uses stereo geometry as a metric reference while incorporating complementary ray evidence to recover reliable 3D structures adaptively. RayLift first employs a Complementary Context Encoder that extracts geometry-aware priors from a frozen 3D vision foundation model, thereby enriching the scene context. It then introduces a Depth Ray Evidence Lifter module that jointly models geometric dissimilarity, depth confidence, and spatial uncertainty to adaptively sample and weight candidate surface locations along each camera ray. Finally, a Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator injects the resulting ray evidence into voxel features by explicitly modeling their spatial support. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that RayLift achieves competitive performance and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [104] [RenderMatte: Exact-Alpha Rendering and Group-Relative Alignment for Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08487)
*Zecheng Ren,Yafei Hu,Jianing Zhao,Ruichen Cong,Qun Jin,Yiren Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RenderMatte，一个基于trimap引导的抠图框架，通过对FLUX.1 Kontext进行全参数微调，结合alpha边缘目标和组相对alpha对齐策略，并构建大规模合成数据集RenderMatte dataset，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界场景中的精确alpha估计仍具挑战性，因为真实前景物体外观多样、透明度模式复杂，现有方法在语义模糊和细粒度透明度变化上表现不佳，尤其是在难以监督的稀疏边界区域。

Method: 1) 基于FLUX.1 Kontext进行全参数微调，利用图像编辑先验进行结构保持的alpha预测；2) 提出alpha-edge目标函数，保留潜在流匹配信号同时加强像素空间边界监督；3) 引入组相对alpha对齐的后训练策略，在同一trimap条件下比较多个采样结果，使用抠图专属奖励衡量alpha准确性、边界保真度、trimap合规性和合成一致性；4) 构建RenderMatte数据集，包含3D渲染RGBA前景与多样多源资产的合成数据，具有精确的strand级alpha标注。

Result: 在全部基准测试中均达到最先进的性能，验证了该方法通往开放世界高保真抠图的可扩展路径。

Conclusion: RenderMatte展示了将图像编辑先验融入抠图任务的有效性，通过精确边缘标注的大规模合成数据与针对性训练策略，为开放世界场景中的高质量抠图提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Image matting is an essential enabling technology for modern visual content production, where foreground extraction determines the realism and editability of downstream creation workflows. However, precise alpha estimation in open-world scenes remains challenging because real foregrounds exhibit highly diverse appearances and opacity patterns. This makes existing methods struggle with semantic ambiguity and fine-grained opacity variation, especially in sparse boundary regions that are fragile and difficult to supervise. To address this gap, we present RenderMatte, a trimap-guided matting framework that adapts FLUX.1 Kontext through full-parameter fine-tuning, leveraging image editing priors for structure-preserving alpha prediction. During supervised adaptation, an alpha-edge objective preserves the latent flow-matching signal while strengthening pixel-space boundary supervision. We further introduce group-relative alpha alignment for post-training. It compares multiple mattes sampled under the same trimap condition using matting-specific rewards for alpha accuracy, boundary fidelity, trimap compliance, and compositional consistency. To overcome the lack of precise edge annotations, we construct the RenderMatte dataset, a large-scale synthetic dataset combining 3D-rendered RGBA foregrounds with diverse multi-source assets. It features exact strand-level alpha annotations and diverse background composites. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks, demonstrating a scalable path toward high-fidelity matting in open-world scenes.

</details>


### [105] [Linguistically-Aligned and Visually-Grounded Preference Optimization for Clinically-Augmented Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08494)
*Qiang Hu,Yuxuan Luo,Yingjie Guo,Hao Wang,Qimei Wang,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DPO-Clin，一个面向医学报告生成的后期训练框架，通过实体级临床诊断（ECD）、检索增强的多模态DPO（M²DPO）和反事实修正三种策略，将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现和跨模态对齐，显著提升SFT基线在临床指标上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DPO的医学报告生成方法采用朴素的偏好构建方式（直接配对模型生成报告与真实报告），导致临床关键发现与无关语言特征被混淆，且缺乏明确的视觉-语言对齐，限制了模型可靠性。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) 实体级临床诊断（ECD）模块，进行精确的实体级事实诊断以生成语言对齐的报告偏好对；(2) M²DPO，一种检索增强的多模态DPO变体，通过视觉上下文切换触发文本偏好反转以实现细粒度跨模态对齐；(3) 对正确但高不确定性的预测实体施加反事实修改，构建针对性偏好数据以缓解潜在风险。

Result: 在两个公共胸部X射线数据集（MIMIC-CXR和IU X-Ray）及一个内部内窥镜数据集上，DPO-Clin显著优于SFT基线，并在临床相关指标上超越现有DPO-based MRG方法，展现出对多种基线架构和医学影像模态的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DPO-Clin通过将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现和跨模态对齐，有效解决了现有DPO方法在医学报告生成中的偏好构建缺陷，提升了模型的临床可靠性和事实准确性，且具有良好的跨架构、跨模态泛化性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Medical Report Generation (MRG), the reliability remains constrained by the prevalence of factual errors. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm to enhance the performance of Supervised Fine-Tuned (SFT) MRG models, existing DPO-based MRG methods typically adopt a naive preference construction that directly pairs model-generated reports with ground truth reports. This strategy inadvertently entangles critical clinical findings with clinically irrelevant linguistic characteristics, and fundamentally lacks explicit vision-language alignment. To address these challenges, we propose DPO-Clin, a novel post-training framework that focuses preference optimization on clinical findings and cross-modal alignment. First, we introduce the Entity-level Clinical Diagnostic (ECD) module to perform a precise entity-level factual diagnosis. ECD guides the generation of linguistically-aligned report preference pairs, isolating clinical discrepancies from linguistic variations. Second, to achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment, we develop M$^2$DPO, a retrieval-augmented multi-modal DPO variant that enforces textual preference inversion triggered by visual context switches. Third, we locate correct yet highly uncertain predicted entities and apply counterfactual modifications to construct targeted preference data for latent risk mitigation, thereby further enhancing the model reliability. Extensive experiments on two public chest X-ray datasets (MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray) and an in-house endoscopy dataset demonstrate that DPO-Clin significantly improves the SFT baselines on clinical-aware metrics. Furthermore, it achieves superior performance over existing DPO-based MRG methods, exhibiting robust generalizability across distinct baseline architectures and diverse medical imaging modalities.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Adaptive Super-Resolution and Quality Assessment via Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08508)
*Ajeet Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于测试时自适应（TTA）的统一范式，用于真实条件下自适应视频超分辨率和感知质量建模，无需重新训练或高分辨率监督即可提升鲁棒性和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数视频超分辨率方法在异构设备、编解码器和网络环境导致的未知退化下难以泛化，亟需一种无需重新训练即可提升鲁棒性的统一解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估框架，为未知失真下的VSR提供感知指导；2) 开发基于Transformer的屏幕内容超分辨率架构，保持文本清晰度和结构保真；3) 引入区域感知的TTA策略，选择性精细化文本与非文本区域，无需高分辨率真值。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的实验结果表明，该方法的感知质量和可读性得到了一致性提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建跨未知域泛化的全自适应视频增强系统奠定了框架基础，并计划继续推进相关工作。

Abstract: This paper presents doctoral research on adaptive video super-resolution and perceptual quality modeling under real-world conditions. Existing video super-resolution (VSR) methods struggle to generalize under unknown degradations arising from heterogeneous devices, codecs, and network environments. We address this challenge through test-time adaptation (TTA), a unified paradigm that improves robustness and perceptual quality without retraining or high-quality supervision. Specifically, we: 1) propose a TTA-based framework for no-reference video quality assessment (VQA), where adapted quality predictions provide perceptual guidance for VSR under unseen distortions; 2) develop a transformer-based architecture for screen-content super-resolution that preserves text clarity and structural fidelity; and 3) introduce a region-aware TTA strategy that selectively refines text and non-text regions without requiring high-resolution ground truth. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in perceptual quality and readability. We also outline ongoing work toward fully adaptive video enhancement systems capable of generalizing across unseen domains.

</details>


### [107] [eBIRD: Event-based Intensity Image Reconstruction Using Controllable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08519)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Fabian Valderrama,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出eBIRD框架，将DDPM与ControlNet条件化结合，用于从事件流重建强度图像。在N-MNIST（手写数字）和RGBE-Gaze（人脸）两个数据集上评估通用与专用扩散学习策略，结果表明通用模型在N-MNIST上表现最佳（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982），而专用模型在RGBE-Gaze上表现最优（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605），说明可控扩散模型是事件引导重建的有力方案，但最佳学习策略取决于重建领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流的二元性、稀疏性和异步性使得从事件数据重建强度图像极具挑战性，现有方法难以充分利用事件的时序信息。

Method: 提出eBIRD框架，将去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）与基于ControlNet的条件化机制相结合，通过事件流引导强度图像重建，并在33ms事件窗口下比较通用和专用两种扩散学习策略。

Result: 在N-MNIST上通用模型取得最佳效果（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB）；在RGBE-Gaze上专用模型最优（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB），初步验证了可控扩散模型的可行性。

Conclusion: 可控扩散模型是事件引导强度图像重建的有效途径，但不同领域需采用不同的学习策略，通用与专用策略各有优劣。

Abstract: Intensity-image reconstruction from event streams remains a challenging problem due to the binary, sparse, and asynchronous nature of event data. This work proposes eBIRD, an event-guided reconstruction framework that combines a DDPM with ControlNet-based conditioning. We analyze generic and specialized diffusion learning strategies for handwritten digit (N-MNIST) and face (RGBE-Gaze) reconstruction using 33ms event windows. On N-MNIST, the general model achieves the best reconstruction quality (MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB), whereas the specialized model performs best on RGBE-Gaze (MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB). These preliminary results suggest that controllable diffusion models are a promising approach for event-guided intensity-image reconstruction, while highlighting that the preferred learning strategy depends on the reconstruction domain.

</details>


### [108] [A Combined Feature-Based Framework for Disguise and Spoofing Detection in Face Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08521)
*Sangiya Pararajasingham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对人脸识别中两种常见且通常被分开处理的失败模式——欺骗攻击（照片/视频冒充）和伪装（合法用户因外观变化被拒绝）——提出了五种结合特征提取与分类的流水线方法（PM、LPM、HPM、SM、HM），在统一框架内同时解决两个问题。实验表明HOG-based流水线（HPM）表现最稳定，LBP-based流水线（LPM）在欺骗检测上准确率最高但姿态鲁棒性较弱，揭示了两种模式之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸识别研究通常将欺骗攻击防护和伪装鲁棒性作为两个独立的问题分别处理，缺乏一个能够同时应对这两种失败模式的统一框架。作者希望探索经典特征表示是否能在一个框架内同时解决欺骗检测和伪装鲁棒性，并量化不同特征表示方法在此权衡中的表现差异。

Method: 本文提出并比较了五种组合特征提取与分类的流水线：PM（PCA+最小欧氏距离）、LPM（LBP+PCA+MED）、HPM（HOG+PCA+MED）、SM（SURF+MED）和HM（Harris角点+MED）。每条流水线遵循两阶段流程：预处理、特征提取、特征过滤和分类。模型在来自FEI、伪装人脸数据库和NUAA数据库的115名被试数据上训练，并在六种测试条件下评估。

Result: HPM在混合外观伪装条件下达到94.59%的准确率，在姿态和光照变化下达到81.5-93.2%，在欺骗检测上达到91.67%。LPM在欺骗检测上达到最高的93.2%准确率，但对姿态变化的鲁棒性较弱。实验结果揭示了欺骗敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间存在可测量的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 经典特征表示在欺骗检测与伪装鲁棒性之间存在固有的权衡，没有任何单一方法能在所有条件下都表现最佳。这一发现推动了后续向深度学习方法和跨数据库扩展的研究方向，以寻求更好的性能平衡。

Abstract: Face recognition systems face two distinct, commonly-separated failure modes: spoofing, where an impostor presents a photograph or video of an authorized user, and disguise, where a legitimate user is rejected because their appearance differs from their enrolled template due to accessories, facial hair, illumination, or pose. This paper proposes and compares five combined feature-extraction and classification pipelines that address both problems within a single framework: PM (PCA and Minimum Euclidean Distance, MED), LPM (Local Binary Patterns with PCA and MED), HPM (Histogram of Oriented Gradients with PCA and MED), SM (Speeded-Up Robust Features with MED), and HM (Harris corner features with MED). Each pipeline follows a common two-phase process comprising pre-processing, feature extraction, feature filtering, and classification. The methods were trained on 115 subjects drawn from the FEI, Disguised Faces Database, and NUAA databases and evaluated on six test conditions covering mixed appearances, frontal faces, dark illumination, left- and right-turned poses, and photo-spoofing attempts. The HOG-based pipeline (HPM) achieved the most consistent performance across conditions, with 94.59% accuracy on mixed-appearance disguise, 81.5-93.2% across pose and illumination variants, and 91.67% on spoofing, while the LBP-based pipeline (LPM) achieved the highest spoofing-detection accuracy (93.2%) but weaker robustness to pose change. These results reveal a measurable trade-off between spoof sensitivity and disguise robustness among classical feature representations, motivating the deep-learning and cross-database extensions discussed in the concluding sections.

</details>


### [109] [ERF-GS: Reconstructing Fast Motion from Disjoint Event-RGB Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08531)
*Xiaoyang Bai,Zhenyang Li,Weiwei Xu,Edmund Y. Lam,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ERF-GS框架，将事件相机信息集成到3D高斯泼溅的优化与稠密化阶段，用于动态3D场景重建，尤其针对快速运动物体和严重运动模糊的场景。该框架在真实仿真设置下开发，支持事件信息独立于RGB输入的训练，在Neu3D和Nvidia数据集上优于4DGS基线和E-D3DGS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于帧式视频的动态3D场景重建方法（如NeRF和3DGS）在处理快速运动物体时存在困难，在体育运动、动物拍摄等场景中表现不佳，亟需能够应对高动态范围和高帧率需求的解决方案。

Method: ERF-GS将事件相机的高帧率信息集成到高斯泼溅管道的优化和稠密化两个阶段中，采用真实仿真设置进行开发，实现了事件学习与RGB输入的解耦，使其能够适用于具有复杂布局、低帧率和严重运动模糊的自然视频场景。

Result: 在Neu3D和Nvidia数据集的不同变体（包含模糊RGB帧和RGB-事件视角不一致的场景）上，ERF-GS在重建精度上优于4DGS基线和同期方法E-D3DGS。

Conclusion: ERF-GS通过融合事件信息增强了动态3D场景重建能力，尤其在快速运动和模糊场景下表现优异，且具备从合成数据推广到自然视频的泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-driven representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) have revolutionized the field of dynamic 3D scene reconstruction with improved visual precision and scalability. However, the reconstruction of fast-moving objects remains a challenge; existing methods based on conventional frame-based videos often struggle in scenarios such as sports events and animal videography. We propose an event-RGB fusion Gaussian splatting (ERF-GS) framework that integrates event information into both optimization and densification stages of the Gaussian splatting pipeline, taking advantage of novel event sensors with high frame-rate. Unlike many other event-assisted scene reconstruction methods, ERF-GS was developed using realistic simulation settings and realizes event-based learning detached from RGB inputs. This design enables its application beyond straightforward synthetic data into the realm of natural video with complex layout, low frame rates and severe motion blur. Our experiments show that ERF-GS outperforms both the 4DGS baseline and the concurrent E-D3DGS on different variants of the Neu3D and Nvidia datasets which include blurry RGB frames and disjoint RGB-event viewpoints. Our code is available at https://github.com/andrewbxy/ERF-GS.

</details>


### [110] [Rethinking Attention Locality in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08541)
*Zeqi Zheng,Zizheng Zhu,Yuping Yan,Wenxuan Pan,Zhaofei Yu,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCLA-BCP方法，通过空间连续局部注意力与边界连续通路增强Spiking Transformer的空间局部性，并设计了分层局部性部署策略，在多个数据集上显著提升检测与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spiking Transformer中的Spiking Self-Attention(SSA)缺乏空间局部token交互能力，现有局部性增强方法未能保证在不同层和不同架构中一致地实现空间局部性，且统一应用相同局部性增强忽略了架构依赖的部署需求。

Method: 提出SCLA（空间连续局部注意力，在非重叠的空间相邻token区域内计算注意力）和BCP（边界连续通路，通过轻量卷积通路实现跨边界信息交换），并开发分层局部性部署策略以适配两种主流Spiking Transformer架构。

Result: 在7个静态和神经形态数据集上（涵盖分类、检测、分割任务）取得一致改进，COCO 2017上mAP@50提升最高9.50%，ADE20K上mIoU提升最高3.42%，且参数和能耗开销有限。

Conclusion: 通过MAD分析揭示了计算局部性不等于空间局部性，提出的SCLA-BCP能有效增强空间局部性并带来显著性能提升，可视化、MAD分析和消融实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers provide a promising paradigm for efficient visual processing with spike-driven computation, yet their Softmax-free Spiking Self-Attention (SSA) struggles to establish spatially localized token interactions. Although existing locality-enhanced SSA methods improve accuracy, it remains unclear whether they consistently induce spatial locality across layers and different Spiking Transformer architectures. Through Mean Attention Distance (MAD) analysis, we reveal that computational locality does not necessarily translate into spatial locality and show that uniformly applying the same locality enhancement overlooks architecture-dependent deployment requirements. Motivated by these observations, we propose Spatially Contiguous Local Attention with Boundary Continuity Pathway (SCLA-BCP). SCLA computes attention within non-overlapping regions of spatially adjacent tokens, while BCP facilitates cross-boundary information exchange through a lightweight convolutional pathway. Furthermore, we develop a hierarchical locality deployment strategy to effectively apply SCLA-BCP across the two major Spiking Transformer architectures. Extensive experiments on seven static and neuromorphic datasets covering classification, detection, and segmentation demonstrate consistent improvements with limited parameter and energy overhead. Notably, our approach improves mAP@50 by up to 9.50% on COCO 2017 and mIoU by up to 3.42% on ADE20K. Visualizations, MAD analysis, and ablation studies further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [111] [SC-Diff: Semantically Calibrated Diffusion for Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08555)
*Junyin Zhang,Siyu Huang,Jianxiong Ye,Haowei Gong,Ruicheng Zhang,Deyu Meng,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SC-Diff是一个语义校准的潜扩散框架，利用SAM3提取语义掩码，在去噪网络内部通过语义引导的自注意力校准（SGSC）增强相同类别token之间的交互，减少跨类别干扰，提高可见光到红外图像翻译的语义一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的可见光到红外图像翻译方法仅将语义先验作为外部条件使用，没有在去噪网络内部显式调节token之间的交互，导致生成图像难以保持目标位置、形状和语义布局，无法满足红外标注复用所需的一致性要求。

Method: 1) 使用预训练SAM3模型结合文本提示从可见光图像提取类别语义掩码并合并成语义图，与可见光图像融合作为输入条件；2) 将语义图转换为token级语义标签；3) 提出Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration (SGSC)，对同类别query-key对施加自适应正偏置，偏置强度取决于注意力在语义类别间的分散度、query自身类别的注意力权重以及原始注意力分数，实现软校准以保留全局上下文交互。

Result: 大量实验表明，SC-Diff在感知质量上优于现有方法，且生成的合成红外训练数据能更有效地提升下游红外目标检测的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将语义先验同时用于条件引导和内部自注意力校准，SC-Diff有效解决了扩散模型在红外图像翻译中语义不一致的问题，验证了软校准策略在减少跨类别干扰同时保留全局交互方面的有效性。

Abstract: Visible-to-infrared image translation provides a practical way to expand infrared training data using abundant visible images. Diffusion models are promising for this task because of their strong generative performance. However, existing diffusion-based methods typically use semantic priors only as external conditions, without explicitly regulating token interactions within the denoising network. Consequently, they struggle to preserve object locations, shapes, and semantic layouts required for reliable annotation reuse. We propose SC-Diff, a semantically calibrated latent diffusion framework that uses semantic priors for both conditional guidance and internal self-attention calibration. A pretrained SAM3 model with predefined text prompts first extracts category-specific semantic masks from visible images. These masks are merged into a semantic map and fused with the visible image as the input condition. The same map is converted into token-level semantic labels to calibrate self-attention in the denoising network. Based on these labels, we introduce Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration (SGSC), which adaptively applies positive biases to query-key pairs of the same category. The query-wise calibration strength depends on the dispersion of attention across semantic categories and the attention assigned to the query's own category. The original attention scores further modulate the bias, giving greater calibration to same-category keys with stronger responses. This soft calibration reduces cross-category interference while retaining global contextual interactions, thereby improving semantic consistency in generated infrared images. Extensive experiments show that SC-Diff improves perceptual quality and produces more effective synthetic training data for downstream infrared object detection.

</details>


### [112] [On-Device Multi-Species Malaria Detection with Uncertainty-Calibrated Slide-Level Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08566)
*Idaya Seidu,Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Charles B. Delahunt,Carine Mukamakuza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向资源受限环境的疟疾自动诊断流水线，在临床约束条件下（包括停止准则、人在回路、多物种识别、厚血膜检测、不确定性计算和边缘设备部署）实现智能手机端离线推理，基于YOLOv13n和TensorFlow Lite完成四物种检测与虫血症量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在资源匮乏地区仍是主要致死原因，而专业显微镜检验人员稀缺。现有机器学习疟疾检测文献通常忽略实际临床部署所需的关键约束（如停止准则、可解释性、物种区分、离线运行等），导致算法难以真正落地。

Method: 与国家级卫生中心密切合作，开发了一套完整疟疾诊断流水线，覆盖六项临床需求：停止准则减少采集时间、人在回路审查机制、四物种（间日疟、恶性疟等）鉴别、厚血膜检测（显微镜标准操作）、高效不确定性计算辅助临床审查，以及基于边缘设备（YOLOv13n + TensorFlow Lite）的全离线推理方案。系统将单图像检测聚合为玻片级别虫血症，采用WHO标准量化方法。

Result: 在覆盖全部四个物种的2,739张标注图像上，系统达到mAP@0.5为0.863，单图像寄生虫计数相关性r=0.812，玻片级相关性r=0.951（软计数，每片10张图），完全离线运行，单张图像流水线耗时10.27±1.65秒。

Conclusion: 本工作表明，将真实临床部署约束纳入机器学习系统设计是可行的，能够在不牺牲检测精度的前提下满足医疗机构的实际需求，为疟疾自动诊断在资源受限地区的落地提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in resource-limited settings, where expert microscopists are scarce. Automated diagnosis based on microscopy images thus has strong potential to improve care delivery. But for an algorithm to deploy, a necessary requirement is that it meet a suite of non-obvious (from a machine learning (ML) perspective) clinical constraints. Therefore, in close consultation with a national health center we developed a malaria diagnosis pipeline which addresses key requirements listed by the health care center but typically ignored in the ML malaria literature. In particular, it includes: (i) stopping criteria (to reduce image acquisition and time-to-result); (ii) human-in-the-loop functionality (for review and accountability); (iii) multi-species discrimination (since treatment varies by species); (iv) thick film detection (standard for microscopy); (v) computationally-efficient uncertainty calculations (to aid clinician review); and (vi) an edge device platform (since internet can be spotty in this catchment area). The mobile system performs all inference on-device using YOLOv13n deployed via TensorFlow Lite. It detects four species and white blood cells from Giemsa-stained thick blood smear images, aggregating per-image detections into slide-level parasitemia with World Health Organization (WHO)-standard quantification. This paper highlights these various clinical constraints and offers methods to address them. Evaluated on 2,739 annotated images across all four species, the system achieves mAP@0.5 of 0.863, per-image parasite count correlation of r = 0.812, slide-level r = 0.951 (soft counting, 10 images/slide), and runs entirely offline with a pipeline time of 10.27 +- 1.65 s per image.

</details>


### [113] [CDGC-Net: 3D Medical Image Segmentation with Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention and Grouped Channel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08575)
*Zheyang Jing,Qin Lu,Jianwang Li,Yujie Yang,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDGC-Net，一种3D医学图像分割网络，通过协作双尺度空间注意力（CDSA）和分组层次通道注意力（GHCA）同时建模长距离解剖上下文与细粒度边界细节，在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA数据集上均取得最优分割性能，同时参数量和计算量相比UNETR++大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学图像分割方法将全局与局部特征分离建模或在不同特征层级处理，导致全局上下文与局部边界之间存在语义不匹配，同时通道关系建模不充分、空间与通道交互弱，且存在冗余表征。

Method: 提出CDGC-Net，每个CDGC块内包含两个核心模块：(1) 协作双尺度空间自注意力（CDSA），将注意力头分配到平行的局部窗口分支和全局稀疏分支，在同一特征层级捕获细粒度空间细节与长距离解剖上下文；(2) 分组层次通道注意力（GHCA），将通道组织为r个组，建模组内与跨组依赖关系。CDSA与GHCA共享键投影以保持特征参考一致性，残差特征对齐将精炼特征与原始表征融合。

Result: 在Synapse（86.96% DSC）、ACDC（92.91%）、BraTS（82.56%）和LA（93.52%）四个数据集上均超过现有最优方法，提升幅度为0.17至0.47个百分点。模型参数量25.83M、计算量28.62G FLOPs，相比UNETR++分别减少39.87%和40.30%。

Conclusion: CDGC-Net通过在同一特征层级协同建模双尺度空间信息并进行分组层次通道建模，实现了分割精度与计算复杂度之间的良好平衡，验证了所提方法在3D医学图像分割任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D medical image segmentation requires the integration of long-range anatomical context with fine boundary detail. Existing methods often model global and local features in separate modules or feature levels and perform channel recalibration independently. This may cause semantic mismatch between global context and local boundaries, insufficient channel relationship modeling, weak spatial-channel interaction, and redundant representations. We propose CDGC-Net, a 3D medical image segmentation network that combines cooperative dual-scale spatial attention with grouped hierarchical channel modeling. With-in each CDGC block, Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention (CDSA) assigns attention heads to parallel local-window and global-sparse branches. The two branches capture fine spatial details and long-range anatomical context at the same feature level. Their outputs are concatenated into an $N\times C$ spatial representation and directly passed to Grouped Hierarchical Channel Attention (GHCA). GHCA organizes the channels into $r$ groups and models both within-group and cross-group dependencies. CDSA and GHCA reuse a shared key projection to maintain a consistent feature reference. Residual feature alignment subsequently integrates the refined features with the original representation. On the Synapse, ACDC, BraTS, and LA datasets, CDGC-Net achieved mean DSC values of 86.96\%, 92.91\%, 82.56\%, and 93.52\%, respectively, exceeding the next-highest reported values by 0.39, 0.47, 0.17, and 0.32 percentage points. CDGC-Net contains 25.83M parameters and 28.62G FLOPs for an input size of $64\times128\times128$, reducing these quantities by 39.87\% and 40.30\%, respectively, relative to UNETR++. These results indicate a favorable trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>


### [114] [Where Is the Bee? Detecting Tiny Pollinators with a Single Collaborative-Head Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08580)
*Junsu Kim,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot挑战赛的解决方案，针对1920x1080田间关键帧中蜜蜂、熊蜂、食蚜蝇和蛾类的检测问题。通过比较10种探测器配置，选用带Swin-L骨干网络的Co-DINO；针对小目标（中位框仅占帧面积0.16%）和类别不平衡（蜜蜂占80%标签）两大难点，采用crop-mosaic池微调提升稀有类别标注占比（从19.9%升至55.1%），并引入类别加权simplex等角紧框架（ETF）损失。最终以0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95]在FinalTest上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛任务面临两个核心难点：小目标检测（中位框仅占帧面积0.16%）和严重的类别不平衡（蜜蜂占80%标签），亟需针对性的检测方案。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(1) 在held-out关键帧上比较10种探测器配置，选取性能最佳的Co-DINO搭配Swin-L骨干；(2) 在crop-mosaic池上微调，使3个稀有类别的合并标注占比从19.9%提升至55.1%；(3) 采用类别加权simplex etc（ETF）损失，将匹配的decoder查询投影状态拉向固定类别方向。训练总时长12+3+2个epoch。

Result: 在不使用推理时集成或测试时增强的情况下，在FinalTest上以0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95]排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过模型选择（Co-DINO+Swin-L）、数据增强策略（crop-mosaic）和损失函数改进（类别加权ETF损失）的组合，有效解决了小目标检测和类别不平衡问题，取得了挑战赛最优性能。

Abstract: The CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot Challenge asks us to detect bees, bumblebees, hoverflies, and moths in 1920x1080 field keyframes. Its annotations carry 2 difficulties: the median box occupies 0.16% of a frame, and bees account for 80% of the labels. To cope with the small boxes, we compare 10 recorded detector configurations on held-out keyframes; plain Co-DINO with a Swin-L backbone has the highest mAP in this comparison, so we select it. Training then addresses the bee dominance in 2 ways: fine-tuning on a crop-mosaic pool in which the combined annotation share of the 3 rare classes rises from 19.9% to 55.1%, and a class-weighted simplex equiangular tight frame (ETF) loss that pulls the projected states of matched decoder queries toward fixed class directions. The full schedule spans 12+3+2 epochs. Without inference-time ensembling or test-time augmentation, we rank first on FinalTest at 0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95].

</details>


### [115] [EvTrajGS: Accurate and Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08585)
*Zixuan Chen,Jiakai Zhang,Junhao Dong,Guangcong Wang,Jianhuang Lai,Yew-Soon Ong,Xiaohua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvTrajGS，一种用于未配准事件流的高效准确的3D高斯泼溅框架。该方法通过连续时间轨迹参数化相机运动，实现从粗位姿先验出发的联合位姿-场景优化，无需昂贵的SLAM流程，在重建质量和位姿估计精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机密集3D重建方法存在两难：基于现有位姿估计的方法效率高但重建保真度低（位姿初始化不准引入累积误差）；SLAM风格方法通过增量跟踪与建图获得高保真度但计算开销大。作者希望打破这一权衡，实现既准确又高效的位姿-场景联合优化。

Method: EvTrajGS采用以下三个关键设计：(1) 将相机运动参数化为从离散相机位姿初始化的连续时间轨迹，提供统一的位姿精化表示；(2) 将相邻轨迹状态聚合为时间耦合位姿，在联合优化中促进时间一致的位姿更新；(3) 引入损失加权的事件采样策略，自适应地强调时间上未充分重建的区间。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，EvTrajGS在几何重建质量和位姿估计精度上均优于最先进方法，PSNR提升3.8 dB，SSIM提升0.1，ATE RMSE降低超过40%，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: EvTrajGS通过连续时间轨迹建模与时间耦合位姿更新，成功解决了事件相机重建中效率与保真度的权衡问题，验证了无需SLAM风格管线也能实现高质量位姿-场景联合优化的可行性。

Abstract: Event cameras, with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and asynchronous sensing characteristics, have shown great potential for dense 3D reconstruction. Traditional reconstruction methods based on off-the-shelf pose estimates achieve high efficiency but produce low-fidelity results, as inaccurate pose initialization introduces cumulative reconstruction errors. In contrast, recent SLAM-style methods stabilize joint pose-scene optimization through incremental tracking and mapping, yielding higher reconstruction fidelity at the expense of considerable computational overhead. To address this trade-off, this paper presents EvTrajGS, an accurate and efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for unposed event streams. Our method enables reliable joint pose-scene optimization initialized from coarse pose priors, eliminating the need for computationally expensive SLAM-style pipelines. EvTrajGS parameterizes camera motion as a continuous-time trajectory initialized from discrete camera poses, providing a unified representation for pose refinement. We then aggregate adjacent trajectory states into a temporally coupled pose, promoting temporally consistent pose updates during joint optimization. Additionally, we introduce a loss-reweighted event sampling strategy to adaptively emphasize temporally under-reconstructed intervals. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that EvTrajGS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of both geometric reconstruction quality and pose estimation accuracy, achieving 3.8 dB higher PSNR, 0.1 higher SSIM, and over 40\% lower ATE RMSE while retaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [116] [RobustDefect-LLM: Explainable and Robustness-Aware Industrial Surface Defect Classification with Decision Support and AI-Assisted Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08589)
*Nazlıcan Düşünmez,Halûk Gümüşkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustDefect-LLM，一个将深度学习分类、可视化证据、置信度感知决策支持、AI辅助报告、可追溯存储和移动交互集成的工业表面缺陷检测框架。在NEU-DET数据集的1,799张六类图像上评估了四种CNN模型，MobileNetV3-Large取得最高测试准确率（99.26%），但模型在强图像退化下性能显著下降（低于40%）。保守的置信度路由策略实现了100%的观察选择准确率，整体验证了集成工作流的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表面缺陷检测需要将深度学习模型与实际质量控制流程深度集成，但现有研究往往只关注分类精度，缺乏对可视化证据、置信度评估、人工审查路由、报告生成和移动交互等全流程的整合考虑。

Method: 采用四种迁移学习CNN（ResNet50、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet121、MobileNetV3-Large）在六类NEU-DET数据集上评估；采用bootstrap置信区间和配对McNemar检验进行统计比较；引入Grad-CAM可视化证据、置信度阈值（<0.90）和top-2 margin（<0.10）路由到人工审查；在受控图像退化条件下进行鲁棒性评估；对AI生成报告进行确定性一致性检查。

Result: MobileNetV3-Large达到最高数值准确率99.26%和宏F1分数0.9926，CPU前向推理0.060秒/次（16.66 FPS）；轻度退化下准确率降至87.78%，强退化下低于40%；保守路由策略在33个合格案例中实现12.22%自动覆盖率及100%观察选择准确率，且两个分类错误均被路由至人工审查；100份报告全部通过一致性检查，平均延迟1.66秒。

Conclusion: 集成工作流的各环节在技术上均可行，但需注意模型对严重图像质量退化的敏感性，需要进一步校准、重复评估以及真实工业场景验证。

Abstract: This paper presents RobustDefect-LLM, an industrial surface-defect inspection framework integrating deep-learning classification, operator-facing visual evidence, confidence-aware decision support, controlled AI-assisted reporting, traceable storage, and mobile interaction in a unified quality-control workflow. Here, robustness-aware denotes explicit evaluation under controlled image degradation and confidence-aware review routing, not an intrinsic robustness guarantee. Four transfer-learning-based convolutional neural networks, ResNet50, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet121, and MobileNetV3-Large, were evaluated on 1,799 images from the six-class NEU-DET dataset using fixed training, validation, and held-out in-domain test partitions. MobileNetV3-Large achieved the highest numerical test accuracy (99.26%) and macro F1-score (0.9926), with a bootstrap 95% accuracy CI of 0.9815-1.0000. An exact paired McNemar test found no significant difference from DenseNet121 (p = 1.000). The selected model averaged 0.060 s per CPU forward pass (16.66 FPS). Under combined synthetic degradation, accuracy fell to 87.78% at mild intensity and below 40% at stronger intensities, revealing sensitivity to severe image-quality deterioration. Grad-CAM supplied visual evidence, while predictions with confidence below 0.90 or a top-2 margin below 0.10 were routed to HUMAN REVIEW. This conservative policy provided 12.22% automatic coverage and 100% observed selective accuracy among 33 eligible cases (95% CI: 89.43%-100.00%), while routing both observed classification errors to review. Under nominal controlled conditions, all 100 generated reports passed deterministic consistency checks, with a mean latency of 1.66 s. Results support the feasibility of the integrated workflow while emphasizing the need for calibration, repeated evaluation, and real-world industrial validation.

</details>


### [117] [Goal-oriented Navigation Instruction Generation with Tour Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08596)
*Fangdi Li,Juncheng Liao,Changxu Cheng,Jiazhi Wang,Senda Chen,Tao Wang,Wuyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VideoNIG，一个以目标为导向的视频导航指令生成任务，从第一人称旅游视频、初始观察和文本/视觉目标中生成导航指令，无需图或地图等中间表征。作者构建了包含60K旅游视频和37K多模态提示的仿真基准，并提出了一种两阶段课程学习框架（Action Warmup + Complexity Progression）来解决该任务，实验表明现有多模态大模型在此任务上表现不佳，而所提方法显著提升了指令质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航指令生成（NIG）大多仅作为视觉语言导航（VLN）的辅助任务，用于数据增强或多任务学习。然而从紧凑的环境先验信息生成导航指令需要精细的空间推理，尤其是当目标路线不简单跟随示范路线时，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 提出VideoNIG任务设定和两阶段课程学习框架：(1) Action Warmup阶段用于空间动作-视图对齐；(2) Complexity Progression阶段使用递增探索难度的轨迹进行训练。同时构建了包含60K旅游视频和37K多模态提示（具有渐进难度）的受控仿真基准，并设计了结合文本相似度、基于选择的空间一致性测试和下游导航执行的诊断评估协议。

Result: 实验表明现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在VideoNIG任务上表现不佳，而所提出的课程学习方法在互补的诊断指标上显著提升了指令生成质量。将VideoNIG生成的指令与VLN智能体集成，验证了该任务公式可用于端到端导航的可执行性。

Conclusion: VideoNIG作为一个目标导向的视频导航指令生成任务，为导航指令生成提供了新的研究方向。所提出的两阶段课程学习框架有效解决了从视频中生成高质量导航指令的挑战，并且生成的指令可以成功驱动下游VLN导航。

Abstract: Navigation Instruction Generation (NIG) aims to produce step-by-step natural language instructions for navigation guidance. Existing studies primarily treat NIG as an auxiliary task for vision-andlanguage navigation (VLN), focusing on data augmentation or multi-task learning. However, generating navigation instructions from compact environmental priors requires meticulous spatial reasoning, especially when the target route does not simply follow the demonstrated tour, and remains challenging for current multimodal models. In this work, we introduce VideoNIG, a goal-oriented video-grounded NIG task that generates navigation instructions from ego-centric tour videos, an initial observation, and a textual or visual goal, without relying on intermediate representations such as graphs and maps. We instantiate VideoNIG in a controlled simulator benchmark with 60K tour videos across continuous indoor environments and 37K multimodal prompts with progressive difficulty levels. We further introduce a diagnostic evaluation protocol that combines text similarity, choice-based spatial consistency tests, and downstream navigation execution. To address this task, we propose a two-stage Curriculum Learning framework that decomposes the learning into foundational motion perception and long-horizon navigation reasoning. Specifically, we first employ Action Warmup for spatial action-view alignment, followed by Complexity Progression using trajectories with increasing exploratory difficulty. Extensive experiments show that existing MLLMs struggle with VideoNIG, while our approach significantly improves instruction quality across complementary diagnostic metrics. Finally, integrating VideoNIG-generated instructions with a VLN agent demonstrates the executability of this task formulation for end-to-end navigation.

</details>


### [118] [Population-Scalable Multi-Agent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08600)
*Renjie Zhao,Yuxiang Wu,Mingyu Zhang,Jiaxin Li,Sisi Li,Yimin Sheng,Tianxi Tan,Zhenkai Zhang,Jianyi Zhu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Khora，一种可扩展的多智能体世界模型，通过将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，并使用群体无关的渲染机制，支持推理时扩展到任意数量的智能体而无需重新训练，实现了近似线性的实际扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在扩展到多智能体环境时面临根本性的可扩展性挑战：大多数方法在训练和推理时假设固定数量的智能体，将模型绑定到预定智能体群体，限制了推理时的可扩展性。

Method: 核心思想是跨视角一致性应源于共享世界状态（其演化不预设智能体数量），而智能体特定观察应通过统一渲染接口查询该状态生成。方法包括：将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，引入群体无关的渲染机制以整合其他智能体信息，通过共享世界状态而非视频生成器内部的稠密观察流交互来维持跨视角一致性。

Result: 定性实验表明该方法能泛化到未见过的智能体数量，同时保持视觉质量和多智能体一致性；实现了实时交互系统以展示可扩展的开放世界模拟，实际扩展规模与查询视角数量近似线性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦世界状态与渲染过程，Khora成功实现了无需重新训练即可扩展到任意数量智能体的多智能体世界建模，在保持跨视角一致性的同时获得了近似线性的可扩展性。

Abstract: World models have recently achieved impressive progress in visual prediction and interactive generation, but extending them to multi-agent environments introduces a fundamental scalability challenge. Existing methods generally assume a fixed number of agents during training and inference, which ties the model to a pre-determined agent population and limits inference-time scalability. Our key insight is that cross-view consistency should arise from a shared world state whose evolution does not assume a predefined number of agents, while agent-specific observations should be generated by querying this state through a unified rendering interface. Based on this insight, we propose Khora, a scalable multi-agent world model that supports inference-time expansion to arbitrary numbers of agents without retraining. Our framework decouples world-state evolution from visual rendering and introduces a population-agnostic rendering mechanism for incorporating other agent information. This design maintains cross-view consistency through the shared world state rather than through dense interactions among observation streams inside the expensive video generator, enabling approximately linear practical scaling with the number of queried views. Qualitative experiments demonstrate that our approach generalizes to unseen numbers of agents while maintaining visual quality and multi-agent consistency. We further implement a real-time interactive system to demonstrate scalable open-world simulation.

</details>


### [119] [REVEAL: A Rubric-Guided Agent for Explicit Evidence Sufficiency Verificationin Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08612)
*Caijun Yan,Yang Zhou,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Yichen Li,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVEAL是一个基于评分准则的智能体框架，通过自适应视觉相似性预处理将连续帧分组为自然事件单元，构建离线-在线视频记忆，并利用自动构建的评分准则库显式验证检索证据的充分性，在证据不足时定位缺失线索并定向补检索，无需额外训练即可在长视频问答任务上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强和记忆增强方法存在两大问题：一是依赖固定长度的时序分块和静态离线记忆库，会割裂连续事件且无法在实时推理中自适应；二是过度关注检索相关性而忽视证据充分性，在仅获得语义相关线索时就停止回答，即使关键的时间、因果或细粒度动作证据仍然缺失。

Method: REVEAL框架包含三个核心组件：1) 基于视觉相似性的自适应预处理管道，将视觉上连贯的相邻帧分组为自然事件单元，构建离线捕捉全局视频上下文、在线动态维护问题条件记忆的离线-在线视频记忆；2) 自动构造的评分准则库，用于显式验证检索到的证据是否满足充分性标准；3) 验证失败时定位缺失线索并定向重新检索补充信息的机制。

Result: 在大量实验中，不依赖任何额外训练，REVEAL在长视频问答任务上一致地超越了闭源和开源的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 显式验证证据充分性而非仅停留在语义相关性，能够检索到先前方法遗漏的决定性线索，从而产生更可靠的长视频推理结果。

Abstract: Recently, retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented methods have emerged as two promising paradigms for long-video question answering. However, existing methods typically rely on rigid, fixed-length temporal chunking (e.g., 10s) and static offline memory banks, which not only fragment coherent continuous events but also fail to adapt during real-time reasoning. Moreover, whether using multi-scale summaries or multimodal knowledge graphs, current approaches prioritize retrieval relevance while overlooking evidence sufficiency, often stopping to answer once only semantically relevant clues are retrieved, even when key temporal, causal, or fine-grained action evidence is still missing. To tackle these challenges, we propose REVEAL, a rubric-guided agent framework. As a foundation, we introduce an adaptive visual-similarity-based preprocessing pipeline that groups visually coherent adjacent frames into natural event units to construct an offline-online video memory---capturing global video context offline while dynamically maintaining question-conditioned memory online. Built upon this structured memory, REVEAL uses an automatically constructed rubric library to explicitly verify whether retrieved evidence satisfies sufficiency criteria, pinpoints missing clues upon verification failure, and directs targeted re-retrieval for complementary information. Without any extra training, REVEAL consistently outperforms both closed-source and open-source state-of-the-art methods across extensive experiments. These results show that explicitly verifying evidence sufficiency, rather than stopping at semantic relevance, retrieves the decisive clues that prior methods miss and yields more reliable long-video reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [VADER: Adaptive Debiasing for Hallucination Mitigation in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08622)
*Dong Xing,Jiaxin Chen,Hang Yang,Peixun Liu,Qiushi Yang,Yuqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VADER，一个无需训练的框架，通过视觉焦点重分配（VFR）和选择性证据擦除（SEE）两个互补模块，对视频-问题输入自适应地进行证据重新加权，解决大型视频语言模型在开放视频理解中产生与视频证据不符的流畅回答的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型视频语言模型（LVLMs）在开放视频理解中虽然表现优异，但容易产生看似流畅却缺乏视频证据支撑的回答。已有的免训练方法要么使用全局固定的视觉干预，无法适应不同视频的融合路径；要么通过输入扰动构建对比分支，但会被跨帧冗余所补偿，导致失效。

Method: 提出VADER框架，包含两个互补模块：（1）视觉焦点重分配（VFR）：自动为每个视频-问题输入实例化干预策略，诊断层级的视觉到文本证据流，确定干预位置，并将pre-softmax注意力从系统token重新分配到视频token块；（2）选择性证据擦除（SEE）：独立地掩盖每帧中高重要性的视觉token，构建一个难以通过邻近帧补偿的先验偏置分支。最后通过对比解码，降低在选择性证据擦除后仍然保持高置信度的预测。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM上，VADER在事件级定位和时间一致性任务上取得了显著改进；在LLaVA-Video-7B上，EventHallusion基准达到了72.60%的准确率。

Conclusion: VADER通过视频自适应的证据重新加权机制，有效缓解了LVLMs在视频理解中的幻觉问题，验证了针对不同输入自适应干预策略和难以补偿的证据擦除方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in open-ended video understanding, yet they remain prone to fluent responses unsupported by video evidence. Existing training-free methods typically apply a globally fixed visual intervention or construct a contrastive branch through input perturbation. The former cannot accommodate video-dependent fusion paths, while the latter can be compensated by cross-frame redundancy. We therefore propose Video-Adaptive Debiasing via Evidence Reweighting (VADER), a training-free framework with two complementary modules. Visual Focus Reallocation (VFR) automatically instantiates an intervention policy for each video-question input: it diagnoses layer-wise visual-to-text evidence flow, determines where to intervene, and derives how strongly to reallocate pre-softmax attention from system-token to video-token blocks. Selective Evidence Erasure (SEE) independently masks high-importance visual tokens in every frame, constructing a prior-biased branch that is difficult to compensate through neighboring frames. Contrastive decoding then down-weights predictions that remain confident after selective evidence erasure. Across multiple VideoLLMs, VADER yields substantial improvements on event-level grounding and temporal consistency; on LLaVA-Video-7B, it reaches 72.60% accuracy on EventHallusion.

</details>


### [121] [VLZip: Unified Visual and Textual Compression for Interleaved Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08630)
*Yuqi Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yuyu Guo,Wenjie Yang,Lingchen Meng,Peng Di,Hang Yu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLZip是一个统一视觉和文本压缩的框架，通过将视觉和文本片段分层蒸馏为紧凑的"软前缀"注入解码器各层的隐藏状态，大幅缩短注意力序列同时保留细粒度全局上下文。实验表明它支持120K tokens训练（比基线提升6倍）、280K以上推理及高达2M tokens的内存扩展能力，在长上下文多模态推理中达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在处理超长交错图文序列时面临自注意力二次复杂度的问题，现有方案要么采用激进token剪枝导致不可逆信息损失，要么采用高效但不精确的架构，且忽略了文本组件的同等重要性。

Method: VLZip提出一个在纯Transformer架构内的统一框架：分层地将视觉和文本片段蒸馏为层特定的紧凑"软前缀"，注入每个解码器层的隐藏状态，从而大幅缩短注意力序列同时保留细粒度全局上下文。此外还引入了LongVLBench基准，基于视频叙事构建，要求整体叙事级推理能力。

Result: VLZip在长上下文多模态推理上取得领先性能，支持120K tokens训练（6倍提升）、280K tokens以上推理且显著降低内存，并展示可处理高达2M tokens的内存扩展能力。在现有方法崩溃的极端上下文长度下表现优异。

Conclusion: VLZip通过统一视觉与文本压缩，建立了高效且强大的长上下文多模态AI新标准，在极端上下文长度下依然保持高保真推理能力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) face significant challenges with ultra-long, interleaved image-text sequences due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Current solutions either resort to aggressive token pruning, risking irreversible information loss, or adopt efficient but less precise architectures, while largely ignoring the equally vital textual component. We introduce VLZip, a framework that unifies visual and textual compression for high-fidelity reasoning within a pure Transformer. At its core, VLZip hierarchically distills visual and textual segments into compact, layer-specific "soft prefixes" and injects them into each decoder layer's hidden states, drastically shortening the attention sequence while preserving fine-grained global context. To address deficient evaluations in the field, we also introduce LongVLBench, a new benchmark derived from video narratives that demands holistic, narrative-level reasoning. Extensive experiments show VLZip achieves leading performance on long-context multimodal reasoning, enabling training up to 120K tokens, a 6x increase over the baseline, and inference beyond 280K tokens with significantly reduced memory, while demonstrating the memory scalability to handle up to 2M tokens. By excelling at extreme context lengths where existing methods collapse, VLZip establishes an efficient and powerful new standard for long-context multimodal AI. Code is available at https://github.com/ShareLab-SII/VLZip.

</details>


### [122] [Agentic Visual Reasoning in Whole-Slide Pathology Images via Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08648)
*Jingyun Chen,Fengchun Liu,Linghan Cai,Songhan Jiang,Shenjin Huang,Hongpeng Wang,Lequan Yu,Yongbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptivePath，一个基于主动感知框架的病理全切片（WSI）视觉推理方法，将WSI证据获取建模为序贯决策问题，通过Navigator学习异常驱动的观察策略，结合Morphology Interpreter、Deliberator和Arbiter实现跨尺度的证据整合与推理，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI方法要么将密集采样补丁压缩为全局表示，要么使用启发式区域选择的预训练视觉-语言模型，导致预测与形态学之间联系薄弱，或缺乏经过病理学训练的观察策略。

Method: AdaptivePath将WSI证据获取形式化为序贯决策过程。Navigator从病理学家审查的标签中学习与问题无关的异常驱动导航策略，通过交替进行表征学习和PPO（近端策略优化）训练，并利用几何和外观一致性目标进行微调以稳定聚焦轨迹。推理时，Navigator在有限ROI预算下从低到高放大倍率分层获取稀疏观察。Morphology Interpreter将观察转化为问题条件化证据，Deliberator跨放大倍率评估证据并修正中间答案，Arbiter整合推理历史生成最终答案。

Result: AdaptivePath在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上达到最先进的零样本性能，在六个TCGA队列的癌症亚型分类中达到80.14%准确率。在盲法诊断效用研究中，使用AdaptivePath选择观察序列的病理学家达到82.9%的准确率。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，学习到的主动感知能够实现对千兆像素病理切片的有效且可追踪的视觉推理，AdaptivePath的观察序列具有诊断效用并能为病理学家提供辅助。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual reasoning requires identifying sparse diagnostic evidence in gigapixel pathology slides and integrating observations across spatial scales. Existing WSI methods either compress densely sampled patches into global representations or use pretrained vision-language models with heuristic region selection, weakening links between predictions and morphology or lacking pathology-trained observation policies. We present AdaptivePath, an active-perception framework that formulates WSI evidence acquisition as sequential decision making. The Navigator learns question-agnostic abnormality-driven navigation from pathologist-reviewed labels to select observation locations and spatial extents, avoiding costly question-specific trajectory annotations. We train this policy through alternating representation learning and proximal policy optimization, followed by fine-tuning with geometric and appearance consistency objectives to stabilize focus trajectories. During inference, the Navigator hierarchically acquires sparse observations from low to high magnification under a limited ROI budget. A Morphology Interpreter converts observations into question-conditioned evidence, while the Deliberator evaluates evidence and revises intermediate answers across magnifications. The Arbiter integrates deliberation history to produce final answers. AdaptivePath achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on WSI and region pathology VQA benchmarks and reaches 80.14% accuracy for cancer subtype classification across six TCGA cohorts. In a blinded diagnostic-utility study, pathologists using AdaptivePath-selected observation sequences achieve 82.9% accuracy. These results demonstrate that learned active perception enables effective and traceable visual reasoning over gigapixel pathology slides.

</details>
