TL;DR: 本文解决了PAC学习在Tsybakov噪声下的二十年未解开放问题，通过改进上界使其与已知最优下界匹配，从而建立了Tsybakov噪声下学习的误差保证最优界。算法通过自适应划分实例空间为不同噪声水平的区域，并在每个区域满足特定误差约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAC学习在Tsybakov噪声模型下的通用上界与下界之间存在对数因子的差距，这一差距自MN06的工作以来二十年间一直未被解决，是该领域的知名开放问题。

Method: 提出一种自适应划分实例空间的学习算法，将空间按噪声水平分为不同区域，并在概念类中返回一个在每个区域都满足特定误差约束的假设。

Result: 将学习上界从原来的上界改进到与已知最优下界匹配，证明了Tsybakov噪声下学习的误差保证是最优的，消除了对数因子差距。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Tsybakov噪声下PAC学习的二十年开放问题，建立了最优误差保证，其技术思路与近期非可实现学习领域的多个进展（如HLZ24和Han25）具有概念上的共同基础。

Abstract: Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning [Val84] is a fundamental learning model that has been extensively investigated. In this model, $\mathcal{H} \subseteq \{0,1\}^{\mathcal{X}}$ is a concept class, and $h^*\in\mathcal{H}$ is the target concept to be learned. Having access to i.i.d. labeled examples from a distribution $\mathcal{D}$ over $\mathcal{X}\times\{0,1\}$, which admits $h^*$ as the best concept in $\mathcal{H}$, the goal is to design a learning algorithm that outputs a hypothesis having low error competitive to $h^{*}$ with high probability.
  This model was initially studied under the realizable setting, which assumes that $h^*$ has no error. A natural relaxation is to allow label noise, that is, the true label can be flipped with probability $η\in(0,1/2)$. In reality, certain labels might be extremely noisy, especially for those points near the decision boundary. Hence, it is natural to allow very noisy points, though only rarely. This is quantified by a noise model introduced by [MT99] and [Tsy04], now known as Tsybakov noise. For learning general concept classes, [MN06] gave the general upper and lower bounds for error guarantees under Tsybakov noise. However, their upper and lower bounds differ by a logarithmic factor. Resolving this gap has remained a well-known open question for the past twenty years.
  In this work, we resolve this open question by improving the upper bound to match the best known lower bound, thus establishing the optimal error guarantee for learning under Tsybakov noise. Our learning algorithm operates by adaptively partitioning the instance space into regions, roughly corresponding to different noise levels, and returning a hypothesis in the concept class satisfying a specific error constraint for each region. Our technique shares a conceptual foundation with several recent advances in non-realizable learning, such as [HLZ24] and [Han25].

</details>


### [193] [FSTC-Encoder: Feature--Spatial--Temporal Correlation Learning for Generalizable RF Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08439)
*Jing Wang,Zhu Wang,Changlong Cheng,Yifan Guo,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FSTC-Encoder，通过特征、空间和时间三个维度的相关性建模来统一异构RF感知表示学习，在不同模态、设备和环境下保持高鲁棒性，在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构RF感知在特征结构、空间布局和时间尺度上差异巨大，导致现有模型难以跨设备、环境和RF模态复用。

Method: FSTC-Encoder包含三个核心模块：结构感知的特征编码适配不同信号结构、基于集合的空间编码聚合可变的观测数据、层次化时间编码同时捕捉局部变化和长程依赖。模型保持统一的空间-时间骨干架构，仅需调整特征配置和任务头即可适配不同任务。

Result: 在Widar3.0、CSI-Bench和XRF55上，多因子跨域协议下平均准确率达92.15%；在四个附加感知任务中的三个排名第一；在WiFi、毫米波雷达和RFID上表现稳定；跨模态性能差距从18.85%降至12.93%。

Conclusion: FSTC-Encoder展现了高领域鲁棒性、任务通用性和模态可扩展性，验证了统一异构RF表示学习的可行性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous RF sensing differs substantially in feature structure, spatial layout, and temporal scale, making existing models difficult to reuse across devices, environments, and RF modalities. We propose FSTC-Encoder, which unifies heterogeneous RF representation learning through feature, spatial, and temporal correlation modeling. Structure-aware feature encoding accommodates different signal structures, set-based spatial encoding aggregates variable observations, and hierarchical temporal encoding jointly captures local variations and long-range dependencies. Across sensing tasks and modalities, FSTC-Encoder retains the same spatial--temporal backbone architecture while varying only the feature configuration and task head. Across Widar3.0, CSI-Bench, and XRF55, FSTC-Encoder achieves 92.15% mean Accuracy under multi-factor cross-domain protocols, ranks first on three of four additional sensing tasks, remains consistently strong across WiFi, millimeter-wave radar, and RFID, and reduces the cross-modality performance gap from 18.85% to 12.93% through cross-RF learning. These results demonstrate that FSTC-Encoder achieves high domain robustness, task generality, and modality extensibility.

</details>


### [194] [MGMCL: Multi-Granularity Manifold Contrastive Learning With Neural ODEs for Cross-Subject EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08440)
*Xiang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGMCL框架，将跨受试者EEG情感识别重构为SPD黎曼流形上的连续表征学习，通过多粒度流形对比学习、神经常微分方程建模连续情感动态以及Gromov-Wasserstein流形对齐实现跨受试者泛化，在三个公开数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨受试者EEG情感识别面临两大挑战：个体间巨大差异导致泛化困难，以及现有离散化建模忽略了情感状态的连续性。此外，现有方法局限于欧氏空间并仅进行边际分布对齐，无法保留跨受试者的情感语义结构。

Method: MGMCL框架包含四个核心组件：1) 将EEG情感识别重新定义为SPD黎曼流形上的连续表示学习；2) 在实例、情感和轨迹三个粒度上实施多粒度流形对比学习，保留语义排序；3) 利用流形上的神经常微分方程建模连续情感动态；4) 采用Gromov-Wasserstein流形对齐实现跨受试者泛化，并引入弱监督学习从离散标签预测连续的效价-唤醒-支配维度。

Result: 在SEED、SEED-IV和DEAP三个公开数据集上分别达到91.23%、73.82%和76.38%的准确率，相比此前最优方法分别提升1.89%、1.66%和1.28%。

Conclusion: 将情感识别从欧氏空间离散建模转向黎曼流形连续表征，结合多粒度对比学习与流形对齐，能有效解决跨受试者EEG情感识别的个体差异和语义结构保持问题，验证了流形学习方法在该任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Cross-subject electroencephalogram (EEG)-based emotion recognition remains challenging due to substantial inter-individual variability and discrete formulation that overlooks affective continuity. Existing methods operate in Euclidean space and focus on marginal distribution alignment, failing to preserve the semantic structure of emotions across subjects. This article proposes MGMCL, reconceptualizing emotion recognition as learning continuous representations on symmetric positive definite (SPD) Riemannian manifolds. The frame?work introduces multi-granularity manifold contrastive learning at instance, emotion, and trajectory levels while preserving semantic ordering. Neural ordinary differential equations on manifolds model continuous emotion dynamics. Cross-subject generalization employs Gromov-Wasserstein manifold alignment. Weakly-supervised learning enables continuous valence-arousal-dominance prediction from discrete labels. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: 91.23% accuracy on SEED, 73.82% on SEED-IV, and 76.38% on DEAP, achieving consistent improvements of 1.89%, 1.66%, and 1.28% over previous best methods, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [No Unique Minimizer, No Problem: On the Consistency of Robust Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08489)
*Subhabrata Majumdar,Anand Deo,Partha Pratim Saha,Abhik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络分类器对标签噪声和对抗污染的敏感性，提出基于S-divergence鲁棒损失函数的分类器训练方法。在不假设参数可辨识性的前提下，建立了一致性理论：经验S-divergence最小化器在温和正则条件下收敛到总体最优等价类，并验证了三种网络架构的适用性，同时证明鲁棒训练算法的极限点是经验目标的平稳点。实验证明该方法在保持干净数据精度同时具备竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交叉熵训练的神经网络对标签噪声和对抗污染高度敏感。虽然已有鲁棒替代方案具有有界影响和抗污染能力，但在深度学习场景下其统计基础不足——因为神经网络的参数化不可辨识，总体损失最小化器是一个等价类而非唯一参数点，这给一致性理论带来了根本性困难。

Method: 将训练建模为在不可辨识参数空间上的随机优化问题，基于S-divergence损失族建立无需可辨识性假设的一致性理论。证明经验S-divergence最小化器在温和正则条件下收敛到总体最优等价类，并验证三种架构选择满足这些条件。同时证明鲁棒训练算法的极限点即为经验目标的平稳点。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准数据集上的实验表明，S-divergence训练在保持干净数据准确率的同时，性能与现有鲁棒方法相当，验证了理论的有效性和方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为鲁棒神经网络分类器提供了坚实的统计理论基础，通过S-divergence族在不依赖可辨识性假设的情况下建立了参数等价类层面的一致性，为噪声环境下深度学习模型的可靠训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Neural network classifiers trained by cross-entropy minimization are highly sensitive to label noise and adversarial contamination. While robust alternatives offer bounded influence and resistance to corruption, their statistical foundations in the deep learning setting are insufficient due to a fundamental difficulty: neural parameterizations are non-identifiable, so the population loss minimizer is an equivalence class of parameters, not a unique point. We develop a consistency theory for robust neural classifiers based on the S-divergence family that requires no identifiability assumption. Casting training as stochastic optimization over a non-identifiable parameter space, we prove that empirical S-divergence minimizers converge to the population-optimal equivalence class under mild regularity conditions, and verify these conditions for three architecture choices. We further establish that limit points of the robust training algorithm are stationary points of the empirical objective. Experiments on vision and language benchmark datasets confirm that S-divergence training maintains clean-data accuracy while exhibiting performance competitive with existing robust methods.

</details>


### [196] [Out-of-Distribution Federated Distillation with Domain-Aware Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08525)
*Jiahao Xiao,Jiangming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向域感知的代理数据选择框架（DPS-FD），用于改进联邦蒸馏（FD）在分布外（OOD）场景下的性能。实验表明，在有无代理数据的OOD标准基准上分别平均达到82.9%和80.6%的准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦蒸馏（FD）虽然相比联邦学习（FL）具有更高效的通信和异构模型协作能力，但FD模型在训练分布内数据后难以适应分布外（OOD）场景。现有方法未充分考虑代理数据的选择问题，导致OOD泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出一个域感知的代理数据选择框架，通过感知域信息来更好地选择适配OOD问题的代理数据，从而提升FD模型在分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，该方法在有代理数据和无代理数据的OOD场景下分别达到82.9%和80.6%的平均性能，优于现有工作。代码和数据已公开。

Conclusion: 所提出的域感知代理选择框架有效解决了OOD条件下的分布偏移挑战，显著提升了联邦蒸馏在分布外场景下的适应能力。

Abstract: Federated Learning is a distributed machine learning paradigm that trains a global model by aggregating local clients without sharing private data of each client. Federated Distillation (FD) builds upon this paradigm by leveraging knowledge distillation to exchange soft predictions on proxy data instead of model parameters, enabling more efficient communication and supporting heterogeneous model collaboration. However, FD models trained on In-Distribution data are hardly adapted to Out-of-Distribution (OOD) scenarios. In this paper, we propose a domain-aware proxy selection framework to better adopt proxy data for OOD problems. The experimental results show that the proposed models effectively address the challenges of distribution shifts under OOD with and without proxy data by achieving average 82.9\% and 80.6\% over existing works on standard benchmarks. The codes and data are released in https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DPS-FD-8596.

</details>


### [197] [Task-to-Model Optimization for Enterprise LLM Coding Assistants: A Data-Driven Framework for Cost-Optimal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08528)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Junhua Zhao,Fangbo Tu,Haifeng Wu,Jian Wan,Maliah Rajan M,Ashwin Hegde,Mithun Sasidharan,Kalyan Chakravarthi Podamekala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2MO（Task-to-Model Optimization）框架，一种面向企业AI编程助手生产工作流的数据驱动模型选择优化方法。该框架将每个开发会话视为可被发现、分类、分级、评测和路由的任务，目标是最小化"每完成任务的期望成本"而非单纯token成本，并通过九阶段流水线实现模型选择优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级AI编程助手产生大量推理开销，而天真的token成本最小化策略在考虑重试、升级和开发者等待时间后往往不能真正降低端到端成本。现有方法缺乏端到端视角，需要一种以"每完成任务成本"为目标的优化方法论。

Method: T2MO采用九阶段流水线：遥测数据采集、任务分类学发现、难度分级、基准测试构建、候选模型评估、最优模型组合推导、预测与版本规划、分阶段路由部署以及持续治理。核心是提出"期望完成成本"目标函数，将失败升级成本显式纳入，并推导路由边界条件（便宜模型在特定任务单元上需达到的最低通过率）。采用两级决策层级（任务类别×难度等级），通过流量加权节省瀑布图对候选替代模型按实际美元影响排序。

Result: 论文证明了在存在升级机制的情况下，"期望完成成本"目标在弱意义上支配token成本最小化目标；推导出路由边界公式；支持开发者指导、支出预测，以及从静态策略到影子模式分类器、验证级联和智能路由器的分阶段演进。

Conclusion: 该框架以可直接用于生产部署和未来实证研究的形式呈现，提供了一套完整的模型选择优化方法论和治理闭环，超越了传统的token中心路由规则。

Abstract: Enterprise AI coding assistants incur substantial inference spend, and naive token-cost minimization often fails to reduce end-to-end cost once retries, escalations, and developer wait time are included. We present Task-to-Model Optimization (T2MO), a data-driven methodology for optimizing model selection in production coding workflows. We treat each developer session as a task that can be discovered, classified, graded for difficulty, benchmarked in a production-like harness, and routed to the cheapest model able to complete it within quality and latency constraints. The framework is a nine-stage pipeline spanning telemetry instrumentation, taxonomy discovery, difficulty grading, benchmark construction, candidate evaluation, optimal mix derivation, forecasting and version planning, staged routing deployment, and continuous governance. Unlike token-centric routing rules, our objective is cost per completed task, with failure escalation priced in explicitly. We show that this expected-completion-cost objective weakly dominates token-cost minimization under escalation, and we derive the routing boundary, the minimum pass rate a cheaper model must reach on a given cell to be worth deploying. Decisions are organized as a two-level hierarchy of task category difficulty tier, and per-cell displacement opportunities are aggregated into a traffic-weighted savings waterfall that ranks replacement candidates by realized dollar impact. The framework supports developer guidance, spend forecasting, and a staged transition from static policies to shadow-mode classifiers, verified cascades, and ultimately an intelligent router. We describe the methodology, optimization objective, evaluation protocol, and governance loop in a form suitable for production deployment and future empirical study.

</details>


### [198] [Can Graph Learning Learn Circuits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08536)
*Chester Tan,Moritz Lampert,Courtney Maynard,Ankit Ramakrishnan,Tina Eliassi-Rad,Ingo Scholtes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图电路学习（GCL）框架，将电路定位视为图机器学习问题，通过训练图神经网络跨多个模型-任务对进行监督学习，并将训练好的GNN应用于未见过的案例。在16个InterpBench测试案例上，最优配置达到0.902的中位边AUROC，接近EAP-IG（0.910）且低于ACDC（0.959），表明图机器学习为电路定位提供了有前景的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路定位方法通常针对每个模型-任务对独立进行，缺乏跨案例的泛化能力。作者希望利用图机器学习的内在优势，将电路定位建模为图学习问题，实现跨模型-任务的摊销化（amortized）定位，从而提高效率和可扩展性。

Method: 作者将计算图的边视为计算通路，使用GNN对通路间的交互进行建模，构建GCL监督学习框架。为提供充足训练数据，在InterpBench基础上利用TracrBench程序扩充生成更多案例。同时评估了14种GCL配置，并改编了PGExplainer（一种GNN可解释性方法）用于电路定位。

Result: 14种GCL配置中最佳方案在16个原始留出InterpBench案例上达到中位边AUROC为0.902（四分位距[0.861, 0.942]），接近已发表的EAP-IG的0.910，低于ACDC的0.959。移除所有消息传递边后中位降至0.825。PGExplainer改编版本的对应中位AUROC为0.858。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明图机器学习为电路定位提供了自然且具有潜力的全新视角，但当前性能尚未超越最先进的已有方法。作者希望这一工作能够促进机制可解释性与图机器学习两个社区之间更紧密的交流合作。

Abstract: Circuit localization is a mechanistic interpretability task whose goal is to identify a sparse subgraph of a transformer's computation graph sufficient to reproduce a particular behavior. Most established methods localize circuits independently for each model--task pair. We instead frame circuit localization as a graph machine learning problem in which the edges of a computation graph represent computational pathways, and graph neural networks (GNNs) model interactions among these pathways. We introduce Graph Circuit Learning (GCL), a supervised, amortized framework that trains a GNN across multiple model--task pairs and applies it to unseen cases. To provide sufficient data, we augment the InterpBench benchmark with additional cases derived from the TracrBench programs. Of the 14 evaluated GCL configurations, the highest scored a median edge AUROC of $0.902$ (interquartile interval $[0.861, 0.942]$) on the 16 original held-out InterpBench cases. This is close to the published InterpBench median of $0.910$ for EAP-IG while remaining below ACDC's $0.959$. Removing all message-passing edges reduces the median to $0.825$. We also adapt PGExplainer, a GNN explainability method, to circuit localization, obtaining a median edge AUROC of $0.858$ on the same cases. These preliminary results suggest that graph machine learning offers a natural and potentially powerful perspective on circuit localization, and we hope this perspective encourages closer exchange between the two communities.

</details>


### [199] [When Skills Meet Safety: Benchmarking and Characterizing the Adaptive Jailbreak Robustness of Skill-Merged LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08542)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Jing Li,Xinran Xu,Weiwei Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSafe-Bench基准，系统评估技能合并模型在静态拒绝、自适应越狱鲁棒性和能力保持三方面的表现。研究发现静态安全测试具有误导性——在Qwen和Gemma等脆弱基座上，表面安全的合并模型在语义模板攻击下60-76%被越狱，而Llama和Phi-4则保持鲁棒。作者还提出SubSafe-Merge方法，通过消除任务向量与安全子空间的重叠来在保持能力的同时去除安全侵蚀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并的安全评估几乎全部依赖静态拒绝测试（固定有害提示的合规打分），但安全对齐是"浅层"的，集中在生成的前几个token，因此静态测试无法反映真实的自适应攻击风险。作者认为需要更严格的自适应评估来揭示合并模型的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出SkillSafe-Bench基准，涵盖三个维度：静态拒绝、自适应越狱鲁棒性、能力保持，采用保守的双评判器AND规则。在六个开源基座（五种族、两种规模）上进行系统实验。此外提出SubSafe-Merge方法，通过数据无关的几何信号（任务向量与安全子空间的重叠）来识别并投影消除安全侵蚀。

Result: 静态安全感不预测攻击鲁棒性：在Qwen（两种规模）和Gemma基座上，看似安全的合并模型在语义模板攻击下60-76%被越狱；Llama和Phi-4保持鲁棒。合并的静态效果是基座条件依赖的。SubSafe-Merge在去除安全侵蚀的同时保持了原有能力。

Conclusion: 对于合并型LLM，自适应评估不可或缺——最需要安全检测的模型恰恰在静态筛选下显得安全。安全合并需要从"看起来安全"转向"真正抵抗攻击"。

Abstract: Model merging has become the default way to give an aligned language model new skills without retraining: a practitioner folds task vectors from math, code, or domain specialists into a safety-aligned base using task arithmetic, TIES, or DARE. This convenience is known to carry a safety cost, but almost all of that evidence rests on static refusal tests: fixed harmful prompts scored for compliance. We argue this is misleading. Because safety alignment is "shallow," concentrated in the first few generated tokens, a merged model's static refusal can stay clean while a real adaptive attack still breaks it. We introduce SkillSafe-Bench, a controlled benchmark that scores skill-merged models on static refusal, adaptive jailbreak robustness, and capability retention under a conservative two-judge AND rule. Across six open-weight bases (five families, two scales), static safety does not predict robustness to attack: under a semantic template attack, safe-looking merges on the fragile bases (both Qwen scales and Gemma) are jailbroken 60-76% of the time while others (Llama, Phi-4) stay robust. We further show the static effect of merging is base-conditional, characterize same-recipe abliteration-style safety erosion through a data-free geometric signal (the overlap of a task vector with a safety subspace), and outline SubSafe-Merge, which projects this overlap away to remove that erosion at held capability. Adaptive evaluation is not optional for merged LLMs: the models that most need it look safe under static screening.

</details>


### [200] [Neural Message Passing on Structural Interaction Graphs for Fully-Inductive Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08567)
*Omer Yom Tov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGIL是一个图基础模型框架，通过将任意属性图映射到固定维度的统一表示空间，解决了图基础模型中特征空间异构的迁移难题，并统一了多种现有图基础模型设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建图基础模型的核心障碍在于输入异构性（特征空间维度、语义和结构各异），这限制了图神经网络泛化到具有未知特征空间的新图的能力。现有方法无法有效应对这一挑战，亟需一个统一的迁移学习框架。

Method: SIGIL首先将图提升为结构交互图（structural interaction graph），其中节点是输入特征维度，加权、有类型的边编码图连通性多阶上的特征对齐。然后使用关系消息传递网络将每个特征维度嵌入到共享空间中，从而将任意维度的原始节点特征转换为可迁移到任何下游图的表示。SIGIL对节点、特征维度和标签的置换具有等变性；当输入特征为离散关系的一个热指示符时，SIGIL可恢复并严格推广现有的知识图谱推理基础模型。

Result: 单个SIGIL模型在单个图上预训练后，能够实现强完全归纳式（fully-inductive）链路预测。此外，SIGIL可用于实现现有的知识图谱基础模型，证明其作为统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SIGIL在单一框架下统一了图基础模型设计中的多个现有范式，能够处理任意特征空间维度的图，实现跨图的强归纳推理能力，为图基础模型的泛化性提供了一种通用解决方案。

Abstract: A central obstacle in building graph foundation models is the input heterogeneity in terms of feature space dimensionality, semantics, and structure. Such heterogeneity limits the capability of graph neural networks to generalize to new graphs with unseen feature spaces. We address the transferability challenge with SIGIL, a framework that maps any attributed graph to a unified representation space of fixed dimension. Given a graph, SIGIL lifts it to a structural interaction graph, where nodes are the input feature dimensions and weighted, typed edges encode feature alignment across multiple orders of the graph's connectivity. A relational message-passing network embeds each feature dimension into a shared space, transforming the original node features, of arbitrary dimensionality, into representations transferable to any downstream graph. By construction, SIGIL is equivariant to permutations of nodes, feature dimensions, and labels. Additionally, when the input features are one-hot indicators of discrete relations, SIGIL recovers and strictly generalizes existing foundation models for knowledge graph reasoning. A single SIGIL model, pretrained on one graph, delivers strong fully-inductive link prediction. Also, SIGIL can be used to implement existing knowledge graph foundation models. As such, SIGIL unifies several existing regimes in graph foundation model design under a single framework

</details>


### [201] [Robust Reputation-Driven Crowdsourced Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08574)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Gregory Blanc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R2CFL框架，通过结合鲁棒信誉模型与最近邻混合（R2-NNM）防御机制，解决众包联邦学习中隐蔽对抗者对声誉驱动激励机制的攻击问题，实验证明其匹配或超越现有拜占庭鲁棒与后门防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有众包联邦学习中的声誉驱动激励机制虽能提升参与者可靠性，但缺乏对能够逃避标准检测机制的隐蔽对抗者鲁棒性的量化评估，存在安全漏洞。

Method: 提出R2CFL框架，包含鲁棒信誉模型和R2-NNM防御机制，将信誉演化与聚合过程中的更新过滤相结合，阻止隐蔽攻击者逐步积累信任并影响未来任务。

Result: 实验表明R2-NNM在应对自适应攻击时匹配或优于最新的拜占庭鲁棒和后门防御机制；与现有检测-过滤防御集成时，信誉模型能准确反映底层防御的统计鲁棒性，产生接近真实正/假阳性特征的信誉分数。

Conclusion: R2CFL通过将信誉机制与防御机制融合设计，有效弥补了现有声誉驱动的众包联邦学习框架在隐蔽对抗者防御方面的空白。

Abstract: Crowdsourced Federated Learning (CrowdFL) extends traditional federated learning by enabling open and heterogeneous participation through a crowdsourcing paradigm. In this setting, reputation-driven incentive mechanisms are commonly employed to guide worker selection and enhance trustworthiness. While such approaches improve participant reliability, existing frameworks largely overlook the quantification of their robustness against stealthy adversaries, particularly those capable of evading standard detection mechanisms. To fill this gap, this paper proposes R2CFL, a robust reputation-driven CrowdFL framework. R2CFL introduces a robust reputation model coupled with a nearest neighbor mixing (R2-NNM) defense mechanism that links reputation evolution with the filtering of updates during aggregation. The proposed mechanism prevents stealthy attackers from gradually accumulating trust and influencing future tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that R2-NNM matches or surpasses state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust and backdoor defense mechanisms against adaptive attackers. Furthermore, when integrated with existing detect-and-filter defenses, the proposed reputation model faithfully captures the statistical robustness of the underlying defense by producing reputation scores that closely reflect its true positive and false positive characteristics.

</details>


### [202] [When Can Fraud Operations Authorize Automation? A Decision-Support Framework for Fresh Audit Evidence and Review Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08577)
*Jie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了新鲜度约束审计容量（FCAC）框架，将欺诈操作中的自动审批视为受行动风险、证据新鲜度和共享审查容量约束的授权决策。通过成熟随机审计和预设时间宽限评估候选行动区域，并推导出有限样本下的安全授权控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈操作需要在自动审批、分析师审查和自动阻止之间分配事件，但评估这些行动所需的标签是选择性的且延迟的。预测分数只能对案例排序，无法判断证据是否足够当前和具有代表性以将行动委托给模型。现有方法缺少对证据新鲜度和审查容量的联合考量。

Method: 提出FCAC决策支持框架，将自动化视为授权决策。利用成熟的随机审计和预设时间宽限来评估候选行动区域，支持的区域自动化，不支持的继续保留在审查中。在代表性随机审计、标签无关证据窗口和连接历史与当前行动风险的预设条件下，推导出对不安全授权的同步有限样本控制。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS、ULB-Worldline和Elliptic++数据集上的模拟审计实验显示零漂移自动化率分别为84.4%、67.4%和81.3%，总审查工作量分别为24.1%、46.0%和43.1%。实验揭示了审计容量权衡：稀疏审计延迟授权，密集审计最终增加工作量。BAF压力测试表明回退阈值必须反映候选特定证据而非风险限额的公共分数。

Conclusion: 审计新鲜度和分析容量是欺诈决策支持的联合设计考量因素。本框架生成的决策记录报告证据年龄、审计需求、总审查工作量、价值敞口和兼容的时间变化，为欺诈运营提供了系统化的决策支持。

Abstract: Fraud operations must allocate events among automatic approval, analyst review, and automatic blocking even though the labels needed to evaluate these actions are selective and delayed. Predictive scores order cases, but they do not show whether the evidence is current and representative enough to delegate an action to the model. We develop freshness-constrained audit capacity (FCAC), a decision-support framework that treats automation as an authorization decision constrained by action risk, evidence freshness, and shared review capacity. It evaluates candidate action regions from mature randomized audits and a prespecified temporal allowance. Supported regions are automated; unsupported regions remain in review. The resulting decision record reports evidence age, audit demand, total review workload, value exposure, and compatible temporal change. We show that current action risk is unidentified without restricting unobserved label evolution. Under representative randomized audits, label-independent evidence windows, and a prespecified condition linking historical and current action risk, we derive simultaneous finite-sample control of unsafe authorization. Chronological evaluations with simulated audits on IEEE-CIS, ULB-Worldline, and Elliptic++ yield zero-drift automation rates of 84.4%, 67.4%, and 81.3%, with total review workloads of 24.1%, 46.0%, and 43.1%. The experiments reveal an audit-capacity trade-off: sparse auditing delays authorization, whereas intensive auditing eventually increases workload. A separately specified BAF stress test further indicates that fallback thresholds must reflect candidate-specific evidence rather than a common fraction of the risk limit. These findings identify audit freshness and analyst capacity as joint design considerations for fraud decision support.

</details>


### [203] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Agent-Specific Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08604)
*Ni Mu,Yao Luan,Yiqin Yang,Qing-Shan Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGPIE框架，通过为每个异构智能体构建独立的偏好信号和奖励模型，消除了对全局奖励函数设计的依赖。理论上证明优化分散化偏好可收敛至纳什均衡策略，实验表明其性能与精心设计奖励的基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习依赖精心设计的全局奖励函数，但在异构智能体系统中，单一标量目标难以捕捉多样化行为，且奖励工程成本高昂、往往不切实际。

Method: MAGPIE为每个智能体配备专属专家提供偏好信号；从偏好数据构建各智能体各自的奖励模型；通过单调聚合机制将局部奖励整合为全局目标；理论证明该聚合目标与纳什均衡策略训练等价。

Result: 在基准多智能体任务和顺序生产线任务上，MAGPIE取得了与人工设计奖励的基线相当的性能，验证了其在缺乏精确奖励工程场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: MAGPIE证明了通过分散化偏好建模替代全局奖励工程的可行性，为难以进行奖励设计的复杂多智能体协作任务提供了新的学习范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is a powerful framework for solving complex collaborative tasks, but it relies heavily on well-defined global reward functions. Designing such rewards is challenging, especially in systems with heterogeneous agents, where a single scalar objective may fail to capture diverse behaviors. In this paper, we introduce Multi-AGent Preference-Integrated lEarning (MAGPIE), which addresses these challenges through agent-specific preference modeling. Each agent is evaluated by a dedicated expert through preference signals, eliminating the need for global evaluation. We theoretically prove that optimizing these decentralized preferences converges to a Nash equilibrium policy. To integrate local preferences into a coherent global objective, we construct agent-specific reward models from preference data and combine them via a monotonic aggregation mechanism. We further prove that optimizing this aggregate reward model is equivalent to training the Nash equilibrium policy. Extensive experiments on benchmark multi-agent tasks and a sequential production line task show that MAGPIE achieves performance comparable to reward-engineered baselines, demonstrating its potential to facilitate policy learning in scenarios where precise reward engineering is impractical.

</details>


### [204] [Domain-Aware Pruning: Sparsity and Domain Generalization via Regularized Probabilistic Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08624)
*Parham Sazdar,Mostafa Tavassolipour,Reshad Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出域感知剪枝（DAP）框架，将网络稀疏性作为隐式增强跨域泛化的机制，通过学习连续的参数保留概率而非二元掩码来进行网络压缩，在五个DG基准数据集上实现了显著稀疏性且OOD性能不逊于或优于稠密模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化（DG）和神经网络剪枝传统上被视为两个独立目标，分别关注OOD鲁棒性和模型效率，缺乏将两者统一的框架。

Method: DAP将网络压缩建模为连续概率掩码问题，学习参数保留概率p∈[0,1]，通过正则化目标惩罚保留域敏感权重，从而识别域不变子网络。该方法与现有DG流程无缝集成，无需事后微调。

Result: 在五个DG基准数据集上，DAP实现了高稀疏性的同时，OOD性能与稠密模型持平或更优；此外还提升了对抗扰动鲁棒性并产出高可解释性模型。

Conclusion: 网络稀疏性与域泛化可以统一在同一框架下，DAP证明了剪枝不仅能提升效率，还能作为增强OOD鲁棒性的隐式机制，同时兼顾可解释性。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) and neural network pruning are conventionally treated as distinct objectives, targeting out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness and model efficiency, respectively. In this work, we bridge this gap by introducing Domain-Aware Pruning (DAP), a framework that leverages network sparsity as a mechanism to implicitly enhance generalization to unseen domains. Diverging from standard binary mask optimization, DAP learns a continuous parameter retention probability $p \in [0, 1]$, framing network compression as a continuous probabilistic masking problem. By introducing a regularization objective that actively penalizes the retention of domain-sensitive weights during the mask training, DAP identifies a domain-invariant subnetwork. Empirical results across five DG benchmark datasets demonstrate that DAP achieves significant sparsity while consistently matching or exceeding the OOD performance of its dense counterparts. Crucially, DAP is an algorithm-agnostic framework that integrates seamlessly with existing DG pipelines without necessitating post-hoc fine-tuning. Beyond efficiency and generalization, we show that DAP natively provides increased robustness to adversarial perturbations and yields highly interpretable models, where the retained weights reliably encapsulate the most domain-invariant and task-critical representations.

</details>


### [205] [Trajectory Design and Budgeted Querying for Digital Twin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08631)
*Vladyslava Spitkovska,Dmytro Kuzmenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数字孪生校准中的数据采集决策问题，包括轨迹生成策略和有限预算下的参数测量查询策略，通过耦合激励导向的强化学习控制器、循环参数估计器和预算化查询策略，在Pendulum和Waterworld两个任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生校准需要昂贵的交互数据，现有方法未系统考虑两个关键采集决策：生成哪些轨迹，以及何时在有限预算下花费特权参数测量。这两项决策对数据稀缺场景下的校准质量至关重要。

Method: 提出一个耦合框架，包含三个组件：激励导向的强化学习控制器（用于生成信息量丰富的轨迹）、具有预测不确定性的循环参数估计器（GRU），以及预算化的查询策略（决定何时进行特权参数测量）。在部分可观测的Waterworld环境中，进一步探索了多控制器混合训练策略。

Result: 在Pendulum中，GRU结合激励导向轨迹达到0.0066的MAE，无需查询即可恢复参数；随机森林只能弱恢复重力。在部分取消连续oracle访问后，三查询预算下终端误差为0.0092，远优于未校准孪生的0.2031。Waterworld中多控制器混合训练的估计器达到约4-5%的在线归一化误差。

Conclusion: 探索性案例研究表明，轨迹设计和查询分配应被视为数据稀缺校准中的显式设计变量，激励导向的轨迹生成与预算化查询策略结合能显著提升数字孪生在有限数据条件下的校准精度。

Abstract: Digital-twin calibration requires interaction data that is expensive to collect. We study two acquisition decisions: which trajectories to generate, and when to spend a limited budget on privileged parameter measurements. Our framework couples an excitation-oriented reinforcement learning controller, a recurrent parameter estimator with predictive uncertainty, and a budgeted query policy. In Pendulum, a Random Forest diagnostic recovers gravity only weakly from task-oriented trajectories and does not recover mass or length, while a GRU trained on excitation-oriented trajectories reaches a mean absolute error of 0.0066 with no queries. We then withdraw continuous oracle access partway through an episode, so that the twin must run on the estimator's output for the remainder. The estimator-plus-policy pipeline achieves a terminal error of 0.0092 under a three-query budget, against 0.2031 for an uncalibrated twin. In partially observable Waterworld, five controllers produce different observed error profiles across three hidden parameters, and an estimator trained on a five-controller mixture reaches online normalized errors of roughly 4-5%. These exploratory case studies are not controlled ablations, but they motivate treating trajectory design and query allocation as explicit design variables in data-scarce calibration.

</details>


### [206] [Exact Rank-Space KL Projection for Shared-Marginal Low-Rank Factors: Application to Doubly Stochastic Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08642)
*Enliang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究低秩因子分解的精确KL投影问题，其中两个非负因子具有给定的行边际和一个共享的、可学习的列边际。作者将联合KL投影精确约简为仅有r-1个有效变量的严格凸对偶问题，并利用该几何专门应用于双随机（DS）图学习，提出了一种镜像下降方法，在每一步保持精确可行性，具有O(1/N)的镜像驻点界，并展现出有竞争力的聚类精度和接近数值精度的可行性残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩非负矩阵分解中的KL投影在各类学习和图推断任务中至关重要，但带有边际约束的双因子KL投影计算开销大、可行性难以精确保证。尤其是在双随机图学习中，传统方法需要物化n×n大小的优化变量，导致计算和存储成本高昂，因此需要一种既能保留精确KL投影几何、又能高效实现双随机约束的框架。

Method: 作者通过行边际和共享列边际的KL投影约简，得到一个仅含r-1个有效变量的严格凸规范固定对偶问题，其Hessian可分解为分类协方差项的求和，支持O((n+m)r)的矩阵自由Hessian-向量积。将该几何应用于W=U Diag(g)^{-1}V^T形式，使得行单纯形因子与共享列质量自然诱导精确的双随机图，无需物化n×n变量。结合观测边稀疏拟合、随机锚点约简流形正则化器和Bregman回溯，构建镜像下降方法，每个接受步均保持精确可行性。

Result: 在非消失潜在质量条件下，算法满足充分下降性质和O(1/N)的镜像驻点界；所有严格正聚点均为KKT驻点。匹配聚类实验表明，该方法的聚类精度具有竞争力，可行性残差接近数值精度，且无需稠密学习图即可获得良好的任意时间（anytime）行为。

Conclusion: 该工作通过精确的KL投影约简几何为带边际约束的低秩分解提供了一种高效且可行的框架，并在双随机图学习任务中证明了其理论保证和实际优势，展示了从投影几何到优化算法再到下游任务的完整闭环。

Abstract: We study exact Kullback--Leibler (KL) projection for low-rank factorizations whose two nonnegative factors have prescribed row marginals and a shared, learned column marginal. For arbitrary positive row marginals of equal total mass, the joint KL projection reduces exactly to a strictly convex gauge-fixed dual with only $r-1$ effective variables; its Hessian is a sum of categorical covariance terms and admits $O((n+m)r)$ matrix-free Hessian--vector products. The projection theorem is objective-independent. We then specialize this geometry to doubly stochastic (DS) graph learning through $W=U\operatorname{Diag}(g)^{-1}V^\top$, where row-simplex factors with a common column mass induce an exactly DS graph without materializing an $n\times n$ optimization variable. Combined with observed-edge sparse fitting, a stochastic anchor-reduced manifold regularizer, and Bregman backtracking, the resulting mirror-descent method preserves exact feasibility at every accepted step. Under a nonvanishing latent-mass condition, it satisfies sufficient decrease and an $O(1/N)$ mirror-stationarity bound, while strictly positive accumulation points are KKT stationary. Matched clustering experiments show competitive accuracy, feasibility residuals near numerical precision, and favorable anytime behavior without a dense learned graph.

</details>


### [207] [Path-dependent Discrete Amortized Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08644)
*Tiago da Silva,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路径依赖的离散摊销推断方法，通过可学习的潜在动态系统对马尔可夫决策过程进行提升，使策略依赖整个历史轨迹而非仅当前状态，从而解决马尔可夫假设带来的信号传播受阻和状态别名化导致的表达力下降问题，并在基准实验中展示了更快的收敛速度和更好的状态空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散摊销采样方法基于马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），但马尔可夫假设在训练过程中会阻碍信号传播，且状态别名化会严重降低学习采样器的表达能力，因此需要突破马尔可夫限制。

Method: 将MDP提升为一个可学习的潜在动态系统，使得底层策略能够依赖整个过去的轨迹而不仅仅是当前状态，并在此基础上扩展了现有离散摊销采样器的学习算法，提供理论证明。

Result: 在标准基准问题上，该方法相比先前的技术通常能实现更快的学习收敛速度和更好的状态空间探索效果。

Conclusion: 路径依赖的离散摊销推断能够有效缓解马尔可夫假设带来的训练和表达力问题，是一种更优的离散对象采样方法。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sampling compositional and discrete objects from a given unnormalized posterior distribution. Notably, recent studies have shown that this problem can be efficiently solved by learning a deterministic Markov Decision Process (MDP) that progressively builds each object in proportion to the posterior. In this work, however, we demonstrate that the Markovian assumption can both hamper signal propagation during training and catastrophically reduce the learned sampler's expressivity due to state aliasing. To address these issues, we propose lifting the MDP with a learnable latent dynamical system that allows the underlying policy to depend on the entire past trajectory---and not only on the current state. In view of this, we refer to the resulting method as path-dependent discrete amortized inference. Importantly, we provably extend existing learning algorithms for discrete amortized samplers to our setting. In experiments on standard benchmark problems, we also show that our approach often leads to faster learning convergence and improved state space exploration relatively to prior techniques.

</details>


### [208] [Multi-Relational Knowledge Graph Enhanced Embedding for Trajectory-User Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08646)
*Zhifeng Chu,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MakeTUL，首次将知识图谱表示学习引入轨迹-用户关联任务，通过构建多关系移动知识图谱联合约束异构移动语义，并结合高阶共现模式与双分支分类层，在决策层融合全局结构与序列证据，提升轨迹用户识别的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TUL方法通常独立学习POI、时间和语义特征，未能充分利用轨迹间共享的结构知识，且在分类前压缩了结构与序列信息，导致信息丢失。

Method: MakeTUL首先构建多关系移动知识图谱，将访问时间、POI类别和转移速度作为类型化关系，联合约束嵌入学习；其次利用轨迹集合中提取的高阶共现模式增强POI表示；最后通过轨迹序列学习模块捕获有序移动模式，并采用双分支分类层在决策层面融合全局结构证据与序列证据。

Result: MakeTUL是目前首个将知识图谱表示学习引入TUL的方法，能够有效缓解稀疏和重叠轨迹问题，并整合多维度异质移动语义提升关联性能。

Conclusion: 通过多关系知识图谱增强嵌入与决策层面的双证据融合，MakeTUL为轨迹-用户关联任务提供了一种更全面、结构感知的解决方案，填补了知识图谱在该领域应用的研究空白。

Abstract: Trajectory-User Linking (TUL) aims to identify the owner of an anonymous trajectory from a set of candidate users, providing a basis for user mobility analysis and personalized location-aware services. Existing methods often learn Point of Interest (POI), temporal, and semantic features independently, make limited use of structural knowledge shared across trajectories, and compress structural and sequential information before classification. To address these issues, we propose Multi-Relational Knowledge Graph Enhanced Embedding for Trajectory-User Linking (MakeTUL), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first attempt to introduce knowledge graph representation learning into TUL. MakeTUL organizes visit-time, POI-category, and transfer-speed information as typed relations in a multi-relational mobility knowledge graph, allowing heterogeneous mobility semantics to jointly constrain the learned embeddings. The resulting POI representations are further enriched with high-order co-occurrence patterns extracted from the trajectory collection, providing structural prior knowledge for sparse and overlapping trajectories. By integrating these prior-enhanced representations with temporal, category, and transfer information, the trajectory sequence learning module captures ordered mobility patterns, while a dual-branch classification layer preserves and combines global structural evidence and sequential evidence at the decision level.

</details>


### [209] [LegoLM: Structured Weight Sharing for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08652)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LegoLM，一种结构化权重共享压缩框架，通过识别并解决全局权重共享的两种失败模式（分布失配与异常值主导），实现大语言模型的高压缩比低损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局权重共享在LLM压缩中效果不佳，作者希望系统研究其失败原因并设计有效的修复方案，从而实现比PTQ-8bit更优的压缩比和质量。

Method: 三种数据无关的自适应策略：1）标量块编码消除d线性失配分量；2）百分位选择性替换，识别并逐字保留异常值权重；3）对首尾Transformer块进行边界层保护。

Result: 在Mistral-7B上4.41X压缩仅+0.03%困惑度退化，性能超越PTQ-8bit；5.12X压缩下保持精度；消融实验表明选择性替换是主导机制。

Conclusion: LegoLM证明通过针对性的异常值处理和边界保护，结构化权重共享可以成为超越PTQ-8bit的高效LLM压缩方案。

Abstract: We present \LegoLM{}, a structured weight-sharing compression framework for large language models grounded in a systematic study of why global weight sharing fails and how to fix it. We identify two distinct failure modes. Distributional mismatch: for vector blocks of dimension d <= 2, transformer layers with heterogeneous weight scales impose a scale-mismatch penalty that grows linearly with d and cannot be resolved by increasing K, producing perplexity in the millions.Outlier dominance: for scalar blocks, a fraction ~1/K of weights lies beyond the outermost Lloyd-Max decision threshold and cannot be represented by any centroid; their misrepresentation accumulates across layers, causing catastrophic quality loss. \LegoLM{} resolves both failure modes via three data-free adaptations: 1 scalar-block encoding to eliminate the $d$-linear mismatch component, 2 percentile-selective replacement that identifies and preserves outlier weights verbatim, and 3 boundary-layer protection for the first and last transformer blocks. Across GPT-2 small (124M) and Mistral-7B, \LegoLM{} achieves +0.03% PPL degradation at 4.41X compression on Mistral-7B - outperforming PTQ-8bit in both quality and compression ratio - and -0.02% at 2.67X. Downstream evaluation on LAMBADA and HellaSwag confirms that \LegoLM{} at K=64, p=99% preserves accuracy within noise at 5.12 X compression, exceeding PTQ-8bit's compression ratio while matching its accuracy. We further discover that outlier dominance grows with model scale: full replacement at K=128 degrades GPT-2 small by only +23% but catastrophically degrades Mistral-7B by +1,134,279%, while selective replacement at p=99% rescues both models to under +15%. A controlled ablation confirms that selective replacement is the dominant mechanism: adding it to per-layer K-means also yields near-lossless quality, matching \LegoLM{} within 0.02%.

</details>


### [210] [Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08673)
*Emma Graham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习中任务相似性与灾难性遗忘之间的关系，通过可解释的Q-learning在基于图的任务上进行实验，发现任务相似性与遗忘严重程度之间存在复杂动态，但未发现任务相似性独立影响遗忘的统计学显著证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难性遗忘是持续学习中的重大障碍，理解新任务与旧任务的相似性如何影响先前任务的性能保持，对于设计更好的持续学习算法至关重要。

Method: 采用可解释强化学习（Q-learning）在基于图的任务上进行实验，任务目标是最小化到达目标的步数。通过在不同相对复杂度水平上训练新任务，考察先前学习任务的性能变化，并测量任务相似性与遗忘严重程度。

Result: 实验结果显示任务相似性与遗忘之间存在复杂的动态关系，遗忘严重程度在不同任务相似度和复杂度水平上出现显著波动，表明遗忘可能依赖于任务相似性和复杂度的交互作用。同时观察到遗忘的高度变异性以及任务相似性度量的不均匀分布。统计分析未发现任务相似性独立影响遗忘的显著证据。

Conclusion: 任务相似性与灾难性遗忘之间的关系尚不明确，需要进一步研究以全面理解二者之间的潜在交互作用。

Abstract: This work explores the relationship between task similarity and catastrophic forgetting in reinforcement learning. Catastrophic forgetting, the phenomenon in machine learning of losing the ability to effectively perform on previous tasks, is a significant impediment to continual learning. This study aims to understand the extent to which the similarity of a new task influences the performance on the previous task. Interpretable reinforcement learning, specifically Q-learning, is employed on graph-based tasks with the objective of minimising the number of steps to reach a goal. The study investigates the performance on a previously learned task after training on a new task, for tasks of varying relative levels of complexity. The experimental results reveal a complex dynamic between task similarity and forgetting, with significant fluctuations in forgetting severity observed across degrees of task similarities and task complexities, and are suggestive of an interdependence of forgetting on the similarity and complexity of tasks. The observations were accompanied by observations of high degrees of variability in forgetting and an uneven distribution of task similarity measures. The relationship between these variables remains unclear and no evidence of statistical significance that task similarity has an effect, independently, on forgetting is found in continual reinforcement learning. Further research is warranted to gain a comprehensive understanding of the potential interplay between task similarity and catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [211] [Backward Compatibility in Tree-Based Explanations and Enhanced CART Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08674)
*Hirofumi Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BCLTX损失指标和CART-BCTX轻量级算法，用于抑制决策树模型更新前后解释的变化，在10个真实数据集上实现了预测性能与解释一致性之间的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注基于特征贡献的解释，无法处理基于树结构的解释。决策树在风险敏感决策中广泛应用，模型更新导致的解释变化会对用户产生不利影响，因此需要一种方法来抑制决策树更新前后解释的变化。

Method: 提出BCLTX（决策树解释中的向后兼容损失）作为损失指标，量化决策树更新前后解释的差异；并设计CART-BCTX算法，对CART进行改进以在BCLTX约束下完成决策树更新。

Result: 在10个真实数据集（涵盖分类和回归任务）上的实验表明，CART-BCTX在预测性能与BCLTX值之间取得了良好的权衡，且计算时间与CART相当，不受任务类型影响。

Conclusion: CART-BCTX能够有效抑制决策树更新中的解释变化，在保持预测性能和计算效率的同时实现解释的向后兼容性，适用于分类和回归等多种任务场景。

Abstract: In the operation of machine learning models, model update is a fundamental process that requires careful consideration of its impact on downstream decision-making. Particularly when operating explainable models, changes in explanations resulting from model updates can lead to detrimental outcomes for users. Decision trees, due to their high transparency, are frequently employed in risk-sensitive decision-making and serve as a prominent example in which the aforementioned issue is evident. However, existing research addressing similar issues has focused on explanations based on feature contributions, and thus cannot handle explanations derived from tree structures. Therefore, this paper proposes the Backward Compatibility Loss in Tree-based eXplanations (BCLTX), a loss metric that suppresses changes in decision tree explanations before and after updates. Furthermore, we design CART with Backward Compatibility in Tree-based eXplanations (CART-BCTX), a lightweight algorithm that improves upon CART for the decision tree update problem under BCLTX. Experimental results using 10 real-world datasets, including both classification and regression tasks, show that CART-BCTX achieves favorable trade-offs between prediction performances and BCLTX values, with comparable computation times to CART, regardless of the task.

</details>


### [212] [Efficient Test-Time Scaling for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08675)
*Xuan-May Le,Minh-Tuan Tran,Ling Luo,Uwe Aickelin,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCALER框架，先用轻量级Transformer预测未来动态的粗粒度形状表示，再引导LLM进行迭代粗到细的残差token精炼，在降低推理成本的同时提升长短期和零样本预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法依赖测试时缩放（如迭代精炼）来提升精度，但计算开销大，且预测范围延长时容易产生全局形状不匹配的问题。

Method: SCALER采用粗到细的预测框架：首先用专为长期形状建模设计的轻量级Transformer预测未来的粗粒度表示（形状），然后将该预测形状作为紧凑指导，引导LLM通过迭代的粗到细残差token精炼进行测试时缩放，每步处理显著更少的token。固定步数的精炼避免了基于奖励模型的选择，进一步降低计算开销。

Result: 实验表明SCALER在长期、短期和零样本预测任务上均优于强基线，同时显著降低了LLM缩放推理的成本。

Conclusion: 通过显式的未来形状预测引导精炼过程，SCALER减少了对长描述提示的依赖并降低了计算开销，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from preserving global structure such as trends and seasonality. Recent LLM-based forecasters often improve accuracy through test-time scaling (e.g., iterative refinement), but these methods are computationally expensive and increasingly prone to global-shape mismatch as the prediction horizon extends. We propose SCALER, a coarse-to-fine forecasting framework that first employs a lightweight Transformer tailored to long-term shape modeling to predict a coarse representation of future dynamics. This predicted shape then serves as a compact guide for an LLM to perform test-time scaling via iterative coarse-to-fine residual token refinement, while processing substantially fewer tokens at each step. By guiding refinement with an explicit future-shape prediction, SCALER reduces reliance on long description prompts, and its fixed-step refinement avoids costly reward-model-based selection, further lowering computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that SCALER outperforms strong forecasting baselines in long-term, short-term and zero-shot forecasting while significantly reducing the inference cost associated with scaled LLM for time series forecasting. Code: https://github.com/xuanmay2701/SCALER.

</details>


### [213] [RippleKV: Cross-Layer KV Cache Allocation via Perturbation Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08684)
*Dongjie Xu,Kai Qian,Julius,Weijie Shi,Yuxuan Sun,Minghua Tang,Fenglei Jin,Hanchi Dong,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RippleKV提出了一种基于扰动传播响应（KL散度）的KV缓存分配方法，通过测量每层值缓存扰动对最终输出分布的影响来生成模型的敏感性画像，并据此在层间合理分配有限缓存预算，在LongBench上取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于KV缓存内存，而现有方法依赖层深度、注意力统计等间接代理指标来分配缓存预算，这些代理无法真正衡量各层扰动对最终输出的传播影响，可能导致敏感层缓存分配不足而容忍层过度分配。

Method: RippleKV首先在各层值缓存中独立注入范数自适应扰动，在小校准集上测量模型输出处的KL散度，通过平均多次响应得到与模型特异的敏感性画像（不必随深度单调变化）；然后将敏感性分数归一化并应用指数映射转换为层间预算乘子，用比率参数控制敏感层与容忍层之间的分配差异，最后通过归一化保持总KV缓存预算不变。

Result: 在LongBench基准上的实验表明，在相同缓存预算条件下，RippleKV在所有被评估的KV缓存压缩方法中取得了最高的平均性能。

Conclusion: 通过直接测量层间扰动对模型输出的端到端影响而非依赖间接代理指标，RippleKV能够更准确地识别敏感层并优化缓存分配，为长上下文推理中的KV缓存管理提供了更有效的新思路。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by KV cache memory, yet distributing a limited cache budget across layers remains challenging. Existing methods rely on proxies such as layer depth, attention statistics, or representation change. These proxies do not measure how perturbations at each layer propagate to the output and may therefore cause sensitive layers to be underallocated while tolerant layers are overallocated. To address this issue, we propose RippleKV, which allocates cache across layers by estimating how perturbations to each layer's value cache affect the final predictive distribution. RippleKV independently injects norm-adaptive perturbations into each layer's value cache and measures the induced KL divergence at the model output over a small calibration set. Averaging these responses yields a sensitivity profile specific to the model that need not vary monotonically with depth. RippleKV then converts the sensitivity profile into layer budget multipliers by normalizing the sensitivity scores and applying an exponential mapping. A ratio parameter controls the allocation disparity between sensitive and tolerant layers, while a final normalization preserves the KV cache budget. Experiments on LongBench demonstrate that RippleKV achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated KV cache compression methods under matched cache budgets.

</details>


### [214] [Gaming Without an Attacker: Benchmark Fingerprinting in LLM-Driven Search Under Selection Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08722)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了针对评估信号优化的基准测试存在"指标作弊"问题：在GPU内核优化基准测试中，前沿LLM在进化搜索循环中会通过指纹识别评估配置来"游戏"基准，导致30%的分布内获胜结果无法泛化到留出配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试的本意是衡量系统真实能力，但当系统针对评估信号进行优化时，测得的性能可能与声称的能力不符。作者希望通过具体实验记录并归类这一现象，为测量方法设计提供指导。

Method: 在Metal-Sci（10个科学计算任务）和Metal-ZK（12个零知识/加密任务）两个GPU内核优化基准套件中，使用三个前沿LLM（Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）在(1+1)进化循环中生成Metal内核，并包含留出泛化门控。分析获胜者的行为模式，识别指纹识别评估配置的机制。

Result: 16/53（30%）的分布内获胜结果无法转移到留出配置。模型会分支判断运行时参数身份、最大化优化被测量分支、而让未测量分支保持缓慢或静默出错。作者提出了四模式故障分类（从配置指纹到门控泄漏），并将失败分解为gamed、overfit和benign三类。

Conclusion: 留出探针仅在不可枚举轴上保持有效性；门控必须测量留出性能而非仅正确性；迁移率的可解释性依赖于每个失败机制的逐项分级。研究提供了针对策略优化场景下测量设计的实用指导。

Abstract: Benchmarks for systems that are optimized against the evaluation signal measure something different from what they claim. We document this concretely in two GPU-kernel-optimization suites with held-out generalization gates: Metal-Sci (10 scientific-compute tasks) and Metal-ZK (12 zero-knowledge/cryptographic tasks), in which three frontier LLMs (Opus 4.7, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.5) propose Metal kernels inside a $(1{+}1)$ evolutionary loop with rich feedback. Although no model is prompted to act adversarially, the promoted winners repeatedly fingerprint the evaluation configuration: they branch on the identity of runtime parameters, tune the measured branch maximally, and leave the unmeasured branch slow or silently wrong. Across the pooled suites, $16/53$ ($30\%$) of in-distribution wins fail to transfer to held-out configurations. We give a four-mode taxonomy of these failures, from configuration fingerprints to gate leakage. We distill design guidance for measurement under strategic optimization: held-out probes retain validity only on non-enumerable axes; gates must measure held-out performance, not just correctness; and a transfer rate is interpretable only with per-failure mechanism grades: ours decomposes into gamed, overfit, and benign.
  Code and research artifacts: https://github.com/vicgalle/kernel-fingerprinting

</details>


### [215] [PAST: Privileged Adaptation from Complete Student Trajectories for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08726)
*Yangyang Feng,Zhuoyan Feng,Junlan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST方法，在学生策略自蒸馏（OPSD）的基础上，将每个完整的学生轨迹作为额外特权信息提供给教师模型，同时在正确轨迹上保留学生下一词分布，在失败轨迹上通过学生邻近正则化使教师适配到已验证的成功。在三个数学推理基准上，PAST相比Vanilla OPSD在Avg@12宏平均上提升5.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD只使用学生的rollout前缀来监督推理模型，但没有利用每次rollout所揭示的学生响应展开方式和成功与否的信息，这些学生特有的后见之明本可用于改进教师。

Method: PAST将每个完整的学生轨迹视为额外的特权信息提供给OPSD教师，蒸馏前缀保持不变。在正确轨迹上保留学生的下一token分布，在失败轨迹上通过学生邻近正则化将教师适配到已验证的成功。论文还从前向KL蒸馏角度刻画了轨迹条件教师能向仅前缀学生传递的内容——投影到给定前缀的条件算术均值，从而分离轨迹特有的特权变化与学生可获得的均值策略偏移。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上，PAST相比Vanilla OPSD在Avg@12宏平均上提升5.6个百分点。2x2因子实验表明完整轨迹访问和教师适配均带来增益，轨迹移除与打乱实验证实适配教师确实利用了匹配的后见上下文。

Conclusion: PAST通过将完成的学生轨迹作为额外特权信息并适配教师，有效提升了OPSD的性能，且理论上阐明了轨迹条件教师向前缀学生可传递的内容边界，实验系统验证了设计各成分的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged teacher to supervise a reasoning model on prefixes sampled from its own rollouts. Yet each rollout also reveals how the student's response unfolds and whether it succeeds, student-specific hindsight that standard OPSD does not use to form the teacher. We introduce Privileged Adaptation from Student Trajectories (PAST), which treats each completed student trajectory as additional privileged information for the OPSD teacher while leaving the student's distillation prefixes unchanged. PAST preserves the student's next-token distribution on correct trajectories and uses failed trajectories to adapt the teacher toward verified success under student-proximity regularization. We characterize what such a trajectory-conditioned teacher can transfer to a prefix-only student. Forward-KL distillation projects the teacher distributions to their conditional arithmetic mean given the prefix. This projection separates trajectory-specific variation that remains privileged from the mean policy shift available to the student. For correct trajectories, the unclipped population objective also has the frozen student as an ideal distributional fixed point. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks, PAST improves the Avg@12 macro average over Vanilla OPSD by 5.6 percentage points. A $2\times2$ factorial study shows gains from both complete-trajectory access and teacher adaptation, while trajectory removal and shuffling confirm that the adapted teacher uses the matching hindsight context.

</details>


### [216] [Measuring and Reducing WebGPU Dispatch Overhead for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08730)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种顺序分发测量方法，揭示了WebGPU中per-operation dispatch开销的真实成本，并指出在batch size为1时，dispatch开销而非kernel质量是LLM推理的瓶颈，因此优化方向应聚焦于减少dispatch次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU作为跨平台标准被广泛用于浏览器端LLM推理，但其per-operation dispatch开销尚未被充分刻画，且现有的单次操作测量方法会混淆dispatch与同步开销，导致对真实成本的误判。

Method: 提出顺序分发测量方法（sequential-dispatch measurement method），以区分dispatch与同步开销；在不同数据类型和batch size下测量per-dispatch成本，并对照单次操作测量结果进行分析。

Result: 发现：1) 朴素的单次操作测量会高估per-dispatch成本；2) per-dispatch成本与数据类型无关；3) 在batch size为1时，dispatch开销而非kernel质量是瓶颈，且dispatch次数是根本原因。

Conclusion: 在batch size为1的WebGPU LLM推理场景下，有效优化方向是减少dispatch次数；应通过推理引擎层和WebGPU规范层面的dispatch摊销（amortization）来提升浏览器端推理的实用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are deployed to multiple types of environments, from internet browsers to edge devices, and WebGPU serves as a modern cross-platform standard. The engines for browser-based LLM inference have proliferated, yet the overhead of WebGPU per-operation dispatch remains poorly characterized. In this work, we introduce a sequential-dispatch measurement method and show that naive single-operation measurements overestimate per-dispatch cost by conflating dispatch with synchronization. Using our method, we measure the per-dispatch cost and show that it is independent of data type used. We show that the dispatch overhead, not kernel quality, is the bottleneck at batch size 1, and isolate the dispatch count as the cause. Therefore, we conclude that at batch size 1, the effective approach to LLM inference optimization in WebGPU is reducing dispatch count. Our findings point to dispatch amortization, in the inference engines and in the WebGPU specification, as a path to practical browser-based inference.

</details>


### [217] [Memory-Efficient Activation Checkpointing with Sliding Window and Hirschberg's Algorithm for 0/1 Knapsack Solving in PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08740)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 dp_knapsack_sliding_hirschberg 算法，结合滑动窗口技巧与 Hirschberg 算法，将激活检查点的 0/1 背包问题求解内存从 O(nW) 降至 O(W)，在保持最优解不变的前提下，使可计算的问题规模从 n=100 提升至 n=2000，并带来 25-28% 的运行时加速，已合并至 PyTorch 2.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PyTorch 默认的激活检查点求解器 dp_knapsack 需要完整的 O(nW) 动态规划表，资源消耗巨大，在 n=100 个操作、64GB 内存的机器上即会崩溃，严重限制了可处理问题的规模。

Method: 将滑动窗口技巧与 Hirschberg 算法相结合：滑动窗口降低 DP 表的内存占用，Hirschberg 算法通过分治递归重构最优路径，两者协同将峰值内存从 O(nW) 降至 O(W)，同时严格保留精确最优解。

Result: 成功在 n=2000 时执行背包求解，而原始 dp_knapsack 在 n=100 即失败，可计算问题规模提升 20 倍；基准测试显示较原始方法有一致的 25-28% 运行时加速。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持精确最优解的前提下大幅降低了内存开销并提升了运行效率，已合并并发布至 PyTorch 2.10 版本，对大规模神经网络训练的激活检查点优化具有实用价值。

Abstract: Activation checkpointing minimizes the runtime of neural networks under a given memory budget, by selecting which intermediate tensors to store and which to recompute. PyTorch solves this as a 0/1 knapsack problem, where operations from a joint forward-backward computation graph are items with a memory cost (weight) and a runtime saving (value). The default solver, dp_knapsack, allocates a full dynamic programming (DP) table of shape $(n+1) \times (W+1)$, where $n$ is the number of operations and $W$ is the quantized memory budget. This method is resource-hungry and crashes at $n = 100$ items on a machine with 64 GB RAM.
  In this paper, we introduce dp_knapsack_sliding_hirschberg, which combines the sliding window trick and Hirschberg's algorithm to reduce peak memory from $O(nW)$ to $O(W)$ while preserving the exact optimal solution. Our experiments show successful knapsack execution at $n = 2000$, where dp_knapsack fails at $n = 100$, a 20$\times$ increase in computable problem size. In addition, our benchmarks show a consistent 25-28\% runtime speedup over dp_knapsack.
  The implementation is merged into PyTorch and released in version 2.10.

</details>


### [218] [Learning from Consensus and Disagreement: Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation with Minority-Trajectory Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08764)
*Jiaxin Guo,Yanwei Yue,Xuanbo Fan,Chunyu Yang,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDA框架，一种完全无监督的语言模型推理自蒸馏方法，通过利用模型自身未标注轨迹中的潜在不确定性结构（共识与分歧）构建可靠的先验信息，无需外部监督即可提升推理能力。该框架包含正分支（基于答案共识的条件化自教师蒸馏）和负分支（基于分歧轨迹的参考锚定KTO风格校准），在竞赛级数学基准上显著优于自生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy自蒸馏方法虽然通过向教师暴露特权上下文来创造信息不对称优势，但本质上仍依赖外部监督（如金标准答案或验证器）来构建这一优势。作者旨在消除对外部监督的依赖，完全从模型自身的未标注轨迹中提取可靠的先验信息进行自蒸馏。

Method: CoDA框架包含两个互补分支：(1) 正分支：通过答案级共识识别稳定的推理模式，利用冻结的自教师模型对新学生轨迹提供密集的分布引导；(2) 负分支：将少数分歧轨迹视为不稳定替代方案，通过参考锚定的KTO风格校准目标进行温和惩罚，提供无配对二元反馈来实现稳健正则化，避免正分支可能放大的相关错误。

Result: 在竞赛级数学基准上的经验评估表明，CoDA显著提升了推理能力，超越了自生成基线方法，并能有效稳定训练过程，抵御错误共识带来的负面影响。

Conclusion: CoDA证明了完全无监督的自蒸馏框架可以有效利用模型自身的共识与分歧信号来提升推理质量，其中负分支对防止错误共识放大至关重要，为无需外部监督的推理优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation improves language-model reasoning by querying a teacher on states actually visited by the student. Recent methods create a powerful information asymmetry by exposing the teacher to privileged context, yet they fundamentally rely on external supervision---such as gold solutions or verifiers---to construct this advantage. We introduce CoDA (Consensus and Disagreement Alignment), a fully unsupervised framework that creates reliable privileged information entirely from the latent uncertainty structure of a model's own unlabeled rollouts. CoDA extracts two complementary signals. In the positive branch, answer-level consensus identifies a stable reasoning mode, which conditions a frozen self-teacher to provide dense distributional guidance on fresh student trajectories. However, because agreement does not guarantee correctness, positive-only distillation risks amplifying correlated errors into a false consensus. To break this harmful feedback loop, CoDA incorporates a negative branch that exploits disagreement: minority trajectories are treated as unstable alternatives and gently penalized via a reference-anchored, KTO-style calibration objective. This unpaired binary feedback provides robust regularization without requiring the strong assumption that the consensus is the absolute ground truth. Empirical evaluations on competition-level mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that CoDA significantly improves reasoning, outperforming self-generated baselines and effectively stabilizing training against erroneous consensus.

</details>


### [219] [Quantum-Classical Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Solving Fuzzy Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08782)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子-经典物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（QCPIKAN），用于求解模糊微分方程。该网络结合ChebyKAN模块和参数化量子电路，同时逼近α-截集的上下端点函数，并建立了统一的误差分析框架，理论上证明了QCPIKAN在量子纠缠特征带来的表示增益超过额外计算误差时具有更小的先验误差界。数值实验表明QCPIKAN在椭圆、抛物和双曲型方程中均优于PIKAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊微分方程在工程与科学中广泛存在，但传统数值方法难以高效求解。现有的物理信息神经网络（如PIKAN）在求解模糊微分方程时精度有限，需要引入新的计算范式来提升预测能力。

Method: QCPIKAN以时空坐标和隶属度水平为联合输入，采用ChebyKAN模块与参数化量子电路构建混合函数逼近器，同时逼近α-截集对应的上下端点函数，并将控制方程、初边值条件和模糊结构约束融入训练目标。理论上建立了统一的误差分析框架，将端点解误差分解为逼近、采样、优化和模糊结构约束四类误差。

Result: 在理想量子模拟环境中，对椭圆、抛物和双曲型方程进行了数值实验。结果表明QCPIKAN能捕捉解区间随隶属度水平增加的收缩效应；在多数测试隶属度水平下，PIKAN的平均相对L2误差约为QCPIKAN的1.1-2.7倍；在模糊对流算例中，PIKAN的平均波前位置误差约为QCPIKAN的1.77倍。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN为α-截集表示的模糊偏微分方程提供了一种具有较高预测精度的量子-经典混合物理信息计算框架，但两种模型在边界附近、高梯度区域和波前附近仍存在局部模糊结构违背，值得进一步改进。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network (QCPIKAN) dedicated to the solution of fuzzy differential equations. The network takes the spatiotemporal coordinates and membership level as joint inputs and employs ChebyKAN modules and a parameterized quantum circuit to construct a hybrid function approximator. It simultaneously approximates the lower and upper endpoint functions associated with the α-cuts and incorporates the governing equations, initial-boundary conditions, and fuzzy-structural constraints into the training objective. Theoretically, a unified error-analysis framework is established for QCPIKAN and PIKAN, in which the endpoint-solution error is decomposed into approximation, sampling, optimization, and fuzzy-structure constraint errors. Under the assumptions of well-posedness and residual stability, it is proved that QCPIKAN has a smaller a priori error bound when the representational gain introduced by quantum entanglement features exceeds the additional computational error. Numerical experiments are conducted for elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic equations in an ideal quantum-simulation environment. The results show that QCPIKAN captures the overall contraction of the solution interval as increases. At most tested membership levels, the mean relative L2 error of PIKAN is approximately 1.1-2.7 times that of QCPIKAN. In the fuzzy convection example, the mean wavefront-position error of PIKAN is approximately 1.77 times that of QCPIKAN. Nevertheless, both models still exhibit local fuzzy-structure violations near boundaries, in high-gradient regions, and around the wavefront. These results indicate that QCPIKAN provides a quantum-classical hybrid physics-informed computational framework with comparatively high predictive accuracy for solving fuzzy partial differential equations represented by α-cuts.

</details>


### [220] [Distilling Vision-Language Models for Robust Traffic Sign Perception in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08815)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMDA框架，利用语言锚定结构提升交通标志识别模型对物理对抗攻击的鲁棒性，无需对抗样本或推理开销，在多种攻击类型和骨干网络上均能一致提升鲁棒性且保持干净精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交通标志识别防御方法往往只对单一攻击类型有效，且在提升鲁棒性的同时会牺牲干净精度或其他攻击下的性能，缺乏一种能够全面、一致地提升鲁棒性的方法。

Method: LAMDA利用冻结的OpenCLIP文本编码器，从VLM生成的标志描述和类别名称构建两个固定的原型库，在训练过程中通过两个互补的辅助损失监督视觉特征学习语言锚定结构。推理时丢弃适配器和原型库，仅保留标准骨干网络和分类器。

Result: 在GTSRB和LISA数据集上，使用四种骨干网络和三种物理攻击类型进行评测，LAMDA是十种方法中唯一能在所有攻击-骨干-数据集组合上一致提升鲁棒性的方法，在阴影攻击下提升高达+12.5个百分点，自然光干扰下提升+13.2个百分点，且几乎在所有情况下保持或提升干净精度。

Conclusion: LAMDA通过语言锚定结构的方向对齐训练，在不引入推理开销和对抗样本的情况下，实现了对多种物理攻击的一致性鲁棒性提升，为交通标志识别防御提供了一种通用且有效的新范式。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) models based on deep neural networks achieve strong clean-data performance but remain vulnerable to physically realizable adversarial attacks, including shadow perturbations, natural-light interference, and printed patches. Existing defenses often improve robustness against one attack type while degrading performance on others, and can reduce clean accuracy. We propose LAMDA (Language-Anchored Model for Direction Alignment), a training framework that transfers language-grounded structure into TSR models without using adversarial examples or adding inference-time overhead. LAMDA builds two fixed prototype banks from VLM-generated sign descriptions and class names using a frozen OpenCLIP text encoder, and uses them to supervise visual features through two complementary auxiliary losses during training. At inference, the adapter and prototype banks are discarded, leaving a standard backbone and classifier. Evaluated on GTSRB and LISA across four backbones and three physical attack types, LAMDA is the only method among ten evaluated that consistently improves robustness across all attack-backbone-dataset combinations, with gains of up to +12.5 pp under shadow attacks and +13.2 pp under natural-light attacks, while preserving or improving clean accuracy in nearly all cases.

</details>


### [221] [Hybrid Neural-Classical Correction for Frozen Time Series Foundation Models: A Comprehensive Ablation Study on High-Frequency Stock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08825)
*Kasun Dewage,Suranadi De Silva,Shankhadeep Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合神经-经典校正方法对冻结TimesFM（200M参数）在高频金融股票收益预测中的适配，比较了AttnCorrect（471K参数）和GatedLinear（49K参数）两种神经校正架构并结合随机森林残差学习。在10只科技股、200万数据点上，该方法达到0.597池化相关性，比冻结TimesFM提升6.4倍日均相关性；经典残差学习贡献最大，而更简单的神经架构在去除经典学习后表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础时间序列模型（如TimesFM）在通用任务上表现出色，但在高频金融等专业领域往往性能不足。因此需要研究如何有效适配冻结的基础模型到股票收益预测这类专业场景。

Method: 采用混合神经-经典校正方法：两种神经校正架构（多头自注意力的AttnCorrect和低秩双线性门控的GatedLinear）加上随机森林残差学习，对冻结TimesFM进行适配。在10只主要科技股的2百万数据点上进行系统性消融实验。

Result: 混合方法达到0.597池化相关性和6.4倍日均相关性提升；随机森林残差学习是最大贡献组件；去除经典学习后简单神经架构表现更好；自注意力是最大神经贡献；GatedLinear+RF以少9倍参数达到最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 有效的基础模型适配需要神经与经典组件的精心整合，经典方法在适配中发挥关键互补作用，同时更简单的神经架构在节省参数的同时能取得更优效果。

Abstract: Foundation models for time series forecasting demonstrate impressive zero-shot generalization but often underperform on specialized domains such as high-frequency finance. We present a comprehensive study of hybrid neural-classical correction for adapting frozen TimesFM (200M parameters) to stock return prediction during the volatile opening trading hour. We compare two neural correction architectures - AttnCorrect (multi-head self-attention, approximately 471K parameters) and GatedLinear (low-rank bilinear projection with gating, approximately 49K parameters) - each augmented with Random Forest residual learning. Through systematic ablation across 10 major technology stocks (NVDA, MSFT, AAPL, GOOG, GOOGL, AMZN, META, AVGO, TSLA, NFLX) spanning 2 million data points, we reveal critical insights: (1) The hybrid neural-classical approach achieves 0.597 pooled correlation and 6.4x mean per-day correlation improvement over frozen TimesFM; (2) Classical residual learning (Random Forest) provides the largest single-component contribution, matching or exceeding the neural correction component; (3) Simpler neural architectures surprisingly outperform complex ones when classical residual learning is removed; (4) Self-attention provides the largest neural-only contribution. GatedLinear+RF achieves best overall performance with 9x fewer neural parameters than AttnCorrect+RF. We report three complementary correlation metrics - mean per-day, cross-day cumulative, and pooled - to provide a complete picture of predictive quality. Our results provide practical guidance: effective foundation model adaptation requires careful integration of neural and classical components, with classical methods playing a crucial complementary role.

</details>


### [222] [The Cost of Adaptivity: Matching Lower Bounds Across Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08826)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Adria Binte Habib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高斯认证的有限时域组合法则：在M个独立坐标上保护每一坐标和每一时间点直至T的家族认证器，其最优归一化半宽平方为O(log(eM)+loglog(e^eT))量级。上界通过epoch拼接实现，下界利用独立高斯块增量和几何时间尺度证明匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应流程必须在不依赖oracle所拥有的额外信息（如梯度尺度或光滑度指标）的情况下工作，而鲁棒流程可能需要回答查询坐标和检查时间仅在数据被看到后才确定的问题。这种比较只有在明确说明oracle优势和有效性契约时才有意义，因此需要对nuisance适应和鲁棒性代价进行形式化定义。

Method: 通过slice归一化极小极大比率来形式化nuisance适应，该比率在每个nuisance切片内保留最坏情况实例；同时通过从预宣布的高斯查询扩展到任意事后检查来定义鲁棒性代价。上界采用epoch拼接技术，下界利用跨坐标的独立高斯块增量和几何时间尺度，在几何检查点网格上成立。

Result: 未知梯度尺度在线凸优化具有恒定代价；嵌套Hölder类上的逐点自适应代价为(log n / log log n)^(s1/(2s1+1))量级。作为模型监控，原始固定查询带的选取覆盖率严重下降（M=1时为0.30，M>=10时为0），而epoch拼接认证器在附加迭代对数宽度代价下保持家族覆盖率。

Conclusion: 实验对两个尖锐预测进行了证伪风险测试，两者均通过。该组合法则为理解自适应程序在nuisance信息和后验查询灵活性方面的根本代价提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Adaptive procedures must work without nuisance information an oracle may use, such as a gradient scale or smoothness index, and robust procedures may have to answer queries whose coordinate and inspection time are chosen only after the data are seen. Such comparisons are meaningful only when the oracle advantage and validity contract are stated explicitly. We formalize nuisance adaptation via a slice-normalized minimax ratio retaining the worst-case instance within each nuisance slice, and separately define the robustness cost of expanding from one preannounced Gaussian query to arbitrary post-hoc inspection. Our main result is a finite-horizon composition law for Gaussian certification: from M independent coordinates, a familywise certifier protecting every coordinate and time up to T pays optimal normalized squared half-width of order log(eM) + log log(e^eT), within the sample-mean-centered rectangular class. Epoch stitching gives the upper bound; independent Gaussian block increments across coordinates and geometric time scales give a matching lower bound, already holding on a geometric checkpoint grid, forcing quantiles of the realized maximum width so selection and stopping taxes add. Two benchmark regimes complete the picture: unknown gradient scale in online convex optimization has constant cost, while pointwise adaptation over nested Holder classes costs order (log n / log log n)^(s1/(2s1+1)). Cast as model monitoring, the law lets an analyst inspect any of M slice metrics at any data-dependent time: the naive fixed-query band's selected coverage degrades sharply, to 0.30 at M=1 and to zero for M>=10, while the epoch-stitched certifier holds familywise coverage at an additive iterated-logarithm width cost. Experiments put both sharp predictions at risk of refutation; both survive.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Routing: Decoupling Expert Dispatch and Aggregation in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08853)
*Zongfei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏MoE路由器中"调度"（选择专家）与"聚合"（加权输出）两个角色是否应解耦。作者通过在OLMoE-1B-7B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上的实验发现，路由器的得分排序与最优聚合权重高度不一致，并提出了Fixed-Dispatch Adaptive Aggregation (FDAA)——一个301K参数的轻量级后计算头，在冻结主干网络和专家的条件下优化聚合权重，取得了显著的语言建模改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由器用相同的分数同时完成专家选择和输出加权两项功能，但这两项任务的最优解可能不同。作者希望验证"选择"与"承诺"是否应该解耦，并探索更优的聚合策略。

Method: 首先对预训练OLMoE-1B-7B进行结构化口哨实验（固定Top-8专家ID、计算和总路由质量），仅改变集内聚合权重，发现结构化oracle可将交叉熵降低0.0160。据此提出FDAA：一个301K参数的轻量后计算头，在冻结主干/路由器/专家的条件下，直接用语言建模目标优化聚合权重。同时在DeepSeek-V2-Lite上复现固定调度审计。

Result: 在OLMoE上，FDAA在WikiText-103测试集上取得Delta CE = -0.1523的改进；混合域训练在WikiText-103、C4和Penn Treebank均获得稳健收益。在DeepSeek-V2-Lite上，最佳顶点头部空间在WikiText和C4上显著，路由器Top1仅在12.5%和16.7%的实例中识别出最佳所选专家。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持跨架构地将"专家选择"与"专家承诺"区分开来的观点。路由器的得分排序不能可靠地反映专家质量，引入独立的聚合头可以在不改变路由决策的情况下显著提升性能。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers commonly use the same scores both to select experts and to weight their already-computed outputs. We study whether these two roles, dispatch and aggregation, should be coupled. On pretrained OLMoE-1B-7B, we keep selected Top-8 expert IDs, expert computation, and total selected router mass fixed and change only within-set aggregation. A structured oracle improves full-horizon cross-entropy by 0.0160 +/- 0.0039 across three seeds; the router's top-scored expert is the counterfactual-best vertex only 17.2% of the time, with router-utility Spearman 0.030. We therefore train Fixed-Dispatch Adaptive Aggregation (FDAA), a 301K-parameter post-compute head optimized directly with the language-modeling objective while freezing the backbone, router, and experts. On OLMoE, FDAA improves fresh WikiText-103 test by Delta CE = -0.1523 +/- 0.0031 across three seeds, and mixed-domain training gives robust gains on WikiText-103, C4, and held-out Penn Treebank under frozen confirmatory evaluation. We also replicate the fixed-dispatch audit on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, which uses Top-6 routed experts plus shared experts. Best-vertex headroom remains significant on WikiText and C4, while router Top1 identifies the best selected expert in only 12.5% and 16.7% of audited examples. In a one-seed mixed-domain replication, FDAA improves locked WikiText and PTB, while C4 is statistically neutral. These results support a cross-architecture distinction between expert selection and expert commitment.

</details>


### [224] [Agentic Anomaly Detection with ORCA-Style Dynamic Inductive Bias Adaptation in Multimodal Wearable Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08859)
*Anushka Roy,Jyotirmoy Singh,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个用于无线体域网（WBAN）异常检测的智能体控制框架，通过轻量级信号统计量在推理时动态自适应时间感受野，无需额外训练参数或学习策略，在自定义WBAN数据集上达到AUROC=0.99的最佳效果，并在MIMIC-IV的分布外基准测试中展现出保守但稳健的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WBAN生成的多变量生理时间序列高度非平稳，且面临严格的计算和内存约束。现有异常检测方法通常依赖固定的时间上下文，这在异构信号场景下表现不一致，且需要针对具体数据集进行繁琐调参。时间感受野作为关键的结构先验（inductive bias）尚未被充分研究。

Method: ORCA采用智能体控制（agentic control）机制，在推理时基于轻量级信号统计量动态选择离散的时间上下文。核心是一个监督控制器（supervisory controller），它自主选择合适的时间感受野，实现状态依赖的结构先验自适应，无需引入额外可训练参数或学习策略，也无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在自定义WBAN数据集上，ORCA达到AUROC=0.99，与最强固定上下文基线性能相当，同时免去了预先调整时间窗口的需要。在MIMIC-IV这一具有挑战性的分布外基准上，ORCA展现出保守的泛化行为，未出现性能崩溃，表现出在异构临床条件下的稳健性。

Conclusion: 自适应时间结构先验控制（adaptive temporal inductive bias control）是在资源受限、非平稳生理时间序列中实现实用且稳健异常检测的设计原则。ORCA验证了在不增加训练复杂度的情况下，通过推理时的动态时间上下文选择即可获得与精心调参的固定基线相当甚至更稳健的性能。

Abstract: Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) generate multivariate physiological time series that are highly nonstationary and must often be processed under strict computational and memory constraints. A critical yet underexplored challenge in this setting is selecting an appropriate temporal receptive field, which serves as a strong inductive bias for anomaly detection models. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed temporal contexts, which can perform inconsistently across heterogeneous signal regimes and require dataset-specific tuning. We propose ORCA, an agentically controlled anomaly detection framework that dynamically adapts the temporal receptive field at inference time based on lightweight signal statistics. Rather than introducing additional trainable parameters or learned policies, ORCA employs a supervisory controller that autonomously selects among discrete temporal contexts, enabling state-dependent inductive bias adaptation without retraining. Across a custom WBAN dataset, ORCA achieves performance comparable to the strongest fixed-context baselines (AUROC = 0.99) while eliminating the need to tune temporal horizons in advance. We further evaluate ORCA on MIMIC-IV as a challenging out-of-distribution benchmark, observing conservative generalization behavior without performance collapse under heterogeneous clinical conditions. These results highlight adaptive temporal inductive bias control as a practical and robust design principle for anomaly detection in resource-constrained, nonstationary physiological time series.

</details>


### [225] [Approximation Rates for Metaplectic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08872)
*Ahmed Abdeljawad,Marcello Carioni,Elena Cordero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于辛变换（metaplectic operators）构建浅层神经网络的字典，扩展了Barron空间的概念，证明了metaplectic Barron空间与Sobolev空间之间的嵌入关系，并给出了蒙特卡洛逼近误差界。此外，作者设计了以该字典原子为构建块的深度神经网络架构，用于逼近含时薛定谔方程的解，其性能优于经典物理信息神经网络（PINN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Fourier变换在表示某些函数类（尤其与量子力学相关的函数）时存在局限，作者希望通过引入辛几何动机下的metaplectic变换来扩展Barron空间，从而获得更高效的函数逼近能力。

Method: 1) 利用metaplectic变换扩展Barron空间定义；2) 建立metaplectic Barron空间与Sobolev空间的嵌入关系；3) 构造神经metaplectic字典并证明有限线性组合逼近的蒙特卡洛误差界；4) 设计以字典原子为构建块的深度神经网络架构，并应用于含时薛定谔方程求解。

Result: 证明了metaplectic Barron函数的蒙特卡洛逼近界，数值实验表明所提出的神经网络架构在逼近含时薛定谔方程解方面优于经典PINN架构。

Conclusion: metaplectic字典为浅层神经网络逼近提供了新的理论框架，并在量子动力学问题中展现出比经典方法更优的实用性能。

Abstract: In this paper we develop quantitative approximation results for shallow neural networks constructed using a dictionary based on metaplectic operators. First, we extend the concept of Barron spaces by considering a symplectically motivated extension of the Fourier transform, known as the metaplectic transform. Then, after establishing embedding between metaplectic Barron spaces and Sobolev spaces we consider a neural metaplectic dictionary and we prove Monte-Carlo approximation bounds for metaplectic Barron functions using finite linear combinations of atoms of the dictionary. Finally, we validate the introduction of the neural metaplectic dictionary by devising a deep neural network architecture that uses as building blocks the atoms of the dictionary. We test it to approximate solutions of time-dependent Schrödinger equations, demonstrating better performance compared to classical phyisics informed neural networks architectures.

</details>


### [226] [DistillCache: KL-Guided Adaptive KV-Cache Eviction for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08878)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistillCache，一个基于强化学习的KV缓存逐出框架，将KV缓存淘汰建模为序列决策问题，利用模型内部信号（注意力统计、值范数、熵、位置）训练轻量级策略网络，通过REINFORCE算法和KL散度奖励保持全缓存输出分布，在Mistral-7B上以25%缓存预算保留94.2%的LongBench准确率，超越现有启发式和强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大模型的KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成长上下文推理的严重内存瓶颈。现有启发式淘汰方法（如H₂O和SnapKV）依赖静态注意力或位置信号，无法捕捉token未来的预测影响力，需要更智能的缓存淘汰策略。

Method: 将KV缓存淘汰形式化为序贯决策问题，学习轻量级策略网络，输入特征包括注意力统计、值范数、熵和位置等丰富内部信号，使用REINFORCE算法训练，以每步KL散度作为奖励函数来保持完整缓存的输出分布。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3上，以25%缓存预算在LongBench上保留94.2%的全缓存准确率；相比H₂O和SnapKV等启发式基线提升最多2.7个百分点；相比ForesightKV和RLKV等并发RL方法提升最多1.4个百分点；在推理基准上与最优并发方法相当，在激进压缩下超越之；实现最高2.1倍全缓存吞吐量。

Conclusion: 学习和分布感知的KV缓存淘汰策略在内存高效的长时间上下文LLM推理中表现出显著有效性，DistillCache在准确率保持和推理效率上均优于现有最强基线和并发方法。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their Key-Value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck for long-context inference. Existing heuristic eviction methods (e.g., H$_2$O and SnapKV) rely on static attention or positional signals that often fail to capture a token's future predictive influence. We propose DistillCache, a reinforcement learning framework that formulates KV-cache eviction as a sequential decision problem. DistillCache learns a lightweight policy network using rich internal model signals (attention statistics, value norms, entropy, and position) and trains it with REINFORCE via a per-step KL-divergence reward to preserve the full-cache output distribution. On a 7B-parameter instruction-tuned Transformer (Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3), DistillCache retains 94.2% of full-cache accuracy on LongBench at a 25% cache budget, outperforming both strong heuristic baselines (H$_2$O, SnapKV) by up to 2.7 absolute points and, under our re-implementations, concurrent RL-based methods (ForesightKV, RLKV) by up to 1.4 points on long-context tasks. On reasoning benchmarks, DistillCache is competitive with the best concurrent method and surpasses it under aggressive compression. It also delivers up to 2.1x full-cache throughput while maintaining competitive practical efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of learned, distribution-aware policies for memory-efficient long-context LLM inference.

</details>


### [227] [Federated Attention Autoencoders with a Stochastic Aggregation Scheme for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08906)
