*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种适用于基于注意力机制的自编码器的新型聚合函数，用于联邦学习中的异常检测任务，在KDDCUP10数据集上相比传统自编码器，F1分数提升2.9%，AUC ROC提升5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化数据环境中的异常检测对机器学习实现是项挑战，尤其在数据无法共享的场景下。虽然联邦异常检测已有进展，但基于注意力机制的自编码器模型在联邦学习中应用不足，主要原因是缺乏针对此类网络的合适聚合函数。

Method: 提出两种针对注意力自编码器的新型聚合函数，能够更好地保留网络中记忆模块内存储的学习信息。

Result: 在KDDCUP10数据集上评估，所提方法相比传统自编码器，F1分数提升2.9%，AUC ROC提升5.1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种聚合函数有效改善了注意力自编码器在联邦异常检测中的性能，优于传统自编码器方法。

Abstract: Outlier detection in decentralized data environments is a challenging task for many machine learning implementations, particularly in settings where data cannot be shared. Recently, there have been advances in federated outlier detection, some of which are based on the use of autoencoder networks. The introduction of attention mechanisms to autoencoders boosts their efficiency. However, the application of attention-based models in federated learning remains underdeveloped due to the absence of proper aggregation functions for these types of networks. In our work, we propose two novel aggregation functions tailored for attention-based autoencoders, which better preserve the learned information stored within the memory modules of these networks. We evaluated our approach on the KDDCUP10 dataset, and we showed that the proposed methods achieve up to 2.9\% and 5.1\% better results for F1 score and AUC ROC respectively when compared to traditional autoencoders.

</details>


### [228] [A Domain-Structured Ensemble Framework for Perioperative Outcome Prediction Using Electronic Health Record Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08920)
*Shikhar Shukla,Cristina Barboi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域结构的集成学习框架用于围手术期结局预测，利用电子健康档案数据，将预测因子分为患者相关、手术相关和麻醉相关三个领域，通过梯度提升模型生成独立风险估计并经逻辑回归元学习器整合。在5,386例手术（术后谵妄）上验证，AUROC达0.899，校准性能优异，优于单阶段模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有围手术期风险预测模型存在局限：手术人群覆盖面窄、术中数据不完整、校准不佳、可解释性有限，需要一种更稳健且可解释的预测框架。

Method: 采用领域结构化集成框架：将预测因子按患者、手术、麻醉三个领域组织，各领域分别构建梯度提升模型产生独立风险估计，再通过逻辑回归元学习器（栈式集成）整合这些估计。以术后谵妄为示例结局，使用病例对照样本（5,386例手术遭遇），严格定义POD（ICD编码+Confusion Assessment Method筛查阳性），排除术前痴呆患者。

Result: 栈式元学习器AUROC达0.899（95%CI: 0.891-0.906），PR-AUC 0.881，Brier得分0.126；优于最佳单阶段模型的AUROC 0.849。领域消融显示优于仅手术领域模型（AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140）。时间外部验证（2017年后数据）AUROC达0.915。校准极佳（截距-0.006，斜率1.035）。决策曲线分析在临床合理阈值区间显示正净获益。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架支持替代结局、扩展预测领域和动态风险更新，为可解释、校准感知的围手术期临床决策支持提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Perioperative risk prediction models are often limited by narrow surgical populations, incomplete intraoperative data, poor calibration, and limited interpretability. We present a domain-structured ensemble framework for perioperative outcome prediction using routinely collected electronic health record (EHR) data. Predictors are organized into patient-related, surgery-related, and anesthetics-related domains. Domain-specific gradient boosting models generate independent risk estimates that are integrated through a logistic regression meta-learner. We demonstrate the framework using postoperative delirium (POD) in a case-control sample of 5,386 surgical encounters (2,693 cases, 2,693 controls) from a statewide health information exchange. POD required both delirium-related ICD codes and a positive Confusion Assessment Method screening within seven postoperative days; patients with preexisting dementia were excluded. The stacked meta-learner achieved AUROC 0.899 (95% CI: 0.891-0.906), precision-recall AUC 0.881, and Brier score 0.126, compared with AUROC 0.849 for the best single-stage model. Domain ablation showed improved discrimination and calibration over a surgery-only model (AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140). Temporal validation on held-out post-2017 data yielded AUROC 0.915. Calibration was excellent, with intercept -0.006 (95% CI: -0.083 to 0.070) and slope 1.035 (95% CI: 0.982 to 1.088). Decision curve analysis, corrected for case-control sampling, showed positive net benefit across clinically plausible thresholds. The modular framework supports alternative outcomes, extension of predictor domains, and dynamic risk updating, providing a scalable foundation for interpretable, calibration-aware perioperative clinical decision support.

</details>


### [229] [Idea Search: Guiding Tree Search with Ideas to Explore Diverse Scientific Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08958)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Hao Cui,Michael P. Brenner,Subhashini Venugopalan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Idea Search框架，通过将方法分解为原子想法并动态维护"想法库"，解决纯树搜索在科学编码中容易陷入局部最优的问题，在scRNA-seq批整合任务上将平均得分从0.678提升至0.697。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯树搜索在自动化科学编码中虽然强大，但在广阔的科学方法搜索空间中容易陷入局部最优或无效循环，缺乏系统性探索能力。

Method: Idea Search包含三步：(1)将现有方法分解为原子想法；(2)从想法库中采样以引导代码变异分支；(3)通过执行发现新想法并动态更新想法库。

Result: 在scRNA-seq批整合任务上，Idea Search突破强纯树搜索基线的瓶颈，平均得分从0.678提升至0.697，最佳得分达到0.728。消融实验发现：想法库增强对bandit采样有效但对随机采样无效；优先探索新想法的提示能发现稀有最优解；增加采样级探索反而适得其反。

Conclusion: Idea Search通过结构化的想法库注入与动态更新，有效提升树搜索的系统性探索能力，并揭示了各设计选择（如采样策略、提示方式、探索程度）对性能的影响规律。

Abstract: Tree Search-based test-time scaling of LLMs is a powerful tool for automated scientific coding. However, pure Tree Search sometimes struggles with systematic exploration, becoming trapped in local optima, or unproductive loops, especially in the vast search space of scientific methods. To address this limitation, we propose Idea Search, a framework that systematically integrates a dynamic "Idea Bank" into Tree Search. Idea Search involves three steps: (1) decomposing existing methods into atomic ideas, (2) sampling from this bank of ideas to guide branches of code mutations, and (3) dynamically updating the bank with new ideas discovered through execution. On single-cell RNA-sequencing (scRNA-seq) batch integration, Idea Search reliably breaks the plateau of a strong pure Tree Search baseline, improving the mean score from 0.678 to 0.697 and reaching a best score of 0.728. We then characterize which design choices drive these gains: bank augmentation helps bandit sampling but not random sampling, "Exploratory" prompting that prioritizes new ideas surfaces the rare best-performing solutions, while increasing sampling-level exploration is counterproductive.

</details>


### [230] [Gradient Under Microscope: Benchmarking Resource Utilization of Memory-Efficient Gradient Computation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08961)
*Sarthak Mahapatra,Zihan Zhou,Khatoon Khedri,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,Reza Rawassizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种记忆策略（标准训练、梯度检查点、梯度累积）和五种优化器（SGD、Adam、Adagrad、Adadelta、共轭梯度下降）在四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B、NanoVLM）上的表现，发现梯度累积是最可靠的方法，且Adam并非普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练的资源和能耗日益攀升，对电力供应和碳预算造成压力，亟需在受限硬件条件下进行内存高效训练的系统性研究。

Method: 在四种Transformer架构上，对五种优化器和三种记忆策略进行交叉基准测试，综合测量训练损失、GPU利用率、训练时间和内存使用等指标。

Result: 梯度累积在视觉语言模型上降低训练损失约一个数量级，在语言模型上降低约四倍且不增加GPU内存；Adadelta和SGD在编码器和自回归架构上优于Adam；梯度检查点对视觉Transformer有益但对编码器模型有害，且可使训练时间增加至多60%；GPU利用率主要由架构决定，范围从8-15%到96-99%。

Conclusion: 研究为资源高效模型训练中的优化器和梯度策略选择提供了实用指南，挑战了Adam普遍最优的常规认知。

Abstract: AI training's rising resource intensity is straining electricity supplies and carbon budgets, motivating systematic study of memory-efficient training on constrained hardware. We benchmark five gradient optimizers (SGD, Adam, Adagrad, Adadelta, and Conjugate Gradient Descent) under three memory strategies (standard training, gradient checkpointing, and gradient accumulation) across four transformer architectures (ViT, ModernBERT, Llama 3.1 1B, and NanoVLM), measuring training loss, GPU utilization, training time, and memory usage. Gradient accumulation emerges as the most reliable strategy, cutting training loss by roughly an order of magnitude on the vision-language model and about four-fold on the language model without additional GPU memory. Contrary to common practice, Adam is not universally superior: Adadelta and SGD outperform it on the encoder and autoregressive architectures. Gradient checkpointing's effect is strongly architecture-dependent, improving vision transformer loss while severely degrading the encoder model, and it increases training time by up to 60% on memory-bound models. GPU utilization is governed primarily by architecture, ranging from 8-15% for the memory-bound language model to 96-99% for compute-bound vision models. These findings provide practical guidelines for optimizer and gradient-strategy selection in resource-efficient model training and deployment.

</details>


### [231] [Math-Vision Diagrams: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Mathematical Diagram Generation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08964)
*Harish Kashyap,Kiran Byadarhaly,Sriram Chakaravarthy,Sanyukta Tuti,Aryan Mistry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Math-Vision Diagrams，这是首个专门用于评估LLM数学图表生成能力的基准，首次在同一统一框架下同时评估文本到代码和文本到图像两种生成范式。基于Math-Vision基准选取了2920张高质量竞赛题目图像，并构建了结合LLM集成与领域专家审核的流水线及评估指标套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本提示生成数学精确图表是LLM尚未充分探索的关键能力，对课程准备、题目自动排序和科学出版等领域具有重要价值。现有基准如MathVision和MathVista聚焦于数学推理，而DiagramGenBenchmark和MermaidSeqBench针对通用图表生成，均缺乏专门用于评估LLM数学图表生成能力的标准化提示-图像对数据集。

Method: 从Math-Vision基准的3040张图像中筛选出2920张来自高质量竞赛题目且具有必要视觉上下文的图像子集。提出了一种结合多个LLM集成与领域专家（SME）审核的新型流水线用于构建基准，并配套开发了一套评估指标体系。基准设计上不依赖于底层编码语言或模型类型，能在统一设置下评估文本到代码和文本到图像两种范式。

Result: 通过测试多个领先模型，实验表明LLMs在数学图表生成任务上表现困难，现有模型难以胜任这一需要空间推理、数学推理和渲染系统完美协调的复杂任务。

Conclusion: Math-Vision Diagrams填补了数学图表生成专用评估基准的空白，为未来改进LLM在数学精确图表生成能力上提供了统一的评估标准。所有代码、数据、流水线和评估脚本将完全开源，以促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: The generation of mathematically precise diagrams from tex- tual prompts has emerged as a critical yet underexplored capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). This has been of interest to researchers in the areas of curriculum preparation, automated ranking of problem sets, and scientific publishing. For LLMs to achieve this, it requires per- fect coordination between Spatial Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, and Rendering systems. While existing benchmarks such as MathVision, MathVista are built for Math Reasoning or DiagramGenBenchmark, Mer- maidSeqBench on general purpose diagram generation, no prior work provides a standardized set of prompt, image pairs that can be used to evaluate the LLMs specifically on math diagram generation. This includes fields that span both both text-to-code and text-to-image paradigms. We introduce Math-Vision Diagrams, the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate LLMs on mathematical diagram generation, and the first to assess text-to-code and text-to-image generation paradigms together in a single unified setting, agnostic of the underlying coding lan- guage or model type. Building on the Math-Vision benchmark, we select a subset of 2920 images out of 3040 from high-quality competition problems with essential visual context. A novel pipeline combining an ensemble of LLMs with Subject Matter Expert (SME) curation is presented, together with a suite of evaluation metrics. Testing several leading models against this benchmark, we demonstrate that LLMs struggle with math diagram generation. All code, data, curation pipeline, and evaluation scripts will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [232] [Label-Free Parkinson's Disease Screening from Face and Voice through Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08976)
*Jiaheng Su,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全无标签的帕金森病（PD）面部+语音筛查方法，仅使用冻结的预训练编码器（面部Vision Transformer和HuBERT），无需任何PD标签参与拟合。语音模态通过合成构音障碍对比激活添加（CAA）方向检测，面部模态通过k近邻异常分数检测。在YouTubePD基准上，等权晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802，支持排除性分流诊断（NPV 0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是第二大常见神经退行性疾病，传统机器学习筛查方法需要PD标签，但数据受限于隐私问题和专家标注成本，因此需要开发无需标签的筛查方法。

Method: 方法包括三个关键部分：(1) 语音模态使用合成构音障碍CAA方向（通过对健康语音进行时间拉伸和气息退化构建），检测合成疾病方向与真实疾病方向的余弦相似度是否大于零（称为对齐原理）；(2) 面部模态使用k近邻异常分数，计算样本到对照嵌入簇的距离；(3) 等权晚期融合两个模态的检测结果。所有编码器均为冻结的预训练模型，无PD标签参与任何拟合过程。

Result: 在YouTubePD基准上，语音模态的合成-真实疾病方向余弦相似度为+0.37（CAA有效，AUROC 0.765）；面部模态余弦相似度为-0.48（CAA失败，但异常检测有效，AUROC 0.751）。等权晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802（95% CI [0.70, 0.89]），NPV为0.95。过度拟合审计显示语音检测器可干净迁移，但面部及融合结果可能偏乐观，需外部验证。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一种完全无标签的多模态PD筛查框架的可行性，语音模态通过合成退化CAA方向有效检测疾病，面部模态通过异常检测补充信号，融合结果支持规则排除的分诊应用场景。同时提出对齐原理作为CAA检测器是否有效的后验分析工具，但面部模态的结果仍需外部验证确认。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is the second most common neurodegenerative disorder. Typical machine learning screening methods require PD labels, but the available data is limited by privacy concerns and the need for expert annotation. We propose a label-free face-plus-voice PD screen built entirely on frozen pretrained encoders--a face-expression Vision Transformer and HuBERT--in which no PD label touches any fit; the reference is training controls only. The voice modality uses a synthetic-dysarthria contrastive activation addition (CAA) direction built from time-stretch and breathy degradation of healthy speech; the face modality uses a k-nearest-neighbor anomaly score to the control embedding cluster. We introduce the alignment principle, a post-hoc analysis showing that a synthetic-degradation CAA detector works when the cosine similarity between the synthetic and real disease directions exceeds zero. Measured on the YouTubePD benchmark, this cosine is +0.37 for voice (CAA works, AUROC 0.765) and -0.48 for face (CAA fails; anomaly succeeds, AUROC 0.751). Equal-weight late fusion reaches AUROC 0.802 (95% CI [0.70,0.89]) with NPV 0.95, supporting a rule-out triage interpretation. An overfitting audit shows the voice detector transfers cleanly, while the face-side--and thus fused--AUROC is potentially optimistic pending external validation.

</details>


### [233] [Twin Rollouts: Noise-Coupled Counterfactual Branching in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08982)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Xinran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了交互式视频世界模型中的反事实生成问题：在模型自生成的轨迹中，若从某一步开始改变动作序列会产生什么结果。作者提出"噪声耦合双子轨迹"框架，通过精确的反绎（abduction）步骤和噪声耦合机制，使最小变化原则成为可验证的属性，并利用模拟器状态分叉生成可验证奖励用于后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型仅在事实预测上进行训练和评估，缺乏对反事实生成的研究。反事实生成对理解和评估世界模型的因果推理能力至关重要，但面临反绎近似反转的难题及最小变化原则难以验证的挑战。

Method: 提出噪声耦合双子轨迹形式化框架：事实分支与反事实分支共享生成前缀和未来外生噪声序列，仅在干预点后的动作流上分歧。由于事实分支是自生成的，其外生噪声精确已知，反绎步骤精确成立。同时定义时空局部性度量来惩罚干预因果后代之外的发散，程序化实现可验证的反事实生成。

Result: 本文为研究笔记性质，尚未完成实验。主要贡献在于：1）建立了反事实生成的形式化框架；2）证明了自生成轨迹下反绎精确性的理论优势；3）设计了可验证的时空局部性度量；4）提出利用模拟器状态分叉生成真值反事实重渲染作为后训练奖励的方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为视频世界模型的反事实推理提供了理论框架和方法论基础，通过噪声耦合巧妙解决了反绎的近似反转难题，将最小变化原则转化为可程序化验证的属性。实验部分将在后续工作中呈现。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate rollouts autoregressively under an action stream, yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on factual prediction. We study counterfactual generation inside the rollout: given a trajectory the model has itself generated, what would have happened had the actions differed from step t* onward? We formalize noise-coupled twin rollouts --- a factual and a counterfactual branch sharing the generated prefix and the future exogenous noise sequence, diverging only in the action stream at an intervention point. Because the factual branch is self-generated, its exogenous noise is known exactly: the abduction step of Pearl's counterfactual procedure is exact by construction, sidestepping the approximate-inversion problem faced by editing-based pipelines. Noise coupling further turns the minimal-change principle into a per-sample verifiable property: we define a spatiotemporal locality metric that penalizes divergence outside the causal descendants of the intervention, computable against simulator ground truth without a learned judge. Forking the simulator state at t* yields ground-truth counterfactual re-renders, which we use as verifiable rewards for post-training. This note establishes the formal framework, metric definitions, and positioning; experiments are forthcoming.

</details>


### [234] [SoftMCC: An MCC-Brier Calibration Bridge for Threshold-Free Model Selection under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08984)
*Özkan Canay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftMCC——一种基于后训练概率混淆计数的MCC验证框架，通过协方差归一化的概率-标签关联度量和Tie感知的共享池选择协议，在18种设置下实现最佳稳定性（平均秩2.31，Kendall's W=0.659），但模型选择效用无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡二分类中的模型选择常用MCC，但阈值化使得验证排名依赖阈值选择，导致排名不稳定且缺乏概率信息利用。

Method: SoftMCC框架包含三个核心组件：（1）在已建立的概率值混淆计数上运行后训练MCC验证；（2）耦合MCC特定的校准恒等式；（3）采用Tie感知的共享池选择协议。核心分数是协方差归一化的概率-标签关联，对硬预测可精确退化为MCC，且受Pearson界约束。

Result: 在18个设置、12次去重安全分组重复中，SoftMCC获得最佳稳定性平均秩（2.31）和最高Tie校正Kendall's W（0.659），Friedman检验显著（p=0.007）。但选型效用无优势：6个预设比较中有3个的测试MCC差为负，仅F1@best通过Holm校正（p=0.014），数据集级检验不显著（p=0.117）。标签置换将W降至0.092；温度缩放会改变SoftMCC排名（Spearman=0.851），而基于秩和阈值优化的指标保持不变量。

Conclusion: SoftMCC是一个对校准敏感的MCC族模型选择器，具有良好的稳定性和有界性，但效用证据有限，且其排名受校准变化影响，在不同设置下与AUPRC和MCC@0.5有统计学区分。

Abstract: Model selection for imbalanced binary classification often uses the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), but thresholding makes validation rankings threshold-dependent. SoftMCC is a post-training MCC validation framework on established probability-valued confusion counts, coupling an MCC-specific calibrated identity with a tie-aware, shared-pool selection protocol. Its core score is a covariance-normalized probability-label association, reduces exactly to MCC for hard predictions, and is Pearson-bounded. Under perfect population calibration it equals the Brier skill score with identical candidate ordering; outside that regime the gap does not identify calibration error. Across 18 settings with 12 duplicate-safe grouped repeats, SoftMCC attains the best stability mean rank (2.31) and highest mean tie-corrected Kendall's W (0.659), with a significant Friedman test (p=0.007); Nemenyi analysis separates it from AUPRC and MCC@0.5, while 14-source-family sensitivity retains only the latter. Selected-model utility shows no advantage. Three of six prespecified comparisons have negative mean test-MCC differences, only F1@best survives Holm correction (p=0.014), and the dataset-level test is not significant (p=0.117). Label permutation lowers mean W to 0.092; temperature scaling shifts SoftMCC rankings (mean Spearman 0.851) whereas rank-based and threshold-optimized metrics remain invariant. SoftMCC is a calibration-sensitive MCC-family selector with bounded stability and utility evidence.

</details>


### [235] [Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Tracking in Vision-Language Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09011)
*Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Shufan Yang,Haoru Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDA-UQ框架，将后验不确定性量化从静态映射范式转变为动态分布感知过程，利用高斯混合模型建模VLM嵌入空间以动态估计不确定性，在推理阶段自适应响应数据分布变化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验不确定性量化方法局限于拟合源域的失败模式，忽视了测试分布的动态变化特性，导致在分布漂移场景下可靠性不足。作者旨在解决后验UQ方法无法动态适应测试分布变化这一核心问题。

Method: 提出动态分布感知不确定性量化框架（DDA-UQ）：训练阶段使用高斯混合模型建模VLM嵌入空间并提取分布证据，动态推导不确定性估计；推理阶段设计能够动态响应数据分布变化的机制。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上显著超越了现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: DDA-UQ将不确定性量化的范式从静态映射转向动态分布感知过程，有效解决了后验方法对源域过拟合的问题，为VLM在安全关键场景中的可靠部署提供了更稳健的不确定性估计方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) aims to measure the reliability of model predictions, serving as a critical safeguard for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in safety-critical scenarios. Post-hoc approaches are widely adopted due to their lightweight nature, mapping the outputs of VLMs to uncertainty measures through learnable modules or inductive summarization. However, Post-hoc approaches remain inherently confined to fitting the failure patterns of the source domain, ignoring the dynamic nature of test distributions. To address this challenge, we propose a Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Quantification framework (DDA-UQ) that shifts the paradigm from static mapping to a dynamic distribution-aware process. During training, we leverage a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the VVLMs'embedding space and extract distributional evidence, thereby dynamically deriving uncertainty estimates. During inference, the design dynamically responds to changes in the data distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [236] [HOPPER: Learnable Hop Extraction for Linearized Graph Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09031)
*Isuru Herath,Arin Gopakumar,Sharan Sahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOPPER是一种端到端可学习的线性化图序列模型（LGSM）扩展，通过可学习的跳序列提取机制替代固定图算子，结合状态空间模型处理长程图依赖，在多个基准上达到最优或具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络通过重复消息传递传播信息，导致信息传播深度与非线性变换深度耦合，引发过平滑、过压缩和长程信息丢失等问题。现有LGSM虽通过解耦信息深度与处理深度来缓解，但依赖固定图算子构造序列，无法根据输入图、节点特征和下游任务自适应调整传播方式。

Method: HOPPER学习如何提取跳（hop）序列，再输入现代状态空间模型处理。支持特征条件化、结构感知、图和跳自适应的传播机制，同时保持排列等变性。标准邻接矩阵和非回溯LGSM序列是该提取器族的特例。

Result: 在ECHO-Synth基准上达到最优或具有竞争力；在LRIM物理长程依赖基准上，通过调整消息回溯取消的最大邻域大小（结构记忆窗口）可优化精度。

Conclusion: 可学习的序列提取为长程图表示学习提供了灵活有效的方法，证明了自适应传播机制在LGSM框架中的价值。

Abstract: Graph neural networks typically propagate information through repeated message-passing layers, coupling the distance over which information travels with the number of nonlinear transformations applied. This coupling can make deep architectures difficult to optimize and can lead to over-smoothing, over-squashing, and the loss of long-range information. Linearized Graph Sequence Models (LGSMs) address this issue by separating information depth from processing depth and treating the successive propagation states of each node as a sequence. However, existing LGSMs construct these sequences using fixed graph operators, limiting their ability to adapt propagation to the input graph, node features, and downstream task. We introduce HOPPER, an end-to-end learnable extension of LGSM that learns how hop sequences should be extracted before they are processed by a modern state-space model. Our framework supports feature-conditioned, structure-aware, graph- and hop-adaptive propagation mechanisms while preserving permutation equivariance. Standard adjacency-based and non-backtracking LGSM sequences arise as special cases of our proposed extractor family. We show that HOPPER is state-of-the-art or competitive across the ECHO-Synth benchmark, and that varying the maximum neighborhood size of message backtracking cancellation (i.e. structural memory window) can optimize accuracy on the LRIM physics-based long-range dependency benchmark. These results demonstrate that learnable sequence extraction provides a flexible and effective approach to long-range graph representation learning.

</details>


### [237] [F2STNet: Fair and Federated Spectral-Temporal Modeling for Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09082)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Hewei Wang,Siyuan Cen,Haidong Huang,Yiyao Zhan,Zheyu Chen,Jinjiang You,Ai Jian,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F²STNet，一种用于图结构化时空预测的联邦学习框架，结合截断图傅里叶特征、轻量级对角状态空间时序编码器、图卷积以及公平性感知联邦聚合（FFA），在交通和空气质量数据集上取得了优于基线的预测精度，并改善了联邦实验中的最差客户端表现和客户端差异指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构化数据的时空预测在交通流量预测和环境监测中至关重要，但去中心化和异质性的数据使得序列建模与协同训练都面临挑战。如何在联邦学习的框架下有效处理图结构数据并进行时序预测，以及如何应对客户端异构性导致的公平性问题，是本文要解决的核心动机。

Method: F²STNet由三个关键组件构成：(1) 截断图傅里叶特征提取，用于暴露图的频率结构信息；(2) 轻量级对角状态空间时序编码器，在线性复杂度下建模长时时间依赖；(3) 图卷积层用于空间特征建模。此外，提出公平性感知联邦聚合（FFA），通过客户端验证损失和递增的公平性调度来调整FedAvg的先验权重，从而改善联邦训练中的公平性。

Result: 在PeMS04、HZMetro和KnowAir三个数据集上的实验表明，相较于评估的基线方法，F²STNet取得了更优的预测精度；在PeMS04上的联邦学习实验中，该方法进一步改善了最差客户端的预测表现以及客户端之间的性能差异指标。

Conclusion: F²STNet验证了将谱域特征、状态空间模型与公平性感知联邦聚合相结合的有效性，在集中式和联邦两种设置下均取得了优于基线的性能，为去中心化异构数据场景下的图时空预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal prediction on graph-structured data is central to traffic forecasting and environmental monitoring, yet decentralized and heterogeneous data complicate both sequence modeling and collaborative training. We propose F$^2$STNet, a federated forecasting framework that combines truncated graph-Fourier features, a lightweight diagonal state-space temporal encoder, graph convolution, and Fairness-aware Federated Aggregation (FFA). The spectral branch exposes graph-frequency structure, while the state-space layer models long temporal dependencies with linear complexity in the sequence length. FFA adjusts the FedAvg prior using client validation losses and an increasing fairness schedule. Experiments on PeMS04, HZMetro, and KnowAir show favorable forecasting accuracy relative to the evaluated baselines; federated experiments on PeMS04 additionally improve worst-client and client-dispersion metrics.

</details>


### [238] [RAVEN: Frozen Random Graph Reservoirs with Physics-Informed Interaction Fingerprints for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09099)
*Qingyang Zou,Jiaye Huang,Hangbo Xie,Jiayue Yin,Youyi Song,Jinfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAVEN是一种用于蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测的新框架，通过多视图冻结原子图编码器池(random reservoir)、确定性物理化学相互作用指纹和异构监督回归器的非负融合，在相似性隔离的PDBbind 2020R1和CASF-2016数据集上表现出强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从三维复合物结构定量估计蛋白质-配体结合亲和力是结构化学和分子建模中的基础任务，但现有的结构-亲和力数据有限、实验异质性高、构象依赖性强且对数据集划分敏感，导致可靠预测仍然具有挑战性。

Method: RAVEN采用多头reservoir机制：多个独立初始化且完全冻结的原子图编码器生成多样化结构投影（无需端到端优化图表示）；结合确定性物理化学相互作用指纹；由异构监督回归器（包括神经网络和基于树的回归器）处理，最后通过基于验证集的非负融合组合输出。随机reservoir扩展了结构特征覆盖范围，物理化学描述符和异构读取器提供互补信息和不同归纳偏置。

Result: 在基于GEMS相似性资源重建的相似性隔离PDBbind 2020R1分割以及受保护的CASF-2016子集上的评估展示了强预测性能。

Conclusion: 冻结的多视图图表示、显式物理化学统计量和异构模型融合为蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测提供了一种稳健且灵活的框架。

Abstract: Quantitative estimation of protein-ligand binding affinity from three-dimensional complex structures is a fundamental task in structure-based computational chemistry and molecular modeling. Reliable prediction remains challenging because available structure-affinity data are limited, experimentally heterogeneous, conformation-dependent, and sensitive to dataset partitioning. RAVEN (Randomized Atomistic Views with Ensemble Neural Reservoirs) utilizes a multihead reservoir of independently initialized and fully frozen atomistic graph encoders to generate diverse structural projections without end-to-end optimization of the graph representation. These projections are integrated with a deterministic physicochemical interaction fingerprint and processed by heterogeneous supervised readers, including neural and tree-based regressors, whose outputs are combined through validation-based nonnegative fusion. The random reservoir expands structural feature coverage across independent encoder realizations, whereas the explicit physicochemical descriptors and heterogeneous readers contribute complementary information and distinct inductive biases. Evaluation on a similarity-isolated PDBbind 2020R1 split reconstructed using GEMS similarity resources, together with the protected CASF-2016 subset, demonstrated strong predictive performance. The results indicate that frozen multi-view graph representations, explicit physicochemical statistics, and heterogeneous model fusion provide a robust and flexible framework for protein-ligand binding-affinity prediction.

</details>


### [239] [Real Data Closes Synthetic-to-Real Gap in Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09100)
*Yani Guan,Dengpan Dong,Zi Wei,Shuang Luo,Dan Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Kang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了化学结构图像识别（OCSR）在真实文档上的性能差距问题。通过微调21个识别器，发现标注的真实训练图像是最大的改进来源：在ACS基准上，Qwen2.5-VL的精确匹配率从0%（无真实数据）提升到46%（50.2%真实数据）。视觉塔LoRA的效果因基础模型而异（对Qwen无效，对InternVL3-8B帮助显著）。最佳配置在干净渲染上达到96%精确匹配，在ACS、CLEF-IP、UOB和USPTO真实基准上分别达到49%、65%、84%和76%。研究还表明基础模型与真实数据混合比例需联合选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OCSR在合成图像上表现看似接近解决，但在真实文档（专利、论文中的化学结构图）上准确率远低于预期（如Qwen2.5-VL-7B从91%降至16%以下）。作者旨在找出真实场景下性能不佳的主要瓶颈和改进来源，以推动大规模化学信息的自动化提取。

Method: 作者微调了21个识别器，系统地变化三个维度：视觉语言模型基础（Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL3-8B、GLM-4.1V-9B）、真实训练数据比例（0%至70%）、视觉塔适配策略（是否使用LoRA）。在ACS、CLEF-IP、USPTO、UOB等多个真实基准上评估精确匹配率，并补充了手写体LaTeX识别和图表转换等跨领域小规模实验。

Result: 真实标注图像是最大改进来源：Qwen2.5-VL在ACS上从15%提升到46%（50.2%真实数据）。视觉塔LoRA效果依赖基础模型：对Qwen无效果（+0.00），对InternVL3-8B大幅提升（+22.8至+34.6pt），对GLM-4.1V-9B中等帮助（+1.0至+9.6pt）。最佳配置达96%（渲染）、49%（ACS）、65%（CLEF-IP）、84%（UOB）、76%（USPTO）。无真实数据时基础模型差距为21%，70%真实数据时缩小至6%且排序改变。跨领域实验也显示基础模型排名不稳定。

Conclusion: 在真实视觉结构识别任务中，模型选择和适应策略必须基于目标任务进行联合评估。训练数据组成（特别是真实标注数据）比视觉塔适配策略的影响更大，且基础模型与数据混合比例不能独立选择。研究为OCSR及更广泛的视觉结构识别任务提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Millions of chemical structures appear in patents and papers only as drawings, and using that information at scale requires reading the drawings. OCSR appears nearly solved on synthetic images yet remains difficult on real documents: the starting recognizer, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, exceeds 91% accuracy on synthetic renders but falls below 16% on three real-world benchmarks (ACS, CLEF-IP, USPTO). To identify the main source of improvement, 21 recognizers were fine-tuned on mixtures of synthetically rendered structures and labeled real depictions from patents, journal figures, and hand-drawn collections, varying the vision language model (VLM) base, the fraction of real training data, and the vision-tower adaptation strategy. Labeled real training images make the largest difference. For Qwen2.5-VL, ACS exact match rises from 0.15 with no real data to 0.37 at 9.5% and 0.46 at 50.2%; a controlled experiment across three base models reproduces the trend. A vision-tower LoRA, in contrast, does nothing for Qwen (+0.00, paired p=1.00), substantially helps InternVL3-8B (+22.8 to +34.6 pt), and modestly helps GLM-4.1V-9B (+1.0 to +9.6 pt), so its value depends on the base model. The best configuration reaches 0.96 exact match on clean renders and 0.49, 0.65, 0.84, and 0.76 on ACS, CLEF-IP, UOB, and USPTO, respectively. Gaps between base models are largest without real data (0.21), shrink to 0.06 at 70% real data, and reorder the ranking; base model and real-data mixture must therefore be selected together. Small-scale experiments on handwritten image-to-LaTeX recognition and chart-to-table conversion show that base-model rankings also vary beyond chemistry. More generally, model and adaptation choices for visual structure recognition should be evaluated on the target task.

</details>


### [240] [A Probabilistic Circuit-Induced Pseudo-Metric for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09117)
*Bhumika K,Vidhya S,Narayanan C Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层似然向量（HLV）和分层似然距离（HLD），利用概率电路（PC）的层次结构进行分布外（OOD）检测，相比仅使用根节点标量似然的方法，能更有效地识别分布偏移并定位到具体PC节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于PC的OOD检测方法忽略了PC内部节点的层次结构，仅将整个电路简化为根节点的标量似然（或其不确定性），未能充分利用PC编码的概率摘要层级信息。

Method: 提出HLV表示（各条目标签对应选定PC节点的似然）和HLD伪度量（通过HLV的期望比较概率分布）；证明HLD是PC自然诱导函数类上的积分概率度量；开发了基于拟合优度假设检验的无监督OOD检测方法，阈值可从训练好的PC中精确解析计算。

Result: 在表格数据和MNIST数据集上的实验表明，利用PC层次概率摘要的方法在OOD检测上优于基于根似然、不确定性、典型性和核方法的基线，并能自然地将分布偏移定位到负责该偏移的PC节点。

Conclusion: PC的层次结构蕴含了丰富的概率摘要信息，通过HLV和HLD可以有效利用这些信息提升OOD检测性能，且无需在部署时使用留出的分布内数据，训练好的PC本身即可作为分布表示。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are tractable generative models whose internal nodes encode a hierarchy of probabilistic sum- maries over different variable scopes. Existing PC-based out- of-distribution (OOD) detection methods ignore this hierar- chy, reducing the entire circuit to the scalar likelihood (or its uncertainty) computed at the root. We introduce Hierar- chical Likelihood Vector (HLV), a representation whose en- tries are the likelihoods associated with selected PC nodes and define the Hierarchical Likelihood Distance (HLD), a PC-induced pseudo-metric that compares the probability dis- tributions through the expectations of their HLVs. We show that HLD is an integral probability metric over a function class naturally induced by the PC and develop a principled goodness-of-fit hypothesis test for unsupervised OOD detec- tion. Unlike existing approaches, the trained PC alone serves as the representation of the in-distribution: no held-out in- distribution data are required at deployment. We further show that the quantities required by the hypothesis test can be com- puted exactly, directly from the trained circuit, yielding an ap- proximate analytic decision threshold. Experiments on tabular and MNIST datasets demonstrate that exploiting the hierarchi- cal probabilistic summaries encoded through the PC improve OOD detection over root-likelihood, uncertainty-, typicality- and kernel-based baselines, while naturally localizing distri- bution shifts to the PC nodes responsible for the shift.

</details>


### [241] [MARA: Flow-Matching-Guided Multi-Agent Resource Allocation for Computational Resource Efficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09130)
*Hanye Zhao,Muning Wen,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARA框架，解决离散计算节点下多任务并发学习的资源分配问题。MARA结合条件流匹配预测损失轨迹，并通过多智能体自回归策略协调计算节点，以基于势能的进度奖励提供中间反馈。实验表明MARA比基线LARA在任务完成率上高出8.54个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将计算视为连续可分的吞吐量，但实际场景中计算由离散节点提供，任务随时间到达，存在不确定需求和受约束的序列决策，现有方法无法很好应对这一现实设定。

Method: 提出MARA框架，包含两个核心组件：一是利用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）预测未来损失轨迹，相比加权最小二乘法更精准；二是通过合作式多智能体自回归策略（cooperative multi-agent autoregressive policy）协调各计算节点。采用基于势能的进度奖励（potential-based progress reward）提供中间训练反馈，同时保持无折扣的任务完成目标。

Result: 在分布内、强化学习和视觉任务负载上，流匹配相比加权最小二乘法降低了剩余资源预测误差。在调度器的训练负载下，MARA平均完成63.46%的任务，比强基线LARA高出8.54个百分点，且在未见过的更重负载下仍保持领先。

Conclusion: MARA在离散计算节点和任务动态到达的现实设定下，通过流匹配损失预测与多智能体协作调度相结合，显著提升了任务完成率，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Allocating limited computation among concurrent learning tasks is difficult when each task must reach a target loss before a deadline but its required training effort is unknown. Existing approaches combine online loss prediction with adaptive resource allocation, yet commonly treat computation as continuously divisible throughput. We instead study a practical setting in which tasks arrive over time and computation is provided by discrete nodes. This setting introduces both uncertain demand and constrained sequential decisions. We propose MARA, which predicts future loss trajectories with conditional flow matching and coordinates compute nodes through a cooperative multi-agent autoregressive policy. A potential-based progress reward supplies intermediate training feedback while preserving the undiscounted task-completion objective. Across in-distribution, reinforcement-learning, and vision workloads, flow matching reduces remaining-resource prediction error relative to weighted least squares. At the scheduler's training load, MARA completes 63.46% of tasks on average, 8.54 percentage points above strong baseline Learning with Adaptive Resource Allocation (LARA), and remains ahead under unseen heavier workloads.

</details>


### [242] [SwiftQK: Fast and Communication-Efficient Tensor Parallelism for Query-Key Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09160)
*Gyudong Kim,Wonjun Han,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SwiftQK，一种多GPU RMSNorm内核，通过只交换标量归一化统计量并利用持久化内核实现死锁安全的通信与计算重叠，大幅减少Tensor Parallelism下QK-Norm的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QK-Norm能提升LLM训练稳定性和质量，但在Tensor Parallelism下每层归一化因子依赖完整隐藏向量，导致额外的跨GPU通信开销，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: SwiftQK设计了一种多GPU RMSNorm内核：仅交换标量归一化统计量而非完整向量，并通过死锁安全的持久化内核将Peer-to-Peer规约与独立的逐元素计算重叠执行。

Result: 在最近LLM上的评估显示，相对标准全量All-Gather方案，QK-Norm延迟降低81.4%-93.9%；端到端推理中，相对All-Gather基线TPOT平均降低29.5%，相对优化的标量聚合实现降低14.3%。

Conclusion: SwiftQK通过标量通信和计算重叠策略有效消除了TP下QK-Norm的通信瓶颈，显著提升LLM端到端推理性能。

Abstract: Query-Key Normalization (QK-Norm) improves the training stability and quality of modern Large Language Models (LLMs). However, under Tensor Parallelism (TP), layerwise QK-Norm introduces additional cross-GPU communication because the normalization factor depends on the full hidden vector. We present SwiftQK, a multi-GPU RMSNorm kernel that exchanges only scalar normalization statistics and overlaps the remaining Peer-to-Peer reduction with independent element-wise computation in a deadlock-safe persistent kernel. Evaluations on recent LLMs show that SwiftQK reduces QK-Norm latency by 81.4--93.9% relative to the standard TP QK-Norm using full-vector All-Gather. In end-to-end serving, SwiftQK reduces TPOT on average by 29.5% over the All-Gather-based baseline and by 14.3% over an optimized scalar-aggregation implementation.

</details>


### [243] [Tabular Numeric Stretch Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09162)
*Zihao Ye,Juyong Kim,Johnna Sundberg,Burak Varici,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为stretch transformation的框架，将数值特征预处理建模为优化问题，以提升目标函数的平滑性和可学习性。框架包含无监督和有监督两种变体，在38个TALENT基准数据集上的实验表明有监督stretch始终优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的数值特征具有异构性，其特征分布、尺度和统计性质差异很大。尽管深度学习在表格数据上的学习能力已有进步，但数值数据如何被转换为模型友好的表示这一问题仍未被充分探索，这正是本文的切入点。

Method: 提出stretch transformation框架，将数值特征预处理形式化为优化问题以平滑目标函数。包含两个变体：(1)无监督stretch，通过极小极大优化均匀重分配特征密度；(2)有监督stretch，通过最小化变换空间中目标函数的Dirichlet能量来优化目标感知的数值特征变换。

Result: 在TALENT基准的38个数据集上，有监督stretch一致优于所有基线方法。理论分析还表明：无监督stretch与分段线性编码（Piecewise Linear Encoding）密切相关，并随分箱数量增长逼近经验CDF变换；有监督stretch在细分箱极限下与目标编码（target encoding）密切相关。

Conclusion: 显式优化目标函数平滑性是一种强大且尚未被充分探索的表格深度学习策略。stretch transformation框架提供了理论依据和有效的实践方法，为表格数据中的数值特征预处理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Tabular data presents unique challenges for deep learning due to its heterogeneous nature, where numeric features exhibit diverse distributions, scales, and statistical properties. Although recent advances have improved how models learn from tabular data, how numeric data are transformed into model-friendly representations remains comparatively underexplored. We introduce the stretch transformation framework, which formulates numeric feature preprocessing as an optimization problem to make the target function smoother and thus more learnable. Our framework has two variants: (1) unsupervised stretch, which uniformly redistributes feature density via minimax optimization, and (2) supervised stretch, which optimizes target-aware numeric feature transformations from the perspective of target-function smoothness by minimizing the target function's Dirichlet energy in the transformed space. Our theoretical analysis further connects this framework to several popular transformations: unsupervised stretch is closely related to Piecewise Linear Encoding through a shared piecewise-linear geometry and approaches the empirical CDF transformation as the number of bins grows, while supervised stretch becomes closely related to target encoding in the fine-binning limit. Comprehensive experiments on 38 datasets from the TALENT benchmark demonstrate that supervised stretch consistently outperforms all baselines. These results show that explicitly optimizing for target function smoothness is a powerful and underexplored strategy for tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [244] [A Time-Frequency Dual-Domain Multi-Scale Convolutional Neural Network for Bearing Fault Diagnosis under Strong Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09174)
*Yanxi Ding,Tingyue Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时频双域多尺度卷积神经网络用于强噪声下的轴承故障诊断，在CWRU数据集上，干净条件下精度达99.75%，-4dB信噪比下保持92.50%，比单域基线提升7.25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强噪声环境下轴承故障诊断精度严重退化的问题，现有单域方法在噪声条件下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 构建时频双域分支网络：时域分支使用三个并行卷积核提取多尺度冲击特征，频域分支通过快速傅里叶变换提取抗噪谱结构信息，两分支特征融合后进行分类，模型仅110,122个参数。

Result: 在CWRU轴承数据集上，七个信噪比等级下，干净条件准确率99.75%，-4dB SNR下保持92.50%，较单域基线提升7.25个百分点，且噪声越强提升越明显。消融实验验证了各分支的独立贡献，对比WDCNN、DRSN-CW、MCNN和1D-LeNet均表现更优。

Conclusion: 时频双域多尺度架构在强噪声下具有显著鲁棒性优势，模型紧凑且性能优越，为强噪声工业场景下的轴承故障诊断提供了有效方案。

Abstract: To address the degradation of bearing fault diagnosis accuracy under strong noise, this paper proposes a time-frequency dual-domain multi-scale convolutional neural network. The time-domain branch employs three parallel convolutional kernels to capture multi-scale impulse features, while the frequency-domain branch applies the Fast Fourier Transform to extract noise-robust spectral structure information. Features from both branches are fused for fault classification, yielding a compact model of 110,122 parameters. Experiments on the CWRU bearing dataset across seven signal-to-noise ratio levels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves 99.75% accuracy under clean conditions and maintains 92.50% at -4 dB SNR, representing a 7.25 percentage-point improvement over the single-domain baseline with monotonically increasing gains under stronger noise. Ablation experiments validate the independent performance contributions of the time-domain multi-scale branch and the frequency-domain branch. Comparative experiments against WDCNN, DRSN-CW, MCNN, and 1D-LeNet confirm the superiority of the proposed method under strong noise conditions.

</details>


### [245] [FedA2L: Adaptive layer-wise learning rate adjustment in decentralized federated learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09208)
*Van Truong Vo,Khoa Nguyen,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedA2L是一种基于模型发散信号的动态逐层学习率调整方法，用于解决去中心化联邦学习在数据异构下的收敛效率问题，可实现最高4.94倍加速收敛并减少最多59%的通信轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中，统一的固定学习率忽略了不同网络层（基础层负责共识、专门层适配本地数据）的差异化优化需求，导致非独立同分布（non-IID）条件下梯度冲突和性能退化。

Method: FedA2L通过模型发散信号动态调整逐层学习率，结合本地更新强度和网络共识约束，无需额外通信或协调即可无缝集成到现有DFL协议中。

Result: 在多种DFL算法、模型架构和数据集上，FedA2L比原始DFL快最高4.94倍收敛，相比基于调度器的方法减少最多59%的通信轮次，并对严重数据异构、较大网络规模和稀疏拓扑表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedA2L是一种通用且高效的优化工具，适用于资源受限或大规模分布式学习场景（如边缘和物联网部署），代码已开源发布。

Abstract: Decentralized intelligence systems with heterogeneous devices and limited coordination increasingly rely on decentralized federated learning (DFL). However, DFL suffers from convergence inefficiency under data heterogeneity due to the use of a uniform learning rate (LR) that ignores layer-specific optimization needs. Foundational layers are responsible for maintaining network consensus, while specialized layers adapt to local data characteristics, leading to conflicting gradients and degraded performance under non-IID conditions. To address this fundamental tension, this work introduces FedA2L, a method that dynamically adjusts layer-wise LRs based on model divergence signals. By leveraging local update intensity and network consensus constraints, FedA2L seamlessly integrates into existing DFL protocols without additional communication or coordination. Extensive evaluations across DFL algorithms, various model architectures, and datasets demonstrate that FedA2L achieves up to 4.94 times faster convergence than vanilla DFL and reduces communication rounds by up to 59% compared to scheduler-based baselines. Furthermore, FedA2L exhibits resilience to severe data heterogeneity, larger network sizes, and sparse topologies, reducing communication overhead and establishing it as a versatile optimization tool for resource-constrained or large-scale distributed learning in edge and IoT deployments. The code is released at https://github.com/nclabteam/FedA2L.

</details>


### [246] [Beyond Solvability: Task Learnability as a Static Prior for LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09217)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Daoyuan Chen,Qianli Shen,Yilun Huang,Ying Shen,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajVal，一种基于轨迹的轻量级任务可学习性估计器，用于改进大型语言模型强化学习后训练中的任务采样策略，通过短探测运行和两个端点评估来预测任务对继续训练的积极响应程度，从而提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练采用统一任务采样，忽略了不同任务对优化的响应差异；已有的任务评估方法主要依赖快照式信号（如当前通过率或奖励），但相似可解性的任务在继续训练中的正向响应可能差异巨大，缺乏对任务可学习性的有效度量。

Method: 首先定义任务可学习性为固定RL后训练策略下任务对继续训练的预期正向响应的条件度量；通过分析每任务奖励轨迹验证可学习性的可复现性和预测性；提出TrajVal，一种轻量级探针式估计器，从短探测运行和两个端点评估中近似估计每任务可学习性，可作为静态先验或在线调度器的乘法先验。

Result: 在数学和逻辑推理基准上、多种模型规模下，TrajVal相比统一采样提升了数据效率，并且与在线调度方法结合时能提供互补增益。

Conclusion: TrajVal有效捕捉了任务在继续训练中的响应差异，为RL后训练的任务采样提供了实用的可学习性信号，可独立使用或与现有在线调度器协同工作。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training paradigm for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models, yet uniform task sampling allocates compute without regard to differences in how tasks respond to optimization. Existing task-valuation methods mostly rely on snapshot-based signals such as current pass rate or reward, which estimate how solvable a task is under the current policy. However, tasks with similar current solvability can still differ substantially in how positively they respond to further training. We study this residual axis as task learnability: a regime-conditional measure of expected positive response to continued training under a fixed RL post-training regime. By analyzing per-task reward trajectories, we find that learnability is reproducible across independently sampled training contexts and predictive of downstream utility. To make this signal practical before training begins, we propose TrajVal, a lightweight probe-based estimator that approximates per-task learnability from a short probe run and two endpoint evaluations. TrajVal can be used either as a standalone static prior for task sampling or as a multiplicative prior for existing online schedulers. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks across multiple model scales show that TrajVal improves data efficiency over uniform sampling and provides complementary gains when combined with online scheduling methods.

</details>


### [247] [Online Learning of Scale Parameters in Score-Driven Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09218)
*Fabrizio Lillo,Giulia Livieri,Gianluca Palmari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将得分驱动滤波器的增益视为决策变量，研究其在线学习机制，建立了基于KL散度的条件预测决策框架，推导了随机梯度和镜像下降更新规则，并给出动态遗憾界。模拟与股票指数波动率面板实证表明，有界镜像增益优于常数增益且避免指数链接的极端尖峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统得分驱动滤波器（如GAS模型）将增益视为固定或预先设定的参数，缺乏对增益本身的自适应学习机制。本文旨在将增益从超参数提升为可在线优化的决策变量，从而提升滤波器的适应性和预测性能。

Method: 将增益视为决策变量，以KL散度为损失建立条件预测决策框架。标量增益通过相邻得分负乘积作为随机梯度，aGAS缩放仅改变有效步长；单调可微链接诱导镜像下降几何，持久性通过Bregman项拉向参考增益。在凸性、紧致性和正则性条件下，为投影和折扣镜像更新建立动态遗憾界。

Result: 建立了动态遗憾界的理论保证；模拟验证了缩放、链接几何、持久性和坐标传输率的作用；股票指数波动率面板实证显示有界镜像增益与常数增益相当或更优，同时避免无界指数链接的极端尖峰，在多危机市场中改进最为显著。

Conclusion: 增益的在线学习是可行且有益的。有界镜像增益提供了稳健且具有理论保障的增益自适应方案，特别在波动率剧烈变化的多危机场景下表现优异，为得分驱动滤波器的增益选择提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: Score-driven filters multiply a scaled log-likelihood score by a gain that controls the update magnitude. We treat this gain as a decision variable and study its online learning. Conditional on the current state, observation, score, and scaling rule, each admissible gain induces a reachable next state and a one-step-ahead predictive density: scalar gains govern distance along a line, while diagonal gains govern coordinatewise transmission. Gain selection is therefore a conditional predictive decision problem with a Kullback-Leibler objective. For a scalar unscaled gain, the negative raw product of consecutive scores is the stochastic gradient of this loss; positive aGAS scaling only rescales the effective step. Monotone differentiable gain links induce mirror-descent geometries on bounded gain domains, while persistence yields a Bregman pull towards a reference gain. Under convexity, compactness, and regularity conditions, we establish dynamic-regret bounds for projected and discounted mirror updates relative to time-varying, current-information comparators. Simulations illustrate the roles of scaling, link geometry, persistence, and coordinatewise transmission rates. An out-of-sample panel of equity-index volatilities shows that the bounded mirror gain generally matches or outperforms a constant gain while avoiding the extreme spikes of a nominally unbounded exponential link, with the strongest improvements observed in multi-crisis markets.

</details>


### [248] [FedTVD: Balancing Data Quality and Quantity for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09221)
*Radwan Selo,Majid Kundroo,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTVD，一种联邦学习算法，通过结合总变差距离（TVD）和数据集规模对客户端贡献进行双重加权，以缓解数据异构性（标签分布偏斜和数据集大小差异）带来的模型偏差问题。实验表明FedTVD在FMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上均优于现有方法，在高度偏斜数据下比FedAvg提升最多10.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性（标签分布偏斜和数据集规模差异）会导致客户端更新有偏，阻碍全局模型收敛。传统方法如FedAvg仅依据数据集大小进行客户端加权，无法有效应对标签分布偏斜问题，因此需要更精细的加权策略。

Method: FedTVD在聚合时引入双重加权机制：一方面利用总变差距离（TVD）度量各客户端局部标签分布与均匀全局分布之间的差异，对偏斜严重的客户端赋予较低权重；另一方面结合数据集大小以保证可扩展性和公平性，从而缓解不平衡数据对全局模型的影响。

Result: FedTVD在FMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100三个数据集上以及所有数据异构水平下均一致优于现有最先进方法。在CIFAR-10高度偏斜数据下相比FedAvg取得最高10.6%的准确率提升，且在中等异构和IID场景下也保持最优性能。

Conclusion: FedTVD通过同时考虑数据质量和数据量的双重加权策略，有效缓解了联邦学习中数据不平衡的影响，实现了更稳定和更泛化的全局模型，为异构联邦学习场景提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed client devices while preserving data privacy. However, FL faces significant challenges due to data heterogeneity, particularly in terms of label distribution skewness and variations in dataset sizes, which can lead to biased model updates and hinder convergence. To address this, we propose FedTVD, a novel FL algorithm that weights client contributions during aggregation by considering both data quality and quantity. Unlike traditional FL approaches such as FedAvg, which rely solely on dataset size for client weighting, FedTVD integrates Total Variation Distance (TVD) to measure the divergence between each client's local label distribution and a uniform global distribution. Clients with highly skewed distributions receive lower weights, preventing unbalanced datasets with imbalances from disproportionately influencing the global model. At the same time, dataset size is incorporated to ensure scalability and fairness. This dual-weighting mechanism effectively mitigates the impact of data imbalance, leading to more stable and generalized global models. Experimental results show that FedTVD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all datasets (FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100) and all levels of data heterogeneity. Notably, it achieves up to 10.6% improvement over FedAvg on CIFAR-10 under highly skewed data, while maintaining top performance even under moderate and IID settings.

</details>


### [249] [Privileged Solutions or Context-Induced Teacher Behavior? Dissecting On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09228)
*Yuki Ichihara,Naoto Iwase,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过设计OP²SD实验（用其他问题的参考解替代配对参考解），解耦了OPSD中"目标特定特权信息"与"教师上下文行为"两个效应，发现OPSD的提升并非必然来自参考解本身，教师受上下文影响的监督行为才是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD（同策略自蒸馏）通常被理解为特权信息传递——教师看到目标问题的已验证解并监督学生的轨迹。但作者指出这种解释混淆了两个效应：参考解既揭示了当前实例的答案，又改变了教师提供词级监督时的上下文环境。作者希望解耦并分别评估这两种效应的重要性。

Method: 提出OP²SD（同策略自蒸馏与其他问题），该方法将配对的参考解替换为来自不同示例的问题与解答，同时保留学生轨迹、教师和蒸馏目标不变。通过对比OP²SD与OPSD的表现，分离出"目标特定特权"与"教师上下文行为"各自的贡献。

Result: 在三个模型和三个数学基准上，OP²SD相比基础模型有提升，且与OPSD的表现相当。这说明即使没有访问参考解决方案，蒸馏仍能取得相近的收益。

Conclusion: OPSD的收益并不必然源于参考解提供的目标特定特权信息；教师因上下文改变而产生的行为差异（context-induced behavior）是重要因素，为理解蒸馏机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is commonly interpreted as the transfer of privileged information: a teacher observes the verified solution to the target problem and supervises the student's trajectory. However, this interpretation conflates two effects. The reference solution not only reveals the answer to the current instance but also changes the context under which the teacher provides token-level supervision. We investigate the role of target-specific privilege with $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ (On-Policy Self-Distillation from Other Problems), which replaces the paired reference with a problem and solution from a different example, while preserving the student rollout, teacher, and distillation objective. Across three models and three mathematics benchmarks, $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ improves over the base model, remains competitive with OPSD. The success of $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ implies that OPSD gains do not necessarily come from access to the reference solution, and that the teacher's context-induced behavior is an important factor.

</details>


### [250] [DreOPD: Degraded-Reference Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation for Flow-matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09233)
*Mingfeng Lin,Chengfei Cai,Lin Xu,Yuxiang Wei,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DreOPD方法，将退化参考外推与在线策略蒸馏（OPD）相结合用于流匹配模型的后训练，通过将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，实现了兼具OPD稳定性和外推能力的后训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在生成领域的应用日益广泛，但下游任务适配通常依赖后训练，可能造成多任务目标冲突。强化学习直接优化奖励但存在高方差梯度和跨任务干扰问题，而传统的在线策略蒸馏缺乏外推能力。

Method: DreOPD将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，实现具有OPD稳定性的外推式后训练；同时使用轻度退化的参考来增强教师参考对比度，从而获得更清晰的外推方向。

Result: 在单教师和多教师设置下，DreOPD在平均性能上优于OPD和多任务强化学习基线，并在大多数指标上超过专业教师模型。

Conclusion: DreOPD成功架起了强化学习与在线策略蒸馏两大范式之间的桥梁，证明了退化参考策略能够有效提升流匹配模型的分布外推训练效果。

Abstract: Flow-matching models are now a mainstream method to image generation, but its adaptation to diverse downstream scenarios typically relies on post-training, which may cause conflicts among task-specific optimization objectives. Reinforcement learning enables direct optimization of task-specific rewards beyond the original models, yet trajectory-level optimization may incur high-variance gradients and cross-task interference. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense and stable supervision on student rollouts, but conventional teacher matching remains imitation-based. We propose DreOPD, a Degraded-reference extrapolative OPD method for flow-matching models that bridges these two paradigms. Our DreOPD converts implicit reward extrapolation into closed-form velocity regression, enabling extrapolative post-training with the stability of OPD. It further uses a mildly degraded reference to strengthen the teacher-reference contrast, yielding a clearer extrapolation direction. Experiments on single- and multi-teacher settings show that DreOPD outperforms OPD and multi-task RL baselines in average performance, while surpassing specialized teachers on most metrics.

</details>


### [251] [Label Granularity Skew in Federated Learning with Hierarchical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09236)
*Jaeheon Kim,Hokeun Kim,Bong Jun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦层次分类中新的统计异质性形式——标签粒度偏斜，并设计了Branch-wise Decoupled Fine-Tuning (BDFT)及其联邦版本FedBDFT来应对该问题，通过分支级分类器的解耦微调和联邦聚合，在严重标签粒度偏斜下显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端不仅存在数据分布差异，还可能具有不同的领域知识和标注能力，导致标签粒度不一致。现有研究忽视了这种标签粒度偏斜对联邦层次分类的负面影响，缺乏针对性的解决方案。

Method: 首先定义了标签粒度偏斜这一新问题，通过概率关系邻域分类器生成客户端特定的局部标签层次，并利用轮廓系数引导的粗化方法构建WordNet导向的层次结构。基于分析发现条件softmax分类器比强耦合层次模型更鲁棒，提出分支级解耦微调（BDFT）及其联邦版本（FedBDFT），对各分支分类器进行独立微调并通过联邦优化聚合。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet上的实验表明，FedBDFT在严重标签粒度偏斜下显著提升了鲁棒性，在偏斜度0.6和0.9时平均分别提升27.9%和56.4%。零样本结果进一步表明FedBDFT能更好地保留未见细粒度类别的层次表示。

Conclusion: FedBDFT有效解决了联邦层次分类中标签粒度异质性问题，证明了分支级解耦策略在处理不完整监督时的优越性，为联邦学习中的层级分类任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaboration across distributed devices without centralizing local data. However, clients may differ not only in data distributions but also in domain knowledge and annotation capabilities. In this paper, we introduce label granularity skew, a new form of statistical heterogeneity in federated hierarchical classification, in which clients provide taxonomy-consistent labels at different levels of detail within a shared class hierarchy. To model this heterogeneity, we generate client-specific local label hierarchies using a probabilistic relational neighbor classifier and construct a WordNet-guided hierarchy via silhouette score-based coarsening. Our analysis shows that strongly coupled hierarchical models are sensitive to incomplete supervision, while the conditional softmax classifier is more robust. Based on this insight, we propose Branch-wise Decoupled Fine-Tuning (BDFT) and its federated version, FedBDFT, which fine-tune branch-wise classifiers and aggregate them through federated optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet show that FedBDFT substantially improves robustness under severe label granularity skew, with average gains of 27.9% and 56.4% at skewness levels of 0.6 and 0.9, respectively. Zero-shot results further indicate that FedBDFT better preserves hierarchical representations for unseen fine-grained classes. These findings demonstrate its effectiveness for federated hierarchical classification with heterogeneous label granularities.

</details>


### [252] [Multimodal Federated Learning under Dual-Axis Modality Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09240)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Jaehyun Kwak,Jaemin Shin,Yunqi Guo,Xiaomin Ouyang,Guoliang Xing,Steven Euijong Whang,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux，一种针对多模态联邦学习中双轴模态缺失问题（客户端间模态集不同、样本内模态子集不同）的框架，通过模态感知置信度调节与梯度解耦私有适配两个互补组件，在四个多模态数据集上取得最优平均宏F1成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态联邦学习部署中普遍存在双轴模态缺失问题，而现有方法通常将这两个维度分开处理，缺乏统一方案。

Method: Flux框架包含两个组件：(1) 模态感知置信度调节——通过掩码感知的单模态监督学习每个模态的样本级置信度，并将观测模态的置信度估计融合为样本自适应温度，根据证据质量和完整性调整预测锐度；(2) 梯度解耦私有适配——该温度仅应用于客户端私有预测通路，共享联邦模型仍使用标准无温度调节的目标训练，从而避免置信度相关梯度干扰共享表征学习。

Result: 在四个多模态数据集上，Flux在每个数据集上均取得最高平均宏F1，比最强数据集特定基线高出0.8~2.2个百分点（平均高1.6个百分点）；额外分析显示其具有良好的校准性、温度对模态缺失和输入损坏的敏感性，以及在私有调节下共享优化更稳定。

Conclusion: Flux通过样本级、客户端局部的置信度自适应，在不干扰共享表征学习的前提下有效解决了双轴模态缺失问题，验证了私有温度调节策略的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Multimodal federated learning (FL) supports collaborative modeling in privacy-sensitive health-sensing and medical settings, but realistic deployments often exhibit dual-axis modality missingness: clients have different modality sets, and individual samples may contain only subsets of the modalities available locally. Existing methods typically address these two axes separately. We propose Flux, a multimodal federated learning framework built around two complementary components. First, modality-aware confidence tempering learns sample-specific confidence for each modality through mask-aware unimodal supervision and fuses the confidence estimates from observed modalities into a sample-adaptive temperature that adjusts predictive sharpness according to evidence quality and completeness. Second, gradient-decoupled private adaptation applies this temperature only to a client-private prediction pathway, while training the shared federated model with a standard, untempered objective. This enables sample-specific, client-local confidence adaptation without allowing confidence-dependent gradients to perturb shared representation learning. Across four multimodal datasets, Flux achieves the highest average macro-F1 on every dataset, outperforming the strongest dataset-specific baseline by 0.8~2.2 points and by 1.6 points on average. Additional analyses demonstrate favorable calibration, temperature sensitivity to both modality missingness and input corruption, and more stable shared optimization under private-only tempering. Our code is available at https://github.com/AdibaOrz/Flux.

</details>


### [253] [FEAST: Federated Shared-Space Training for Resource-Heterogeneous Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09250)
*Bostan Khan,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEAST，一种联邦共享空间训练框架，通过预算定制的子超网络路由和稀疏聚合，解决联邦学习中客户端计算能力异构导致的弹性模型训练不平衡问题，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要服务计算能力各异的设备，固定模型无法适配所有设备，而按部署场景逐一训练模型成本高昂。联邦超网络训练虽可学习弹性模型，但当客户端推理预算不同时，高成本子网络专属参数只能被少数客户端访问，导致训练不平衡。

Method: FEAST框架包含三个核心组件：1) 在每个客户端计算限制内联合训练多个子网络以平衡参数可达性；2) 预算定制的子超网络路由，仅发送相关的超网络部分；3) 稀疏聚合来合并返回的参数切片。此外提出γ分配协议解耦训练数据量与推理预算的耦合效应。

Result: 在596M推理MACs下FEAST达到71.06%准确率，比最强的模型异构权重共享基线高出2.4个百分点，而SuperFedNAS和DeepFedNAS在25M下接近随机水平、在596M时最多仅达17.09%。在CIFAR-100、CINIC-10和TinyImageNet-200上均取得最高人口平均准确率，子超网络路由将聚合模型参数流量减少6.8倍。

Conclusion: FEAST有效解决了联邦超网络训练中由异构推理预算引起的训练不平衡问题，训练出的超网络可直接服务联邦过程中使用的子网络，并支持无需联邦重训的后验提取额外子网络，为异构联邦学习提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) must serve devices with varying computational capabilities. A fixed model cannot suit all devices, while training one model per deployment limit is costly. Federated supernet training instead learns one elastic model with differently sized subnetworks, then deploys a suitable one to each device. When client inference budgets differ, however, parameters exclusive to high-cost subnetworks are reachable by fewer clients. We propose FEAST, a federated shared-space training framework that counters this imbalance by jointly training multiple subnetworks within each client's limit. Budget-tailored sub-supernet routing sends only the relevant supernet portion, and sparse aggregation merges the returned parameter slices. The trained supernet directly serves the subnetworks used during federation and supports post-hoc extraction of additional subnetworks without federated retraining. We further show that independently assigning clients' training-data volumes and inference budgets can distort accuracy--inference-cost comparisons in heterogeneous FL simulations, and introduce a one-parameter $γ$-allocation protocol to control this coupling. In our experimental setup, the SuperFedNAS and DeepFedNAS supernet training procedures remain near chance at 25M and reach at most $17.09\%$ at $596$M inference MACs; FEAST reaches $71.06\%$ at $596$M, $2.4$ points above the strongest model-heterogeneous weight-sharing baseline at its largest tier. Across CIFAR-100, CINIC-10, and TinyImageNet-200, FEAST achieves the highest population-averaged accuracy among the evaluated weight-sharing methods when each client receives its largest affordable subnetwork. Sub-supernet routing reduces aggregate model-parameter traffic by $6.8\times$ relative to full-supernet transmission.

</details>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [World Simulator: Queer Erotica and the Absurdity of AI Video Models That Promise the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07510)
*Adam Cole,Mick Grierson*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一件名为"World Simulator"的视频装置艺术作品，该作品通过将同性色情内容输入AI视频生成模型，利用模型因训练数据过滤而无法识别这些图像、从而产生超现实幻觉的特性，批判了AI视频模型自称"世界模拟器"的夸大宣传，并探讨了感官表征的更多可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频模型被广泛宣称为"世界模拟器"，声称能模拟无限现实，但这些模型系统性地排除了人类具身体验的重要方面（尤其是性），作者旨在揭示这一矛盾。

Method: 采用视频装置艺术的形式，将露骨的男同性恋色情内容输入AI视频到视频转换管线中，利用模型因缺乏相关训练数据而产生的幻觉现象，生成超现实替代画面（如厨房电器、奇异建筑、抽象肉身）。

Result: 作品展示了AI模型如何将亲密行为转化为平庸的日常场景，直观地可视化了合成知识的局限性，挑战了"世界模拟器"这一称谓的傲慢。

Conclusion: 作者不仅批判了AI模型声称能模拟世界却对具身（尤其是性的）体验结构性失明的问题，还进一步追问模拟本身的价值，提出更具扩展性和肯定生命意义的感官表征形式可能存在的假设。

Abstract: Increasingly, AI video models are marketed as "world simulators," suggesting their ability to model infinite realities. Despite such claims, these models systematically exclude significant aspects of embodied human experience, particularly sexuality. World Simulator is a video installation exploring the poetic friction between these universal claims and the models' inherent blindness. To do so, the work feeds explicit gay erotica into an AI video-to-video pipeline. Lacking the training data to recognize these images, the system hallucinates surreal alternatives, transforming intimate acts into banal scenes of kitchen appliances, strange architectures, and abstract flesh. By visualizing the limits of synthetic knowledge, the work challenges the hubris of the "world simulator" label, asking how a system, trained primarily on large filtered video datasets, can claim to simulate the world while remaining structurally blind to the body. Beyond this critique, we question the value of simulation itself, asking what forms of sensual representation might offer more expansive, life-affirming possibilities.

</details>


### [255] [SAMOT: State-Aware Step Modulation and Optimal Transport Matching for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08147)
*Kai Peng,Yunzhe Shen,Miao Zhang,Leiye Liu,Wei Ji,Jingjing Li,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于音视频实例分割（AVIS）的新框架，通过自适应动态步长调制（ADSM）和基于最优传输的匹配调制（OT-MM）分别解决长序列建模中模态状态变化适应性问题以及跨模态实例匹配中的分布不一致问题，在AVIS基准上取得了最先进的性能（+3.76 FSLA, +2.75 HOTA, +2.58 mAP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVIS任务中，现有方法（固定步长Transformer和递归Mamba模型）缺乏对模态状态变化的适应性，同时隐式匹配方法忽略了跨模态之间的分布不一致性，这两大问题制约了长视频序列中实例级建模的精度。

Method: 提出双模块框架：(1) ADSM自适应调制Mamba步长，利用时间变化、跨模态差异和历史上下文来平衡快速响应与稳定建模；(2) OT-MM将实例级跨模态匹配显式建模为熵正则化的最优传输问题，通过log域Sinkhorn迭代求解，并引入MMD正则化器增强分布级一致性。

Result: 在AVIS基准上取得最先进性能，FSLA提升3.76，HOTA提升2.75，mAP提升2.58，并通过全面的定性可视化验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ADSM和OT-MM分别有效解决了模态状态变化适应性和跨模态分布不一致性问题，验证了自适应调制与显式最优传输匹配在音视频实例分割中的优越性。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation (AVIS) aims to simultaneously classify, segment, and track sounding objects within video sequences. Unlike Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (AVS), AVIS involves instance-level modeling across longer video sequences, introducing two key challenges: (1) complex modality-state changes disrupt long-range modeling, and (2) substantial structural and distributional discrepancies between modalities hinder precise instance-level association. Existing methods rely on fixed-step Transformers and recursive Mamba models, lacking adaptability to modality-state changes. In addition, methods performing implicit matching ignore the inherent distributional inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a framework with Adaptive Dynamic Step Modulation (ADSM) and Optimal Transport-based Matching Modulation (OT-MM). ADSM adaptively modulates Mamba step sizes using temporal variation, cross-modal discrepancy, and historical context, balancing rapid response to modality-state changes with stable long-range modeling. OT-MM explicitly formulates instance-level cross-modal matching as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem solved via log-domain Sinkhorn iterations, and further enforces distribution-level coherence with an MMD regularizer. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the AVIS benchmark (+3.76 FSLA, +2.75 HOTA, +2.58 mAP), verified through comprehensive qualitative visualizations. The code and model are available at https://github.com/happylife-pk/SAMOT.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将计算论证作为评估性AI（EAI）的形式化基础，通过呈现竞争性假设及其正反证据来支持人类决策，旨在建立可解释、可质疑的分布式人本EAI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EAI仅输出单一推荐，缺乏对竞争假设的多角度呈现；需要一种既能形式化计算又支持可解释性和可质疑性的范式来支撑EAI的发展。

Method: 作为立场论文（position paper），作者主张采用（计算）论证作为EAI的形式化、可计算的基石，并据此提出一个面向分布式和人本EAI系统的长期研究议程。

Result: 论文并未给出具体的实验结果，而是提出了一种概念框架和研究方向，论证了计算论证范式对EAI可解释性和可质疑性目标的适用性。

Conclusion: 计算论证是支撑可解释、可质疑的EAI系统的合适范式，为未来构建分布式、以人为中心的EAI系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [257] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出人类监督AI在高损失领域存在结构性瓶颈：核心约束是AI输出速度V与每项认知负荷L的乘积（V×L），而非单纯的速度V。L由分诊、判断和响应三部分构成，其中只有判断成本会随AI能力提升而下降，且这种下降往往通过省略而非真正降低实现。为此提出"逐流控制"（Flow-by-Flow）治理范式，通过基于形式化可计数特征的非线性成本函数和机构容量上限来管理监督负荷，并推导出四个设计不变量。蒙特卡洛模拟显示该方法在90.8%的试验中优于单纯监督强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究表明在高损失领域，当AI输出速度V超过人类认知容量C_max时，人类参与监督在结构上不可行。但作者指出真正的约束是V×L而非V本身，且现有治理机制（无论是委托AI自我评估还是人类评估）都存在根本缺陷：前者继承幻觉风险，后者受限于V×L天花板。因此需要一种不依赖内容评估的新治理范式。

Method: 提出Flow-by-Flow治理范式：1) 基于形式化、可计数特征（而非语义内容）构建认知成本评分，对高产量生产施加非线性成本；2) 设置机构容量上限，将处理量控制在C_max以内。推导出四个设计不变量：无内容判断、不可规模化消耗检查者容量、身份绑定的逐应用摩擦、无批量清关。并通过蒙特卡洛分析（1000次参数抽取）比较复合多指标流控与单纯监督强化。

Result: 理论推导表明四个设计不变量在参考实现中可联合满足（同时坦诚承认实际困难）。蒙特卡洛分析显示，在1000次参数抽取中，复合多指标流控制在90.8%的试验中优于单纯监督强化方案。

Conclusion: AI能力提升不会降低总体认知负荷L，而是重新结构化L（分诊成本不变、响应成本不变、判断成本可能通过省略而"下降"）。因此治理应从"评估AI输出是否正确"转向"控制监督流程负荷"，Flow-by-Flow提供了这一转变的设计框架。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [258] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，用于从多元结构理论中构建规范解释，区分了三种规范化层次（闭包稳定、全局完备、确定性），并将非确定性分类为认知多元性（Type E）和结构多元性（Type S），提出了基于算子和基于选择器的两种规范化机制，并探讨了其在LLM辅助推理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构理论缺乏统一的数学框架来描述规范化过程，非确定性的来源和分类不够清晰，无法系统地区分不同类型的非确定性及其对应的规范化策略。

Method: 引入了结构理论三元组T=(Σ,A,I)的形式化定义；区分三种规范化层次（闭包稳定、全局完备、确定性）；将非确定性分为认知多元性（Type E）和结构多元性（Type S），并提出Type S-strong子类；提出了两种规范化机制：基于算子的完备化和基于选择器的构造；通过多级规范化算子系统展示其结构性非交换性。

Result: 提供了两种机制存在的充分结构条件；证明了在正向、非回溯规则下且满足可靠性条件时，纯基于推理的完备化可归结为饱和闭包算子；对Type E理论建立了闭包稳定性，完全确定性取决于尚未解决的全局合流性质；对Type S-strong理论通过规范选择实现了确定性；建立了条件分类定理将理论内在机制归结为闭包或选择。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一套完整的工具来分析和构建规范解释，揭示了多级规范化的非交换结构性质，并成功将框架应用于LLM辅助推理，将幻觉视为不受支持的规范化现象。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [259] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在连续状态空间的主动推理驾驶模型中扩展情绪（效价和唤醒度）表示的框架，通过将情感估计不仅基于当前状态还基于预测的未来结果，在两个交互式驾驶场景中验证了情感信号与真实场景中的情感模式相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动推理驾驶模型忽略了情感状态这一影响交通决策的重要因素。虽然非交通领域已有基于circumplex模型（效价和唤醒度）的主动推理智能体研究，但仅限于离散状态空间的简化设置，无法应用于更复杂的连续状态驾驶场景。

Method: 提出一种扩展的效价和唤醒度公式，可从具有连续状态的复杂主动推理驾驶模型中提取情感估计。关键在于情感估计不仅基于当前状态，还考虑了预测的未来结果（即对前瞻性结果的评判）。

Result: 在两个交互式驾驶场景中进行了评估，结果显示所提出的情感信号与相似场景中报告的真实情感模式相对应，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将情感建模从离散简化场景拓展到连续状态的复杂驾驶场景，使主动推理框架能够更全面地模拟真实驾驶中的情感-决策交互过程。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [260] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据驱动的并行（DCP）方法，通过让数据本身驱动运行时，根据每个批次的序列长度动态调整并行大小、梯度累积和重计算等运行时设置，以打破变长序列深度学习训练中效率与易用性之间的权衡。在32块H200 GPU上最高实现2.88倍加速，且仅需10行代码即可集成到任何模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变长序列上的深度学习训练存在计算挑战，现有方法在效率和易用性之间存在难以调和的权衡：简单的静态配置会导致工作负载不均衡和低效率，而复杂的方法则会带来显著的实现复杂性和代码变更。

Method: DCP的核心原则是让数据本身驱动运行时，通过根据每个批次的实际序列长度动态调整直接运行时设置（如并行大小、梯度累积、重计算），实现工作负载的自适应均衡，从而不需要复杂的代码改动即可优化训练效率。

Result: 在32块H200 GPU上最高实现2.88倍加速，且该方法具有良好的泛化能力，任何模型仅需10行代码即可完成集成。

Conclusion: 这种简单而有效的方法有望作为稳健的基线，为分布式训练中变长序列的后续研究提供基础，推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [261] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 线性探针能以接近完美的准确率检测语言模型中的上下文损坏，但这并不能可靠地预测最终答案是否正确。研究揭示了"知道但不表述"的解离现象，表明探针监测只能作为口头置信度的必要补充，而没有单一干预手段能全面占优，部署时需要进行模型感知和错误类型感知的路由选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性探针虽能高精度检测模型中的上下文损坏，但其是否能转化为实际的下游错误预测尚不清楚。这种解离对部署监控有直接影响，因此需要系统研究探针检测能力与实际错误预测之间的差距。

Method: 在多跳算术链任务上，采用预注册的"持久性胜于峰值"假设，对多模型家族（包括推理模型）进行系统评估。测试了多种干预手段：branch-and-pick、reprompt和replace-prior，并比较了探针检测到的损坏与其对最终答案正确性的预测能力。

Result: 探针能近乎完美地检测上下文损坏，但对最终答案正确性的预测毫无信息量。结构化置信格式下模型塌缩为两个错误率无差异的值。预注册的"持久性胜于峰值"假设被证伪。干预效果因模型和错误类型而异：branch-and-pick在所有模型上净收益为正且对Llama-3.1-8B唯一不具破坏性（4修复、0破坏），而reprompt和replace-prior在修复错误答案的同时也会破坏正确答案。

Conclusion: 探针监测是口头化置信度的必要补充，但没有任何单一干预手段全面占优。实际部署应采用模型感知、错误类型感知的路由策略。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [262] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NL2SHACL-Bench，这是首个专门用于评估自然语言到SHACL翻译任务的基准测试套件，并利用该基准评估了四个大语言模型的表现。结果显示现有LLM能够生成语法正确的SHACL，但在复杂逻辑和结构模式的语义等价约束方面仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL是验证RDF知识图谱一致性的核心技术，但编写SHACL形状需要专业技术知识，领域专家往往缺乏这种能力。将自然语言需求翻译成SHACL（NL2SHACL）可以降低这一门槛，但目前没有专门的基准测试，且评估生成的形状需要超越字符串比较的方法，因为语义等价的形状可能在序列化和结构上有所不同。

Method: 论文构建了NL2SHACL-Bench基准套件，专门用于自然语言到SHACL翻译任务的评估。该基准考虑了语义等价性而非仅字符串匹配。作者使用该基准对四个最先进的大语言模型进行了系统评估。

Result: 四个最先进的LLM在生成语法有效的SHACL方面表现出色，但在生成复杂逻辑和结构模式的语义等价约束方面仍然存在困难。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench为衡量NL2SHACL领域的技术进展提供了有意义的基础，同时揭示了当前LLM在语义等价约束生成方面的局限性，指明了未来改进的方向。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [263] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体选择与通信建模为条件任务下的合作博弈，通过边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器优化智能体选择与通信边，在合成实验中达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，同时平均仅激活8个中的1.96个智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多智能体LLM系统要么预先固定通信结构，要么允许全广播通信，两者都导致token成本、延迟、冗余和错误传播随活跃智能体和通信链路数量增加而急剧上升，需要更高效的选择与通信机制。

Method: 将智能体选择和通信建模为带任务条件净效用的合作博弈，提出边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器；扩展模型以优化带边成本的通信边；使用估计的Shapley值预测执行前和执行期间值得联系的智能体；并将其与子模最大化建立联系，给出曲率精化界、1/2近似界和Shapley-子模三明治界。

Result: 贪心路由在合成实验中达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均激活8个中的1.96个智能体，而全广播仅达38.8%；性能对激活成本和冗余权重鲁棒，但在子模性被严重违背或价值估计有噪声时降至66%。
