Evaluated on our benchmark, Qwen3-VL-4B trained with POISE achieves an overall reward score of 77.96%, nearly matching GPT-5-mini (78.09%, gap 0.13%) and outperforming the strongest RoboReward-4B baseline by 10.13%. It also lifts test-time score-pair consistency to 71.90%, exceeding RoboReward-4B (57.26%) and GPT-5-mini (68.09%). Integrating TrustJudge aggregation during inference boosts the overall score to 78.57%, surpassing the GPT-5-mini teacher model.

</details>


### [364] [MathShikkha: A Controlled Study of Answer-Only and Chain-of-Thought Supervision for Bangla Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08503)
*Rahma Simin Ali,Jawad Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了低资源语言（孟加拉语）中教师生成思维链（CoT）监督是否优于普通监督微调。通过构建MathShikkha数据集，在受控实验下发现：CoT在域内推理上不显著优于仅答案微调（除弱模型外），但在域外基准上显著提升（20.1-28.1分），并提升目标语言遵循性和推理可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理在孟加拉语等低资源语言中仍然具有挑战性，现有研究未明确CoT监督在低资源语言中相对普通监督微调（仅答案微调）是否带来额外收益，需要严格匹配协议下的实证研究。

Method: 构建包含GPT-5.4生成推理过程的孟加拉语数学推理数据集MathShikkha；在匹配协议下（共享数据分割、响应掩码、解码和评分）微调四个4B-7B学生模型，仅改变训练目标（仅答案 vs CoT）；在域内集和污染审计后的BanglaMATH基准上评估；进行人评研究（Cohen's κ=0.76-1.00）。

Result: 域内评估中CoT对三个较强骨干模型无显著提升（CI包含零，p≥0.17），但对较弱4B模型显著提升18.56分。域外BanglaMATH上CoT对全部四个模型显著超越仅答案微调（20.1-28.1分）。仅答案微调导致三个模型的域外精度低于基座模型，而CoT保持或改善全部模型。人评未发现CoT在推理内容上优于基座模型，其可测效应是目标语言遵循和可检查推理。

Conclusion: 思维链监督的价值取决于骨干能力与分布偏移：在本场景中，其主要收益是孟加拉语遵循、可审计推理和域外鲁棒性，而非改善域内推理有效性。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning remains challenging in low-resource languages such as Bangla. We study whether teacher-generated Bangla Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision provides benefits beyond ordinary supervised fine-tuning. We construct \textsc{MathShikkha}, a Bangla mathematical reasoning dataset with GPT-5.4-generated rationales, and fine-tune four 4B--7B student models under a matched protocol in which answer-only and CoT conditions share data splits, response-only loss masking, decoding, and scoring, differing only in the training target. In-domain, CoT provides no significant improvement over answer-only fine-tuning for three stronger backbones (paired bootstrap 95\% CIs include zero; exact McNemar $p \geq 0.17$), despite generating 15--52$\times$ more tokens, but significantly improves the weaker 4B model by 18.56 points ($p < 0.0001$). On the larger, contamination-audited BanglaMATH benchmark, this pattern reverses: CoT significantly outperforms answer-only supervision for all four models by 20.1--28.1 points (all $p < 0.0001$). Answer-only fine-tuning also reduces out-of-domain accuracy below the base model for three models, whereas CoT preserves or improves it for all four. A human study with two co-author annotators, external-expert adjudication, and Cohen's $κ= 0.76$--$1.00$ finds no significant CoT improvement over the base model on reasoning-content criteria; instead, its measurable effect is target-language adherence and producing inspectable reasoning. Overall, rationale supervision's value depends on backbone capability and distribution shift: in this setting, its main benefits are Bangla adherence, auditable reasoning, and out-of-domain robustness rather than improved in-domain reasoning validity.

</details>


### [365] [Understanding Calibration and Truncation Error Propagation in Training-Free Low-Rank Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08506)
*Mohanad Odema,Gabrielle De Micheli,Dayin Gou,Nilesh Malpeddi,Prathamesh Vaste,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种免训练的低秩压缩框架改进方法，解决了现有SOTA框架中校准数据激活误差跨层累积和层重要性分布假设失效两个关键问题，通过逐层压缩校准校正和迭代压缩秩分配校正，在Llama和Qwen3模型上实现1-2.5个准确率点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练低秩压缩框架存在两个关键缺陷：(1)压缩时校准数据激活的残差误差跨层累积，导致压缩时模拟的表示与推理时实际经历的表示不一致；(2)压缩后层重要性分布保持不变的假设不成立。这两个问题共同导致压缩过程与部署模型之间存在错位，影响压缩效果。

Method: 提出了两个简单且免训练的方法，可兼容现有框架：(1)逐层压缩与校准校正(Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction)，解决激活误差跨层累积问题；(2)迭代压缩与秩分配校正(Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction)，修正层重要性分布假设失效的问题。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen3模型上，跨多个基准测试和压缩率评估，相比逐权重和联合分解基线，在零样本任务上实现了约1-2.5个准确率点的提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别并解决压缩过程中的两个关键错位来源（激活误差累积和秩分配失真），该研究为免训练低秩压缩提供了简单有效的改进方案，且无需额外训练即可兼容现有压缩框架。

Abstract: Training-free low-rank compression frameworks have been gaining prominence for LLM compression given their effectiveness in reducing model parameter count while maintaining task-level accuracy. However, existing SOTA frameworks share two key limitations: (1) residual errors in calibration data activations accumulate across layers during compression, causing misalignment between representations simulated at compression time and those experienced at inference; (2) the assumption that layer importance distribution is preserved post-compression does not hold. Together, these two effects introduce misalignment in the compression process in relation to the deployed model. We study these effects and propose a simple, training-free methodology compatible with existing frameworks to mitigate them, comprising: (1) Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction; (2) Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction. Implemented atop an existing SOTA decomposition framework, and evaluated on Llama and Qwen3 models across various benchmarks and compression rates, our approach demonstrates up to ~1-2.5 accuracy point improvements over per-weight and joint decomposition baselines on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [366] [Time Present and Time Past: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Temporally Evolving Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08512)
*Mahbub E Sobhani,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Fahmid Hasan Chowdhury,Md Adnan Arefeen,Farig Sadeque,Md. Faizul Bari,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Evolving documents, such as laws, tax codes, and software documentation, are amended, replaced, and sometimes reverted over time, so a question has different correct answers at different dates. In contrast to encyclopedic knowledge, where an old fact is simply overwritten, an amendment is itself an official text that states what it replaces and when it takes effect, and the earlier version stays correct for its validity period. The central challenge is therefore version resolution, that is, identifying the version in force on the queried date. Existing temporal QA datasets treat time only as an annotation, so version resolution stays untested. We present TIDE, an expert-verified benchmark of 3,050 QA pairs over 644 official customs instruments issued between 1969 and 2025 by the Government of Bangladesh, covering eight task types over deeply code-mixed documents that are heterogeneous in layout and dated in two calendars. In addition, we evaluate nine recent LLMs under a single protocol across parametric, gold-context, and retrieval access, scored by a three-judge LLM council with a hard date gate separating correct meaning from correct time. The best macro-averaged accuracy is only 68.5%. Resolving a version from an implicit date reaches 59.7%, and detecting that the supplied version does not govern the query reaches only 26.7%. Models are more likely to find correct versions than to reject incorrect ones, and they tend to follow a confident parametric answer over the supplied authoritative text. All code and data are available at https://github.com/icsetepa44/TIDE

</details>


### [367] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了两种提升LLM推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），并在多个新任务领域和模型中进行了压力测试。结果显示：RPC在原始推理路径上能精确复现原论文结果，但在四个新领域上相对自洽性的优势从未达到统计显著；LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但较弱，其正效应不显著，且在三个模型中的两个上反而显著降低了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种方法的原始评估均由作者自己完成，从未被独立复现或跨模型、跨领域进行压力测试；此外LCF没有公开代码。验证这些可靠性声明至关重要，因为未经独立验证的方法可能在实际应用中表现不如预期。

Method: 重新运行RPC的已发布路径聚合方法，重新实现LCF的投影器、对比学习和干预流程。将两种方法扩展到四个新任务领域（text-to-SQL、法律抽取、谬误识别、先例分级），并使用多种模型（Qwen3-8B用于RPC，四种7-8B模型用于LCF），同时对LCF的表示进行直接探测。

Result: RPC精确复现了作者发布的推理路径上的原始网格结果，但在四个新领域中其相对自洽性的优势从未显著（配对p≥0.28）；在BIRD领域，K增大时优势增强的预测部分成立，但扩大样本后最大优势反转。LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但较弱（0.82可分性vs对照的0.95），其唯一正效应不显著（p=0.56），且在三个模型中的两个上显著降低了性能。

Conclusion: 独立复现揭示了两方法的可靠性声明均存在明显夸大：RPC的优势在跨域泛化时不显著，LCF的效果在跨模型迁移时不仅微弱甚至有害。这强调了独立复现和跨域压力测试对验证方法声明的重要性，尤其是当方法由原作者自行评估且缺乏公开代码时。

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


### [368] [Discovering Diverse Planning Policies for Multimodal Embodied Agents with Quality-Diversity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08523)
*Pengfei Xu,Yong Liu,Xiaoya Nan,Qiang Yang,Peilan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于质量-多样性（QD）框架的方法，用于为多模态具身智能体发现多样化的规划策略，通过建立行为索引的策略档案库，在检测到执行停滞时切换策略以支持自适应规划和在线故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大模型的多模态规划器在长时程任务执行中通常依赖于单一主导规划风格，一旦该风格失效，智能体可能长时间停滞不前，反复与环境交互却无法取得有意义进展。

Method: 将规划策略模板视为可进化的个体，组织成行为索引档案库。离线阶段：将 rollout 轨迹总结为结构化的成功与失败经验，指导策略变异（重组和经验引导突变），并按交互强度和目标导向性映射到行为空间，每个生态位保留最高质量的策略。在线阶段：每次执行一个策略并监控任务进展，检测到持续停滞时回滚到最近检查点并切换至行为上不同的档案策略继续执行。

Result: 在 ThreeDWorld 运输基准上的实验表明，所提出的框架相较于代表性基线规划器，在任务成功率和交互效率两方面均取得了改进。

Conclusion: 发现多样化的策略库是支持自适应多模态规划和在线故障恢复的有效途径，为长时程具身任务中执行模式失效的问题提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal embodied agents are increasingly required to solve long-horizon tasks by integrating visual observations, textual goals, and interaction history into closed-loop decision making. However, state-of-the-art large-model-based planners often rely on a single dominant planning style during execution. Once this execution mode becomes ineffective, the agent may remain stalled for many steps, repeatedly interacting with the environment without making meaningful progress. We address this limitation by proposing a Quality-Diversity (QD) framework for discovering diverse planning policies for multimodal embodied agents. The proposed method treats planning-policy templates as evolvable individuals and organizes them into a behavior-indexed archive rather than collapsing search to a single prompt style. In the offline stage, rollout trajectories are summarized into structured success and failure experiences, which guide policy variation through recombination and experience-guided mutation. The resulting policies are mapped into a behavior space defined by interaction intensity and goal-directedness, and the highest-quality policy in each niche is retained in the archive. In the online stage, the agent executes one policy at a time while monitoring task progress. When persistent stall is detected, the system rolls back to the latest checkpoint and switches to a behaviorally distinct archive policy to resume execution. Experiments on the ThreeDWorld transport benchmark show that the proposed framework improves both task success and interaction efficiency over representative baseline planners. These results suggest that discovering diverse policy repertoires is an effective way to support adaptive multimodal planning and online failure recovery.

</details>


### [369] [Deep probabilistic logic programming for diagnostic reasoning from incomplete information: A case study in stroke detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08561)
*Felix Weitkämper,Monchito Avila,Elizabeth Nanjala,Siska,Grace Zawadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一个从文献摘要统计信息到DeepProbLog神经符号诊断系统的完整工作流程，以脑卒中多模态检测为案例研究，利用最大熵技术完善概率信息，并通过ProbLog 2从因果模型转向判别式神经符号模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学应用中原始数据涉及隐私问题，需要从文献中的摘要统计信息进行编码；同时深度学习虽擅长分析视觉/听觉传感器数据，但缺乏透明性和可解释性，亟需结合符号推理的神经符号方法。

Method: 采用DeepProbLog框架整合连接主义组件进行患者图像分析，置于概率逻辑编程（分布语义）的透明严格框架中；使用最大熵技术补全文献中不完整的概率信息，通过ProbLog 2从熵最大化因果模型转化为DeepProbLog可表达的判别式神经符号模型。

Result: 比较了从较不完整数据推导出的模型性能，分析了ProbFOIL 2在压缩大型判别式模型方面的潜力，并讨论了DeepProbLog用于诊断推理的视角与含义。

Conclusion: 本文展示了从文献统计摘要到可解释的神经符号诊断系统的可行路径，DeepProbLog为医学诊断推理提供了透明且严格的概率框架，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In medical applications, raw data is frequently associated with significant privacy concerns, lending particular importance to the encoding of summary statistics from the literature. On the other hand, deep learning has become an invaluable tool for assessing symptoms based on visual or auditory sensor data. DeepProbLog allows for an extensible neuro-symbolic approach that accommodates connectionist components to analyse patient images within a transparent and rigorous probabilistic framework, namely probabilistic logic programming under the distribution semantics. Framed as a case study in stroke detection from multimodal data, this contribution explores the pathway from summary statistics available in the literature to a DeepProbLog-based diagnostic system. It suggests a workflow using established maximum entropy techniques to complete available probabilistic information and the probabilistic logic programming system ProbLog 2 to move from the entropy-maximising causal model to a discriminative neuro-symbolic model expressible within DeepProbLog. The relative performance of models derived from less complete data is analysed alongside the potential of the probabilistic inductive logic programming system ProbFOIL 2 for compressing large discriminative models, and the perspectives and implications of using DeepProbLog for diagnostic reasoning are discussed.

</details>


### [370] [VoxZip: Semantic-Anchored Temporal KV Cache Compression for Long-Context Audio Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08569)
*Wenxu Jia,Dongjie Fu,Xize Cheng,Fangming Feng,Linjun Li,Wenshi Chen,Yingming Li,Zhou Zhao,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VoxZip是一种无需训练的、两阶段语义锚定KV缓存压缩框架，用于解决语音大语言模型在长上下文推理中KV缓存内存需求过大的瓶颈问题。它利用ASR转录文本作为语义锚点来压缩音频token，并结合基于时间衰减累积注意力的动态过滤策略进一步减少非必要token，在20倍压缩比下仍能保持90%以上的基线性能，同时显著提升推理吞吐量并降低峰值内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音大语言模型在长上下文推理中面临KV缓存内存需求过大的严重瓶颈，现有文本中心压缩方法无法有效处理音频数据，容易破坏语音连续性或丢失关键语义信息。

Method: VoxZip采用两阶段框架：第一阶段利用ASR转录文本作为显式语义锚点，对音频token进行时间对齐、压缩与融合，减少初始KV缓存并提升token信息密度；第二阶段采用基于时间衰减累积注意力的动态过滤策略，剔除非必要token并缓解早期token偏差问题。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-Omni上跨六个音频基准测试中，VoxZip在长音频推理任务中表现优异，短时任务上保持高保真感知能力；在20倍KV缓存压缩比下仍维持基线90%以上的性能；在4倍压缩比下实现1.9倍推理吞吐量提升和3.3倍峰值内存开销降低。

Conclusion: VoxZip通过语义锚定与动态过滤的结合，实现了高效且无损的语音LLM KV缓存压缩，为语音大模型的长上下文部署提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Speech Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding complex audio tasks. Despite this progress, their long-context inference remains severely bottlenecked by prohibitive KV cache memory demands. Existing text-centric compression methods struggle here, often disrupting speech continuity or discarding crucial semantic cues. To address this, we propose VoxZip, a train-free, two-stage semantic-anchored KV cache compression framework. The first stage uses automatic speech recognition (ASR) transcriptions as explicit semantic anchors to temporally align, compress, and fuse audio tokens, significantly reducing the initial KV cache while elevating token information density. To further improve the compression ratio, the second stage employs a dynamic filtering strategy based on temporally decayed accumulated attention to evict non-essential tokens while mitigating early-token bias. Comprehensive evaluations on Qwen3-Omni across six diverse audio benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach. VoxZip excels in long-audio reasoning and consistently maintains high-fidelity perception on short-form tasks. Notably, it sustains over 90\% of the uncompressed baseline performance even under an aggressive 20x KV cache compression in long-context scenarios. Furthermore, at a 4x compression ratio, VoxZip yields a 1.9x increase in inference throughput alongside a 3.3x reduction in peak memory overhead. Code and models will be available at https://github.com/MM-Speech/VoxZip.

</details>


### [371] [FailForge: Distilling Procedural Competence from Persistent Failures into Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08570)
*Dongyi Lv,Fushun E,Aichen Cai,Liang Huang,Ya Zhang,Qiuyu Ding,Canhui Wu,Zhi Wang,Yuesong Zhang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FailForge是一个将失败轨迹转化为训练信号的智能体框架。它通过诊断失败原因、提炼可操作技能并引导二次尝试，成功恢复了超过26%的原本失败实例，并将Qwen3.5-4B在SWE-bench Verified上的解决率提升了6.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准拒绝采样微调（RFT）在训练代码智能体时，会直接丢弃无法通过测试的失败轨迹。然而这些被丢弃的样本恰恰是最困难、信息量最大的数据，且来自昂贵的人工筛选实例。即使更强的基座模型也只能减少失败数量，剩余困难案例仍是提升的前沿。

Method: FailForge采用代理式框架：对于每个失败实例，智能体从错误反馈和执行轨迹中诊断失败原因，将诊断提炼为简洁可操作的技能，并将技能注入智能体上下文以引导二次尝试。引导下成功的轨迹会被回收到RFT语料中。关键设计是：技能在训练时被移除，使模型内化恢复的行为而非依赖推理时的外部提示。

Result: FailForge以极低的额外成本恢复了超过26%的原本失败实例。将Qwen3.5-4B在增强语料上训练后，SWE-bench Verified解决率相比强RFT基线提升了6.6个百分点，且提升集中在最困难的问题上。

Conclusion: 通过将失败的强化学习轨迹转化为训练信号，可以有效突破标准RFT的天花板，尤其是在困难问题上的表现提升显著。该框架验证了"失败即宝藏"的理念在代码智能体训练中的可行性。

Abstract: Rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) is widely used to train code agents by generating trajectories on verifiable software engineering tasks, retaining those that pass the tests, and fine-tuning on the successful rollouts. However, even strong code agents repeatedly fail on a substantial fraction of such tasks, and standard RFT simply discards these failures. The discarded samples are precisely the hardest and most informative ones, drawn from verifiable instances that are costly to curate. Stronger base models may reduce the number of failures, but the remaining hard cases still define the frontier for further improvement. We propose FailForge, an agentic framework that converts failed rollouts into training signal. For each failed instance, an agent diagnoses the failure from error feedback and execution traces, distills the diagnosis into a concise and actionable skill, and injects the skill into the agent context for a guided second attempt. Trajectories that succeed under skill guidance are folded back into the RFT corpus. Crucially, the skill is removed at training time, so the model internalizes the recovered behavior rather than relying on external hints at inference. FailForge recovers over 26% of previously failed instances at marginal additional cost, and training Qwen3.5-4B on the augmented corpus improves the SWE-bench Verified resolve rate by 6.6 points over a strong RFT baseline, with gains concentrated on the hardest problems.

</details>


### [372] [SDDBMs: Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08594)
*Shiyi Qi,Kun He,Mingmou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出软去噪扩散桥模型（SDDBMs），通过将扩散桥的终端约束从硬性精确匹配松弛为高斯软约束，解决了现有扩散桥模型中终端边界奇异性问题，并在图像恢复任务上取得了更优的数值稳定性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散桥模型依赖硬性终端条件约束，强制终端状态精确匹配目标分布，导致终端定律退化为Dirac测度，且终端附近的漂移系数病态化，产生数值不稳定和生成质量下降的问题。

Method: SDDBMs直接在终端约束层面引入正则化：在变换路径测度下规定非退化的高斯终端边缘分布（含灵活的终端中心和方差），并基于此构建软桥的完整闭式框架，包括高斯终端重加权、软h函数、高斯前向边缘分布以及免于x0的动力学。

Result: 理论分析表明SDDBMs提供了统一的概率视角，将现有扩散桥模型（DDBMs、GOUB、UniDB）作为特定参数下的特例涵盖在内。大量图像恢复实验表明，SDDBMs在数值稳定性和生成质量上均优于现有基于桥的方法。

Conclusion: SDDBMs通过软终端约束有效缓解了扩散桥的边界奇异性问题，为扩散桥模型提供了更一般化的框架，在理论统一性和实际性能上都具有显著优势。

Abstract: Diffusion bridge models leverage Doob's \(h\)-transform to construct stochastic transports between arbitrary endpoint distributions, and have shown strong potential in image-to-image translation and restoration. However, most existing bridge models rely on hard endpoint conditioning, which forces the terminal state to match a prescribed target exactly. This hard constraint induces terminal-boundary singularities: the terminal law collapses to a Dirac measure, and the resulting drift coefficients become ill-conditioned near the endpoint. In this paper, we propose Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models (SDDBMs), a generalized framework that regularizes diffusion bridges directly at the level of their terminal constraints. Instead of imposing an exact endpoint, SDDBMs prescribe a non-degenerate Gaussian terminal marginal under the transformed path measure, with a flexible terminal center and variance. Starting from this prescribed marginal, we develop a complete closed-form construction of the soft bridge, including the Gaussian terminal reweighting and soft \(h\)-function, the induced Gaussian forward marginals and \(\mathbf{x}_0\)-free dynamics. Theoretically, SDDBMs provide a unified probabilistic perspective that encompasses existing diffusion bridge models, including DDBMs, GOUB, and UniDB, as special cases under specific parameter choices. Extensive experiments on image restoration tasks demonstrate that SDDBMs achieve improved numerical stability and superior generation quality over existing bridge-based methods.

</details>


### [373] [Unaccountable Delegation, Fading Skills: Mapping the Risks of Workplace AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08601)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Lakmal Meegahapola,Edyta Bogucka,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Elizabeth Black,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多层级AI智能体风险分类框架，基于O*NET数据库中2,078项工作任务生成8,356个风险场景，并扩展出15类工作场所AI智能体风险分类体系。研究发现：增强模式并非天然安全（可能导致技能侵蚀）；错误智能体行为占比最高且严重风险集中；自动化主要带来组织风险，增强主要带来工人风险；工人偏好该分类体系，认为其比现有其他分类更易用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险分类体系侧重广义风险，未覆盖AI智能体在具体工作岗位中引入的特定风险。为填补这一空白，需要构建能捕捉岗位级AI智能体风险的分类体系，以帮助组织预判AI智能体带来的社会技术风险。

Method: 首先通过文献综述开发多层级框架，建模智能体、目标、环境三大核心组件及其交互；随后将该框架嵌入结构化提示词，应用于O*NET数据库中2,078项工作任务描述，生成8,356个风险场景并按严重程度和部署模式（自动化或增强）标注；再用45名覆盖10个岗位的工人和独立LLM裁判验证场景合理性；最后扩展现有分类体系形成15类工作场所AI智能体风险分类。

Result: 发现四项关键结论：1）增强模式并非固有安全，过度依赖会侵蚀工人技能和监督；2）错误智能体行为占比最大且严重风险最集中，多发生在人机边界；3）自动化主要关联组织风险，增强主要关联工人风险；4）工人在风险分类任务中发现本文分类体系比另外两个分类体系更易用，在与近期生成式AI风险分类的对比中64%非平局比较中获偏好。

Conclusion: 工作场所AI智能体风险不仅源于智能体本身，还取决于人们如何与智能体协作以及智能体的部署方式。更安全的工作场所需要更安全的智能体，更需要精心设计的人机协作机制。

Abstract: To anticipate socio-technical risks from AI agents, organizations need taxonomies to classify them. However, existing AI risk taxonomies focus on broad risks and do not capture job-specific risks introduced by agents. To address this gap, we make three main contributions. First, we developed a multi-layer framework from a literature review of AI agents. The framework models three core components and their interactions: agents, goals, and environment. Second, we embedded this framework in a structured prompt and applied it to descriptions of 2,078 job tasks from the O*NET database, producing 8,356 risk scenarios labeled by severity and deployment mode (automation or augmentation). We validated these scenarios with 45 workers across 10 job roles and an independent LLM judge, confirming their plausibility and alignment with job tasks. Finally, we extended an existing taxonomy to create a 15-category taxonomy of workplace AI agent risks that covers all our risk scenarios. Our analysis highlights four findings. First, augmentation is not inherently safe because overreliance on agents can gradually erode workers' skills and oversight. Second, Erroneous Agent Actions accounts for the largest share of risk scenarios and has the highest concentration of severe risks. Many arise at the human-agent boundary. Third, automation is associated mainly with organizational risks, while augmentation is associated mainly with risks to workers. Fourth, workers found our taxonomy easier to use for a risk classification task than two other taxonomies and preferred it in 64% of non-tied comparisons with a recent generative AI risk taxonomy. These findings show that workplace AI agent risks do not arise from agents alone; they also depend on how people work with agents and how agents are deployed. Safer workplaces require not only safer agents but also carefully designed human-AI agent collaboration.

</details>


### [374] [ForestBench: A Unified Graph Framework for Evaluating Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08605)
*Guo Chen,Ziwen Li,Reed Li,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Bingbing Xu,Junjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForestBench，一个基于统一协作图的通用多智能体系统（MAS）评估框架，将异构MAS执行轨迹映射到共享空间，通过与查询特定的参考森林（由多种验证成功的协作图组成）进行结构比较来评估，无需额外LLM推理，毫秒级完成评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估存在两大问题：基于结果的基准丢弃了智能体间的协作信息；LLM-as-Judge评估依赖额外的模型推理且结果随LLM和评分标准变化而不稳定。MAS的执行轨迹异构，缺乏统一的评估基础。

Method: 将原生MAS轨迹映射为统一协作图；每个查询构建参考森林（包含多种验证成功的协作图，记录不同MAS方法完成任务的方式）；候选图与查询特定参考森林进行结构比较；从7个公开数据集中筛出844个协作必要查询，每个查询预计算10个成功的目标条件参考图，并评估6个代表性MAS框架。

Result: 框架实现为ForestBench，评估6个代表性MAS框架；通过受控骨干、参考构建和扰动研究测试了评估的稳定性和范围；基准森林构建后，每条轨迹的评分可在毫秒内完成且无需额外LLM推理。

Conclusion: ForestBench为异构MAS协作轨迹提供了可复用、稳定、高效的结构化评估基础，避免了结果导向基准丢失协作信息和LLM-as-Judge的不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) are proliferating rapidly, but their heterogeneous execution traces provide no common basis for evaluation across methods. Outcome-only benchmarks discard collaborations, whereas LLM-as-Judge evaluation requires additional, model-dependent inference and can vary with the LLM and rubric. We introduce a generalizable evaluation framework that maps native MAS traces into a shared space of unified collaboration graphs, enabling different methods to be evaluated under the same representation, reference set, and metric panel. Candidate graphs are compared with a query-specific reference forest. Each forest is a benchmark-provided collection of verified-success graphs: it records diverse ways in which representative MAS methods can complete the task, rather than prescribing a unique optimal process. Instantiating the framework as ForestBench, we filter $844$ collaboration-necessary queries from seven public datasets, precompute ten successful target-conditioned reference graphs per query, and evaluate six representative MAS frameworks. Controlled backbone, reference-construction, and perturbation studies test the stability and scope of evaluation. Once the benchmark forests are built, ForestBench scores a trace in milliseconds without further LLM inference, providing a reusable structural basis for comparing diverse MAS collaboration traces.

</details>


### [375] [Walking through Discussions: A Mobile Visual Analytics System for In-Situ Group Discussion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08617)
*Yiping Sun,Ziyao Kang,Wei Zeng,Minli Wu,Jiazhi Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileGroupVis是一个面向移动端的可视化分析系统，帮助教师在实体课堂中实时监测多个小组的讨论状态、诊断目标小组问题并实施教学干预，通过轻量级流式分析管道将音频转换为结构化讨论数据，结合六种协调视图实现快速异常定位与深入诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师在实体课堂中难以同时监控多个小组的讨论情况，也无法快速诊断出需要关注的小组。现有的可视化分析工具主要支持桌面端的事后分析，缺乏对教师课堂走动式教学的实时在场支持。

Method: MobileGroupVis包含一个轻量级流式分析管道，将小组音频转换为结构化讨论数据，并通过对话分析模块提取交互模式、话题进展和话题偏离信息。系统设计了六种协调视图，包括紧凑图形（编码词数、交互强度和话题偏离）、观点演化、交互动态、话题覆盖和对话记录等详细视图，适配小屏幕触控交互。

Result: 通过两项案例研究和专家访谈进行系统评估，结果提供了初步证据，表明MobileGroupVis能够支持教师理解讨论过程、识别需要关注的小组，并促进课堂中的实时干预。

Conclusion: MobileGroupVis填补了移动端实时课堂讨论可视化分析的空缺，验证了在小组讨论场景中进行在场式诊断与干预的可行性，为未来的智慧课堂教学支持工具提供了新方向。

Abstract: Group discussion-based teaching is widely used to foster collaborative learning, yet teachers in physical classrooms often struggle to simultaneously monitor multiple groups and quickly diagnose a target group before intervening. Existing visual analytics tools primarily support post-hoc analysis on desktop, providing limited support for in-situ walk-around teaching. To address this gap, we present MobileGroupVis, a mobile visual analytics system for in-situ analysis of classroom group discussions. MobileGroupVis integrates multi-group monitoring, single-group diagnosis, and instructional intervention into a concise analytical workflow tailored for small-screen touch interaction. The system is powered by a lightweight streaming analysis pipeline that converts group audio into structured discussion data and further extracts interaction patterns, topic progression, and topic deviation through a dialogue analysis module. To enable both glanceable overview and traceable diagnosis, we design six coordinated views, including a compact glyph that visually encodes word count, interaction intensity, and topic deviation for efficient cross-group comparison and anomaly localization, along with detailed views for opinion evolution, interaction dynamics, topic coverage, and dialogue records. We evaluate MobileGroupVis through two case studies and expert interviews. The results provide preliminary evidence that MobileGroupVis supports teachers in understanding discussion processes, identifying groups in need of attention, and facilitating in-class intervention.

</details>


### [376] [Business Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents in a Realistic Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08621)
*Yijun Pan,Yukun Lian,Kunyu Shi,Junbo Li,Hongwei Xue,Sicong Xie,Guannan Zhang,Xiaoying Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Business Arena，一个基于真实阿里巴巴采购数据和权威市场条件校准的受控环境，用于评估AI代理在跨境电商场景下长期经营店铺的能力。通过利润、人类策略对比、技能级指标和行动级归因等多维度评估15个前沿模型，发现最佳模型仍落后于人类设计的策略，且模型间最终净资产差距达九倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM代理已能完成复杂工作流，但现有的代理评测基准很少涉及商业经营能力。经营企业需要从不完整的信号中推断机会、在不确定性下投入资本、适应延迟结果并满足监管要求，这些能力亟需被系统评估。

Method: 构建Business Arena受控环境，模拟跨境外贸店铺的长期经营；基于真实Alibaba.com采购数据和权威来源校准的市场条件；采用利润作为综合结果指标，同时结合人类设计策略对比、技能级指标和行动级损益归因进行分析，并通过机制消融实验排除捷径和模拟器特定漏洞。

Result: 评估了15个前沿模型，发现最终净资产均值存在九倍差异；即使最佳模型也落后于人类设计的策略；技能级分析揭示了从利润率优先的高端卖家到高周转批发商和客服专家等不同经营风格；行动级归因识别出采购、定价和回款决策对价值的创造或破坏作用。

Conclusion: 商业经营对LLM代理而言仍具挑战性，Business Arena为端到端商业代理的评估提供了一个现实且可信的测试平台，是迈向该方向的第一步。

Abstract: Running a business is a challenging form of intelligent work. Operators must infer opportunities from partial signals, commit capital under uncertainty, adapt to delayed outcomes in a changing market, and satisfy regulatory obligations before trading legally. Frontier LLM agents can increasingly complete complex workflows, yet business-related capabilities are rarely evaluated in existing agent benchmarks. We introduce \textbf{Business Arena}, a controlled environment where an AI agent runs a cross-border shop, buying from suppliers and selling to buyers over a long horizon. We ground the arena in real Alibaba.com sourcing data and market conditions calibrated from authoritative sources. Delayed and coupled consequences make individual business decisions difficult to judge, but their combined outcome is measurable through profit. Because profit alone cannot explain why an agent succeeds or fails, we compare agents with human-designed strategies to estimate available opportunity, use skill-level metrics to reveal underlying strengths and weaknesses, and trace realized gains and losses to the actions that produced them. We use mechanism ablations to establish that strong results reflect genuine business intelligence rather than neglect or simulator-specific shortcuts. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find a ninefold difference in mean final net worth. Even the best model falls behind human-designed strategies, indicating that business operation remains challenging for LLM agents. Skill-level analysis reveals operating styles, from margin-focused premium sellers to high-turnover wholesalers and customer-service specialists, while action-level attribution identifies the sourcing, pricing, and recovery decisions that create or destroy value. Together, Business Arena takes a first step toward a realistic and trustworthy testbed for evaluating end-to-end business agents.

</details>


### [377] [MedCalc-R1: Knowledge-Guided Reward Framework for Medical Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08623)
*Haotian Wang,Lian Yan,Xingzhi Yao,Fanshu Meng,Ye He,Jingchi Jiang,Yi Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedCalc-R1，一种面向数学推理任务（特别是临床场景）的知识引导混合奖励框架，通过知识验证奖励机制和软硬结合奖励方案，解决了传统基于容差的浮点奖励在阈值校准、训练稳定性和精度方面的局限，实验证明其在推理准确性和泛化能力上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR框架中，数学推理任务的浮点结果通常使用基于容差的奖励进行评价，但该方法存在阈值标定困难、训练动态不稳定和精度有限等挑战，尤其在临床安全关键场景中问题更为突出。

Method: 提出知识引导混合奖励框架MedCalc-R1：(1)引入知识验证奖励机制，强制显式生成计算公式并由外部验证器验证，增强可解释性和推理可靠性；(2)设计软硬混合奖励方案，将基于临床安全阈值的硬约束与对精度敏感的软奖励相结合，在可接受范围内逐步引导学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在推理准确性和泛化能力上均显著优于现有基线方法，验证了该方法在安全关键领域中的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: MedCalc-R1通过知识引导的混合奖励机制有效解决了传统容差奖励在临床数学推理任务中的局限，提升了模型的安全性和可靠性，证明了其在安全关键场景中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) frameworks for mathematical reasoning tasks, floating-point results are typically evaluated using a tolerance-based reward. However, this strategy suffers from challenges such as difficulty in threshold calibration, unstable training dynamics, and limited accuracy, especially in clinical scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge-guided hybrid reward framework (\textsc{MedCalc-R1}). Specifically, we introduce a knowledge verification reward mechanism that enforces explicit generation of computational formulas, which are further validated by an external verifier to enhance interpretability and reasoning reliability. Furthermore, we design a hybrid soft-hard reward scheme combining a hard constraint based on clinical safety thresholds with a soft, precision-sensitive reward that progressively guides learning within the acceptable range. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines in both reasoning accuracy and generalization capability, validating the effectiveness and applicability in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [378] [UniMoMo: Expert Merging-Based MoE Acceleration for Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08627)
*Lei Xin,Bin Gu,Peize Li,Zitong Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Changjiang Jiang,Yanyue Xie,Chao Huang,Xuyang Zhao,Zunhai Su,Fanhu Zeng,Zhenglun Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMoMo，一种针对推荐系统中稀疏专家混合（MoE）模型的后训练压缩框架。它将专家压缩建模为带约束的图粗化问题，基于功能相似性而非参数距离对专家进行分组，并引入层自适应保护机制限制高流量专家的合并。在多个数据集上，四专家模型在保持约99.92%-102.30%的NDCG@10性能的同时实现1.28-1.63倍A100加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE层通过条件计算扩展了推荐能力，但训练好的检查点仍然存储并对所有专家进行路由，导致部署成本高。现有压缩方法要么依赖参数距离（未考虑功能相似性），要么需要添加压缩特定的在线模块，增加了部署复杂性。因此需要一种无需在线模块的后训练压缩方法。

Method: UniMoMo将专家压缩形式化为带约束的图粗化问题：1）使用无标注校准集，基于专家对共享推荐状态的反应相似性度量功能相似性而非参数距离，对专家进行分组；2）引入层自适应保护机制，根据路由暴露程度限制高流量专家的合并，防止性能退化；3）通过完整的转换-适应流程将训练好的checkpoint导出为更小的标准MoE。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、KuaiRec和TenRec数据集（含2、4、6个MoE块）上，四专家checkpoint的源相对五轮平均NDCG@10比率为99.92%-102.30%，A100加速为1.28-1.63倍；激进的二专家top-1运行点达到98.36%-104.24%的比率和1.47-2.21倍的加速。

Conclusion: 实验表明训练好的推荐MoE可以在多种部署预算下成功导出，UniMoMo在保持甚至提升推荐性能的同时实现显著推理加速，验证了基于功能相似性的图粗化方法和层自适应保护机制的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers expand recommendation capacity through conditional computation, yet a trained checkpoint still stores and routes over its full expert bank. We study a deployment problem: convert that checkpoint to a smaller standard MoE under an explicit expert budget, without adding a compression-specific online module. To address this, we introduce UniMoMo, a post-training compression framework formulated as a constrained graph coarsening problem. Rather than relying on parameter distance, UniMoMo groups experts based on their functional similarity, using an unlabeled calibration set to measure how similarly experts respond to shared recommendation states. To prevent performance degradation, we introduce a layer-adaptive protection mechanism that restricts the merging of high-traffic experts based on their routing exposure. Across Amazon Beauty, KuaiRec, and TenRec with 2, 4, and 6 MoE blocks, the final four-expert checkpoints obtain source-relative five-run mean NDCG@10 ratios of 99.92%--102.30% and measured A100 speedups of 1.28$\times$--1.63$\times$. An aggressive two-expert, top-1 operating point obtains ratios of 98.36%--104.24% and speedups of 1.47$\times$--2.21$\times$. These endpoint results evaluate the complete conversion-and-adaptation workflow and show that a trained recommendation MoE can be exported at multiple serving budgets.

</details>


### [379] [A QUBO-Inspired Computational Framework for Airport Landside Bottleneck Diagnosis and Dynamic Dispatch Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08632)
*Wuming Lei,Xiaobin Li,Mingyan Sun,Jianing Long,Yulin Tong,Yanbin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种受QUBO启发的计算框架，用于机场陆侧交通枢纽的瓶颈诊断与动态调度。该框架基于五分钟状态模型，结合多个瓶颈诊断指标和两种调度方案（有限动作模型预测控制与QUBO启发式模拟退火），以上海浦东和杭州萧山机场为案例进行验证。结果表明QUBO启发式方法在强高峰场景下能显著减少旅客排队长度，且两机场呈现不同的主导瓶颈特征，鲁棒性测试也验证了方法在多种扰动下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场陆侧交通中心在高峰时段会面临旅客排队、车辆排队、接客泊位、停车区域和通行道路之间的耦合拥堵问题，亟需有效的瓶颈诊断与动态调度方法来解决这一复杂系统优化难题。

Method: 提出五分钟状态模型，将旅客到达、车辆供给、接客泊位服务、车辆存储和道路容量相互关联；采用服务强度、道路需求饱和度、瓶颈频率、排队严重度、影子价格杠杆和综合拥堵严重度指数进行瓶颈诊断；测试两种调度方案：有限动作模型预测控制和QUBO启发式模拟退火。

Result: 在强高峰基线场景下，QUBO启发式方法将上海浦东机场最终旅客队列从3445人降至2477人，杭州萧山机场从2053人降至1482人。两个机场表现出不同的主导瓶颈：上海浦东受道路饱和影响更大，杭州萧山受接客泊位服务影响更大。在需求、供给、服务、道路容量、模态分担和随机噪声扰动下，排队减少效益依然保持。

Conclusion: QUBO启发的计算框架能够有效进行机场陆侧枢纽的瓶颈诊断和动态调度，在不同机场情境下均能显著减少旅客排队长度，且对不同类型不确定性扰动具有鲁棒性。研究还揭示不同机场的瓶颈特征存在差异，为针对性优化提供了依据。

Abstract: Airport landside traffic centers connect terminal arrivals with taxis, ride-hailing vehicles, private cars, buses, metro services, parking facilities, and terminal-area roadways. Peak arrivals can create coupled congestion across passenger queues, vehicle queues, pickup berths, storage areas, and access roads. This study proposes a QUBO-inspired computational framework for bottleneck diagnosis and dynamic dispatch in this setting. Shanghai Pudong International Airport and Hangzhou Xiaoshan International Airport serve as case airports. A five-minute state model links passenger arrivals, vehicle supply, pickup berth service, vehicle storage, and road capacity. Bottleneck diagnosis uses service intensity, road demand saturation, bottleneck frequency, queue severity, shadow-price leverage, and a composite congestion severity index. Two dispatch schemes are tested under consistent demand inputs: finite-action model predictive control and quadratic-unconstrained-binary-optimization-inspired simulated annealing. In the strong-peak baseline scenario, the QUBO-inspired method reduces the final passenger queue from 3445 to 2477 passengers at Shanghai Pudong and from 2053 to 1482 passengers at Hangzhou Xiaoshan. Case results indicate different dominant bottlenecks. Shanghai Pudong is more affected by road saturation, whereas Hangzhou Xiaoshan is more affected by pickup berth service. Robustness tests under demand, supply, service, road-capacity, modal-share, and random-noise perturbations show retained queue-reduction benefits under the tested uncertainty levels.

</details>


### [380] [Can Open-Weight Models Compete on Financial Text Comprehension?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08634)
*Jan Spörer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文更新了Financial Touchstone基准测试，覆盖20个模型和495份国际年报中的2,967个问答对，评估了开源与闭源语言模型在真实金融任务上的可靠性。Claude Opus 4.6准确率最高（88.4%），Gemini 2.5 Pro幻觉率最低（0.08%）。值得注意的是，开源模型Kimi K2.6准确率排名第三，GLM 5和Mistral 3等非推理模型也跻身前五，挑战了推理架构或专有权重是金融理解先决条件的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管中国AI实验室的开源权重模型在基准测试上已接近专有前沿模型，但其在真实金融任务上的可靠性仍未得到充分验证。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，通过更大规模的基准和更多样化的模型覆盖，了解这些模型的真实金融能力。

Method: 更新了Financial Touchstone基准，构建了包含2,967个问题-上下文-答案三元组的评估集，覆盖495份国际年报。模型覆盖面从11个扩展到20个（涵盖10家提供商），包括GLM 4.7、GLM 5、Kimi K2.6、DeepSeek V3.2等开源模型及Qwen3-Max专有模型，从准确率和幻觉率两个维度进行系统评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6准确率最高（88.4%），Gemini 2.5 Pro幻觉率最低（0.08%）。Kimi K2.6准确率排名第三，GLM 5和Mistral 3分列第四、第五。信息检索是主要瓶颈，占所有失败的48.9%。此外发现中国模型存在地缘政治内容过滤器，会拒绝合法金融问题（占0.08%的尝试），且拒绝行为受访问路径影响。

Conclusion: 开源权重模型在金融任务上的表现挑战了此前认为推理架构或专有权重为必要条件的主流假设。信息检索是当前金融AI的主要瓶颈，应优先改进。中国模型的内容过滤器对金融任务构成实际干扰，其行为因访问路径而异，这一现象值得进一步研究。数据集和评估框架已公开。

Abstract: Open-weight language models from Chinese AI labs caught up on benchmarks relative to proprietary frontier models in recent months. Yet their reliability on real-world financial tasks remains largely untested. We updated the Financial Touchstone benchmark, which now has 2,967 question context-answer triplets across 495 international annual reports. We also apply a new set of models on the benchmark, expanding coverage from eleven to twenty models across ten providers, including recent open-weight models such as GLM 4.7, GLM 5, Kimi K2.6, and DeepSeek V3.2, as well as Alibaba's proprietary flagship Qwen3-Max. Anthropic's Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest accuracy (88.4%), while Google's Gemini 2.5 Pro maintains the lowest hallucination rate (0.08%). Notably, the open-weight Kimi K2.6 ranks third in accuracy, and the non-reasoning models GLM 5 and Mistral 3 rank fourth and fifth, challenging the assumption that reasoning architectures or proprietary weights are a prerequisite for strong financial comprehension. Information retrieval remains the primary bottleneck, accounting for 48.9% of all failures. We also document a new finding: geopolitical content filters in Chinese models refuse legitimate financial questions (0.08% of attempts), sometimes without clear reason, and the refusal behavior depends on the access route as much as on the model. The complete dataset and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [381] [Smart Compaction: Predicting Compaction Utility from Lakehouse Table Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08639)
*Jannic Cutura,Subash Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个开放仿真框架，生成2376个Iceberg表并提取17个元数据特征，用XGBoost预测文件缩减比（R²=0.998）。但发现二元压缩决策仅需单一阈值max_files_per_partition>4即可完美区分。跨模式验证（TPC-H 96表）确认泛化能力，查询基准测试显示压缩对元数据密集型查询有益但可能降低全扫描聚合的任务并行度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湖仓表格式（如Apache Iceberg）中小文件会随时间累积并损害查询性能。当前压缩决策依赖启发式阈值，但哪些元数据特征真正决定压缩效用尚未被充分理解，缺乏系统性的研究框架。

Method: 构建开放仿真框架：生成覆盖三个数量级文件大小的2376个Iceberg表，从manifest文件中提取17个元数据特征（无需读取数据），使用XGBoost预测连续的文件缩减比；同时对比学习模型与简单阈值规则的决策效果，并通过TPC-H基准表进行跨模式验证。

Result: XGBoost预测文件缩减比达到R²=0.998、RMSE=0.013；二进制压缩决策可由单一阈值（每分区文件数>4）完全分离，无需复杂模型；跨模式验证R²=0.976；查询基准显示压缩对元数据密集型查询有益，但会因减少任务并行度而拖慢全扫描聚合。

Conclusion: 压缩效用问题中，连续回归预测需要复杂模型，但实际工程中的二元决策仅靠简单阈值即可解决。压缩策略需根据查询类型权衡：对元数据开销敏感的场景有利，对依赖并行度的全扫描场景可能有害。

Abstract: Open lakehouse table formats accumulate small data files over time, which degrades query performance. Deciding when compaction is worthwhile remains threshold-driven, but which metadata features actually determine compaction utility is not well understood. We present an open simulation framework that generates 2,376 Apache Iceberg tables spanning three orders of magnitude in file size, extracts 17 metadata features from manifest files without reading data, and trains XGBoost to predict the continuous file-reduction ratio (R2 = 0.998, RMSE= 0.013). The binary compaction decision turns out to be trivially separable by a single partition-level threshold max_files_per_partition> 4, requiring no learned model. Cross-schema validation on 96 TPC-H tables confirms generalisation without retraining (R2 = 0.976). A query benchmark reveals that compaction benefits metadata-heavy queries but can slow full-scan aggregations by reducing task parallelism. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [382] [SkillReason: Reasoning-Enhanced Agent Skill Retrieval for Implicit User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08640)
*Donghong Jiang,Endian Lin,Luoping Cui,Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Fan Yang,Hong Wang,Zhao Yang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillReason-Bench基准（含3,729个查询和61,228个技能，覆盖九个领域）以及SkillReason两阶段框架，利用链式思维推理作为训练时监督来提升大规模技能库检索的性能，在三个基准上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体依赖可复用技能扩展能力，但从大规模技能库中检索合适技能具有挑战性，因为真实用户请求往往简洁且信息不足，仅陈述任务目标而隐含所需能力和执行步骤。现有基准对此类请求覆盖不足。

Method: SkillReason采用两阶段框架：第一阶段利用更强教师模型生成的能力推理轨迹，通过对比学习、检索分布对齐和语言建模提供显式监督，使检索器将能力推理内化到查询表示中；第二阶段通过检索引导的GRPO目标鼓励模型探索更契合自身能力且对检索更有效的推理轨迹。推理时直接编码原始查询，无需自回归CoT生成。

Result: 在SkillReason-Bench、SkillRet和SRA-Bench三个基准上的大量实验表明，SkillReason在所有基准上均达到最先进的性能，证明推理增强的训练能更好地弥合高级任务目标与技能能力之间的语义鸿沟。

Conclusion: 推理增强训练是提升技能检索性能的有效途径，能够在不牺牲推理效率的前提下弥补用户任务目标与技能能力之间的语义差距，为大规模技能库检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on reusable skills to extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowl- edge. However, retrieving the appropriate skill from a large- scale library remains challenging because realistic user re- quests are often concise and underspecified, stating only the task goal while leaving the required capabilities and execu- tion steps implicit. Existing benchmarks provide limited cov- erage of such requests. To address this gap, we introduce SkillReason-Bench, a large-scale cross-domain benchmark containing 3,729 queries and a retrieval corpus of 61,228 skills spanning nine domains. We further propose SkillRea- son, a two-stage framework that uses chain-of-thought rea- soning as training-time supervision for skill retrieval. In Stage I, capability reasoning traces generated by a stronger teacher provide explicit supervision through contrastive learning, re- trieval distribution alignment, and language modeling, en- couraging the retriever to internalize capability reasoning in its query representation. In Stage II, a retrieval-guided GRPO objective encourages the model to explore reasoning trajecto- ries better suited to its own capabilities and more effective for retrieval. At inference, SkillReason directly encodes the orig- inal query without autoregressive CoT generation, preserv- ing efficient query-only retrieval. Extensive experiments on SkillReason-Bench, SkillRet, and SRA-Bench show that Skill- Reason achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating that reasoning-enhanced training better bridges the semantic gap between high-level task goals and skill capabilities.

</details>


### [383] [The Scaffolding Matters More Than the Interface: A Controlled Comparison of MCP and CLI Tool Use Across Seven Agent Scaffoldings, Five Language Models, and One Software Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08654)
*Marc Alier Forment,María José Casañ Guerrero,Francisco José García-Peñalvo,Juanan Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统测算了AI编码代理在使用MCP协议与普通CLI接口时的工具调用成本，发现代理脚手架（scaffolding）而非接口类型才是成本的主导因素。在7种脚手架和5种语言模型上完成同一任务，支持MCP的脚手架比仅支持CLI的脚手架贵5.0至28倍，而MCP与CLI的MCP/CLI成本比率跨度达0.43x至29x，差异不稳定。MCP在失败成本上显著高于CLI（12.9% vs 2.2%的资金浪费），但失败频率两者相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献对MCP与CLI工具接口的成本差异估计相差超过一个数量级，且基于无法复现的从业者报告，缺乏严谨、可复现的实验测量。

Method: 设计固定软件任务（对私有在线git仓库执行6次操作），在7种代理脚手架和5种语言模型上运行，通过检查仓库实际状态而非代理自报告来验证任务完成情况。对比仅CLI、仅MCP及两者配对的运行成本，并开放源代码、数据集和验证工具。

Result: 脚手架是成本主导因素：两种无MCP支持的脚手架全部通过CLI完成任务，比支持MCP的五种脚手架便宜5.0至28倍；小模型下成本跨脚手架变化达139倍。MCP与CLI的13对严格配对比率在0.43x至29x之间，结果不稳定。MCP运行的失败成本占12.9%资金，CLI仅占2.2%，但失败频率两者相同。代理经常无视被分配的接口，使未验证行为的比较不可靠。

Conclusion: 接口选择（MCP vs CLI）对成本的影响远小于代理脚手架设计，且MCP/CLI成本比较因脚手架而异、缺乏稳定性。CLI对这类任务而言足够且更经济，MCP的主要代价体现在失败成本上。研究强调必须验证代理实际行为而非自报告，否则比较衡量的是未知混合体。

Abstract: How much an AI coding agent costs to run can depend more on the agent scaffolding that drives it than on the interface through which it
  reaches its tools. We set out to measure the cost of tool use over the Model Context Protocol (MCP) against tool use over an ordinary
  command-line interface (CLI), a difference on which published estimates disagree by more than an order of magnitude while resting on
  practitioner reports that cannot be reproduced. We ran one fixed software task -- six operations against a private online git repository --
  across seven agent scaffoldings and five language models, and we verified completion by inspecting the repository state rather than trusting
  the agent's self-report. The dominant effect was the scaffolding. Two of the seven ship no MCP support at all; they completed every run using
  only the CLI, which shows that MCP is unnecessary for this class of work, and they were 5.0x to 28x cheaper than the five scaffoldings that
  do support MCP, comparing CLI runs alone with no MCP server attached anywhere. The effect was largest for a small 27-billion-parameter model
  running locally, whose cost varied 139x across scaffoldings while it completed the task under all of them. The comparison we set out to make
  proved unstable: thirteen strictly paired MCP-to-CLI ratios span 0.43x to 29x, with outliers on both sides. The two interfaces separate on
  the cost of failure, where 12.9 per cent of the money spent on MCP runs bought no completed work against 2.2 per cent on CLI runs, but not on
  its frequency: failures were equally common in both, in the original runs and in their repetitions alike. Agents frequently ignored the
  interface they were assigned, so comparisons that do not verify actual behaviour measure an unknown mixture. The harness, the task, the
  verification and the complete dataset are released as open source.

</details>


### [384] [Branch2Skill: Efficient Skill Evolution Through Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08677)
*Yanwei Ren,Haotian Zhang,Likang Xiao,Jiaxing Huang,Jiayan Qiu,Baosheng Yu,Quan Chen,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Branch2Skill是一个高效框架，通过对单一推理树进行蒙特卡洛树搜索，将多样化的推理轨迹转化为稠密的逐步监督信号，从而减少技能进化中反复的rollout-update循环和token开销。在六个基准上，相比SkillOpt减少73.2%的token消耗且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能进化方法主要依赖单一轨迹，早期推理错误会向后传播并在后续步骤中削弱可用的反馈信号，导致需要通过反复的"展开-诊断-更新"循环来改进技能，产生高昂的token成本。

Method: Branch2Skill包含三步：(1) 在固定预算下对每个任务执行蒙特卡洛树搜索，获取多样化的推理轨迹；(2) 将精英路径与共享相同前缀的兄弟备选方案进行对比，提取关于哪些推理模式应保留、修订或避免的逐步证据；(3) 将多步证据蒸馏为可复用的技能更新，使单一推理树能为多个推理步骤提供监督。

Result: 在六个涵盖推理和智能体任务的基准上，Branch2Skill持续提升了任务性能和技能进化效率。以GPT 5.5为目标模型时，相比SkillOpt减少了73.2%的token消耗，同时取得了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 推理树不仅能支持更有效的轨迹搜索，还能提供更丰富的监督信号，从而实现更高效的技能改进。证明将单次推理转化为稠密监督是提升技能进化效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Skill evolution improves agent skills through feedback over time, with failed trajectories often providing informative signals by revealing incomplete or misleading behaviors. However, existing methods mainly rely on single trajectories, where early reasoning errors can propagate through subsequent steps and weaken the feedback available for skill refinement. Consequently, improving skills requires repeated cycles of rollout, diagnosis, and update, incurring substantial token costs. To address this challenge, we introduce Branch2Skill, an efficient framework that transforms a single reasoning tree into dense supervision for skill evolution. For each task or problem, Branch2Skill performs Monte Carlo tree search under a fixed budget to obtain diverse reasoning trajectories, then compares an elite path with sibling alternatives sharing the same prefixes to extract step-wise evidence about which reasoning patterns to retain, revise, or avoid. Finally, Branch2Skill distills multi-step evidence into reusable updates, allowing one reasoning tree to provide supervision across multiple reasoning steps and reducing the need for repeated rollout-update cycles. Across six benchmarks covering reasoning and agentic tasks, Branch2Skill consistently improves task performance while enhancing skill evolution efficiency. For example, with GPT 5.5 as the target model, Branch2Skill uses 73.2% fewer tokens than SkillOpt, while achieving superior performance. These results demonstrate that reasoning trees can support not only more effective trajectory search, but also richer supervision for more efficient skill improvement. Code will be published.

</details>


### [385] [A Structural Dynamics Graph World Model: Unified Modeling, Constrained Rollout, and Interpretable Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08689)
*Wei Wang,Yaosen Chen,Han Yang,Yuegen Liu,Mingli Luo,Xinxin Jiao,Xuming Wen,Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SD-GWM，一个结构化动力学图世界模型，将系统状态演化分解为节点自动力学、边邻域耦合动力学、有界残差和全局投影四部分，实现异构机制集成、语义保真与可审计治理，在洪水与径流预测中显著优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑盒模型无法审计机制归因与约束保持，而将所有机制强行纳入单一方程族又会丢弃成熟的领域求解器。需要一个既能保留异构机制资产、又能实现可审计约束保持的结构化方案。

Method: SD-GWM作为可执行结构契约：节点声明自动力学S，边声明邻居图耦合动力学N（均为固定形式机制资产，仅校准授权参数）；可选有界残差R集中可学习性；全局投影将状态映射到可行域以强制执行约束。

Result: 在八个预注册研究问题上：异构集成使规则和求解器原生接入；禁用R时语义逐位保真；逐步追踪实现反事实故障定位（top-1=1.0）。在半合成洪水测试台和USGS径流数据上，约束违规降至浮点容差/零；254天极端洪水期间基线崩溃（RMSE 892-3007 cfs）而SD-GWM保持108 cfs（8-28倍提升）。

Conclusion: SD-GWM定位为可验证的、审计友好且约束安全的地理时空挖掘基底，而非普遍优越的预测器。其价值在于机制可归因、约束可保持、过程可追踪的结构化建模范式。

Abstract: The state evolution of a complex system arises jointly from object laws, relational propagation, domain conservation, and unmodeled error. Forcing all sources into one black box makes mechanism attribution and constraint preservation unauditable; forcing every mechanism into one equation family discards mature domain solvers. We propose SD-GWM, a Structural Dynamics Graph World Model as an executable structural contract: nodes declare self-dynamics S, edges declare neighbor graph-coupled dynamics N---both fixed-form mechanism assets (rules, ODEs, solvers) calibrating only authorized parameters. An optional bounded residual R concentrates learnability, while a global projection maps states to feasibility, enforcing constraints without guaranteeing accuracy gains. On eight pre-registered research questions, SD-GWM delivers (i) heterogeneous integration: rules and solvers plug in natively; (ii) semantic fidelity: disabling R preserves source semantics bit-for-bit, with four theory properties under explicit proof/empirical boundaries; (iii) auditable governance: stepwise traces enable counterfactual fault localization (top-1 = 1.0) without post-hoc approximations. On a semi-synthetic flood testbed and USGS streamflow, SD-GWM reduces constraint violations to floating-point tolerance in analytical tests and to zero in semi-synthetic and real-data cases. Persistence matches SD-GWM in calm periods, but during a 254-day extreme-flood shift persistence and all neural baselines collapse (90-min RMSE 892-3007 cfs) while SD-GWM holds at 108 cfs (8-28x gain). The bounded residual cuts RMSE ~50% only under backbone bias. We position SD-GWM not as a universally superior forecaster, but as a verifiable substrate for auditable, constraint-safe spatiotemporal mining.

</details>


### [386] [EnergyBridge: Benchmarking Household Energy Management, User Participation, and Grid Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08691)
*Xudong Wu,Zeqing Wu,Jiarui Zhang,Xuhao Fan,Ziang Ding,Yuming Zhuang,Mingqi Yuan,Yilun Du,Hongjie Jia,Yunfei Mu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EnergyBridge基准与智能体框架，将容量报告、用户授权和物理执行串联起来，通过LLM用户参与模拟器评估虚拟电厂灵活性容量的可靠性，并在天津和柏林两个区域进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟电厂基准只评估控制算法，忽略了事件特定的用户授权环节。但物理灵活性只有在居民授权计划并兑现承诺响应时才能成为可靠容量，因此需要一个连接授权与实际执行的评估框架。

Method: 构建EnergyBridge，包含天津和柏林两个区域特定的EnergyPlus仿真环境，以及基于LLM的用户参与模拟器（User Participation Simulator）。用户模拟器通过584个与人物画像和事件匹配的人工角色扮演判断进行校准验证。

Result: LLM用户参与模拟器在保持方法排序的前提下，平均绝对接受度误差仅5.3分。在传统控制器和智能体基线对比中，EnergyBridge在两个区域均取得最高模拟授权率、最低事件窗口能耗和最可靠的容量承诺。

Conclusion: EnergyBridge验证了将用户授权纳入虚拟电厂评估的必要性和可行性，并公开了人类数据与代码，为以人为中心的电网灵活性研究提供可复现的基础。

Abstract: Residential virtual power plants (VPPs) can provide grid flexibility by shifting household demand, but physical flexibility becomes dependable capacity only when residents authorize a plan and the promised response is delivered. Existing benchmarks evaluate control but omit event-specific authorization. We present EnergyBridge, a benchmark and agent framework connecting capacity reporting, household authorization, and physical execution. It combines region-specific EnergyPlus environments for Tianjin and Berlin with an LLM-based User Participation Simulator. Against 584 persona- and event-matched human role-play judgments, the LLM-based User Participation Simulator preserves method ordering with a 5.3-point mean absolute acceptance error. Across conventional controllers and agent baselines, EnergyBridge achieves the highest simulated authorization, lowest event-window energy, and the most reliable capacity commitment in both regions. We release human data and codes for reproducible human-centered grid-flexibility research: https://github.com/Agentic-Intelligence-Lab/EnergyBridge.

</details>
